
download the ollama : https://ollama.com/

check ollama version : ollama -v

how to check ollama is running or not default port is : 

spring.ai.ollama.base-url=http://localhost:11434

Accha question hai 👍

**Large Language Model (LLM)** जैसे GPT में **token** बहुत important concept है।

### 🔹 Token क्या होता है?

* **Token मतलब text का छोटा टुकड़ा (unit of meaning)** जिसे model समझता और process करता है।
* ये हमेशा **पूरा word** नहीं होता, कभी-कभी word का हिस्सा भी हो सकता है।
* Model text को पहले tokens में तोड़ता है → फिर उन tokens पर calculation करता है।

### 🔹 Example

मान लो text है:

```
I love programming
```

इसे tokenize करने पर:

* "I" → 1 token
* " love" → 1 token (शुरुआत में space भी गिना जाता है)
* " programming" → 1 token

तो पूरे sentence के 3 tokens बनेंगे।

अगर हिंदी लें:

```
मुझे पढ़ना अच्छा लगता है
```

इसे model subword tokens में तोड़ेगा, जैसे:

* "मुझे"
* " पढ़"
* "ना"
* " अच्छा"
* " लगता"
* " है"

(कुल 6 tokens)

### 🔹 क्यों जरूरी है?

1. **Input/Output Limit** → हर LLM में token की limit होती है (जैसे GPT-4 में 128k tokens तक)।
2. **Cost Calculation** → API call का खर्च tokens पर आधारित होता है।
3. **Processing Efficiency** → Model words के बजाय tokens पर training करता है, जिससे अलग-अलग भाषाएँ और complex words handle हो जाते हैं।

### 🔹 ध्यान रखने वाली बात

* 1 token ≠ 1 word
* English में 1 token ≈ 4 characters (औसतन)
* 100 tokens ≈ 75 words (approx)

---

क्या आप चाहोगे मैं आपको एक छोटा demo दूँ कि आपका लिखा हुआ कोई भी text कितने tokens लेगा?
===================================================================================================================
Bahut accha सवाल है 👌.
Aap **apna Large Language Model (LLM)** bana sakte ho, lekin uske level aur scale depend karte hain ki aap **scratch se banana chahte ho ya kisi pre-trained model ko fine-tune** karna chahte ho।

मैं step by step समझाता हूँ:

---

## 🔹 1. Scratch se apna LLM banana

Yeh research-level kaam hai, aur **बहुत resources** chahiye hote hain (GPUs, data, infra).
Steps roughly aise hote hain:

1. **Data collect karo**

   * Bahut bada text corpus chahiye (billions of tokens).
   * Sources: Wikipedia, books, research papers, code, social media (अगर policy allow करे)।

2. **Data cleaning & preprocessing**

   * Text को साफ करना (HTML tags, duplicates हटाना)।
   * Tokenizer banana (जैसे BPE, SentencePiece)।

3. **Model architecture design**

   * Mostly **Transformer** architecture use hota hai (Vaswani et al. 2017).
   * Decide: कितने layers, कितने attention heads, embedding size, hidden size।

4. **Training**

   * GPU/TPU clusters चाहिए (NVIDIA A100/ H100 level cards)।
   * Training में हफ्तों/महीनों लग सकते हैं।
   * Loss function: Cross-entropy (next token prediction)।

5. **Evaluation & testing**

   * Benchmarks pe test karo (perplexity, downstream tasks)।

⚠️ Problem: ये बहुत महंगा है (millions of dollars लग सकते हैं)।

---

## 🔹 2. Fine-tuning pre-trained LLM (सबसे practical तरीका)

Yeh aap easily kar सकते ho.
Already trained open-source models le lo (जैसे HuggingFace पर)। फिर unhe **fine-tune** करो apne data par.

Steps:

1. **Model choose karo**

   * GPT-NeoX, LLaMA, Falcon, Mistral, Gemma (open-source available)।

2. **Tokenizer load karo** (जो उस model ke साथ आता है)।

3. **Data prepare karo**

   * Apna domain-specific dataset बनाओ (जैसे finance, medical, coding, Hindi text)।

4. **Fine-tuning** techniques:

   * **Full fine-tuning** (पूरे model ko train karo → heavy compute chahiye)।
   * **LoRA / PEFT (Parameter Efficient Fine-Tuning)** → sirf कुछ parameters ko train karo (cheap & fast)।

5. **Training framework use karo**

   * HuggingFace Transformers + PyTorch
   * deepspeed / accelerate for distributed training

6. **Deploy karo**

   * Model ko API ke रूप में expose करो (FastAPI, Flask, LangChain)।

---

## 🔹 3. Chhota apna model (learning ke liye)

Agar आप बस सीखना चाहते हो तो:

* एक छोटा **Transformer** खुद implement कर सकते हो PyTorch me।
* छोटे dataset (Wikipedia subset) pe train karo।
* ये आपको समझाएगा कि internally LLM kaise काम करता है।

---

👉 Mere hisaab se agar aapke paas **zyaada infra nahi hai**, to best option hai:
**HuggingFace par pre-trained model + LoRA fine-tuning** use karna.

---

Kya aap chahte ho main aapko ek **practical roadmap** bana kar doon (kaunse tools, kaunse models, kaunse steps follow karne hain) ताकि aap apna chhota LLM bana sako?
=====================================================================================================================================================================
बहुत बढ़िया सवाल 👌

LLM और Machine Learning में **embedding** बहुत important concept है।

---

## 🔹 Embedding क्या होता है?

**Embedding = किसी शब्द/वाक्य/चीज़ का numerical (vector) representation**
मतलब:

* Text को numbers में बदलना
* ताकि model उस text को समझ सके और उस पर calculation कर सके

ये numbers randomly नहीं होते, बल्कि ऐसे बनाए जाते हैं कि **similar meanings वाले words करीब हों और अलग meanings वाले दूर हों**।

---

## 🔹 Example

मान लो हमारे पास 3 words हैं:

* "king"
* "queen"
* "apple"

अगर इन्हें embedding space (say 3D space) में map करें, तो result कुछ ऐसा होगा:

* **king → [0.8, 0.2, 0.1]**
* **queen → [0.79, 0.25, 0.15]**
* **apple → [0.1, 0.9, 0.7]**

👉 यहाँ "king" और "queen" एक-दूसरे के **पास होंगे**, लेकिन "apple" उनसे बहुत **दूर** होगा।

---

## 🔹 क्यों जरूरी है?

1. **Semantic meaning पकड़ना** → Words को सिर्फ spelling से नहीं, उनके meaning से represent करना।
2. **Similarity search** → अगर कोई "doctor" लिखे, तो "physician" भी पास आना चाहिए।
3. **Input for ML models** → Model सीधे words पर काम नहीं कर सकता, उसे numbers चाहिए।

---

## 🔹 Embedding कहाँ use होते हैं?

* **Search engines** → Similar documents ढूँढने के लिए
* **Recommendation system** → Similar products suggest करने के लिए
* **Chatbots / LLMs** → Context और semantic meaning समझने के लिए
* **Clustering** → Similar चीज़ों को group करने के लिए

---

## 🔹 Real-world Example

Sentence:

```
I love programming
```

Model embeddings बना सकता है:

* "I" → [0.12, -0.45, 0.87, ...]
* "love" → [0.91, 0.33, -0.12, ...]
* "programming" → [0.75, 0.65, -0.22, ...]

अब इन vectors पर model math कर सकता है।

---

👉 Short mein:
**Embedding = Text को ऐसे numbers (vectors) में बदलना जो meaning preserve करें।**

---

क्या आप चाहोगे मैं आपको Python का एक छोटा demo दूँ (HuggingFace या OpenAI embedding API से) ताकि आप देख सको कि किसी word/sentence का actual embedding vector कैसा दिखता है?

Samajh gaya 👍
aap puchh rahe ho ki **LLM ke andar alag-alag categories** hote hain jaise:

---

## 🌐 Types of AI / LLM-based Models

### 1️⃣ **Chat Models (Conversational)**

* Human jaisa baat karte hain.
* Based on **autoregressive (decoder-only)** models.
* Fine-tuned on dialogues, instructions, safety rules.
* ✅ Example: **ChatGPT, LLaMA-2-Chat, Mistral-Instruct, Vicuna, Claude**

---

### 2️⃣ **Speech-to-Text Models (ASR – Automatic Speech Recognition)**

* Voice ko text me convert karte hain.
* Audio input lete hain aur transcription output dete hain.
* ✅ Example: **Whisper (OpenAI), DeepSpeech (Mozilla), wav2vec 2.0 (Meta)**

---

### 3️⃣ **Text-to-Speech Models (TTS)**

* Text ko natural sounding voice me bolte hain.
* Use: Virtual assistants, audiobooks, accessibility.
* ✅ Example: **VALL-E (Microsoft), Tacotron, Coqui TTS, ElevenLabs**

---

### 4️⃣ **Multimodal Models**

* Ek hi model jo **text + image + audio + video** sab handle karta hai.
* Example: Image input + text output, ya text input + image output.
* ✅ Example: **GPT-4V (Vision), Gemini, LLaVA, Kosmos-1, CLIP**

---

### 5️⃣ **Code Models**

* Programming help karne ke liye trained hote hain.
* ✅ Example: **Codex (GitHub Copilot), StarCoder, CodeLLaMA**

---

### 6️⃣ **Domain-Specific Models**

* Special purpose models jo ek hi field ke liye optimized hote hain:

  * **Medical** → Med-PaLM
  * **Legal** → LawGPT
  * **Finance** → BloombergGPT

---

📌 Matlab:

* **Chat models** → baat karne ke liye
* **Speech-to-Text** → voice ko text me
* **Text-to-Speech** → text ko voice me
* **Multimodal** → text + image + audio + video ek sath

---

👉 Kya aap chahte ho main ek **table bana du** jisme Chat, Speech-to-Text, Text-to-Speech, Multimodal models ke examples aur use-cases ek jagah compare karke dikhau?

ठीक है Vishnudas 🙂 मैं simple language में समझाता हूँ:

---

## 🔥 Hugging Face kya hai?

Socho **GitHub jaisa ek website/platform**, lekin **AI aur Machine Learning ke models** ke liye.

* Agar aapko **pre-trained model** chahiye (jaise ChatGPT jaisa chhota model, speech-to-text, translation, sentiment analysis), to Hugging Face par aapko **ready-made models** mil jaate hain.
* Wahan **datasets** bhi hote hain (Wikipedia text, movie reviews, images, audio, etc.) jinse aap apna model train kar sakte ho.
* Aur aap apne banaye models/datasets ko **upload karke share** bhi kar sakte ho.

---

## 🛠 Example use

1. Aapko **sentiment analysis** karna hai (“I love you” positive hai ya negative?).
2. Normal coding me aapko khud model banana padta.
3. Lekin Hugging Face me bas 2 line likho:

```python
from transformers import pipeline

sentiment = pipeline("sentiment-analysis")
print(sentiment("I love Hugging Face!"))
```

👉 Output: `Positive 😊`

Matlab aapko **zero training** ki zaroorat nahi, bas model load karke use kar sakte ho.

---

## 📌 Simple words me:

* **GitHub → code ke liye**
* **Hugging Face → AI models ke liye**

# Spring AI – Part 1: Introduction & Basic Concepts

Based on your YouTube video transcript • Teacher–Student Q&A • Simple Hinglish • Text-file revision notes

Is notes mein hum video ke important concepts ko simple Hindi wali English mein samjhenge, examples ke saath. Notes ka focus hai Spring AI kya hai, kaise kaam karta hai, REST API se iska kya relation hai, aur ye developers ki life kaise easy banata hai.

## Module 1: What is Spring AI?

Q1. Teacher: Spring AI kya hai?

Student: Spring AI ek Spring ecosystem ka project/module hai, jiski help se hum apni Java ya Spring Boot application mein AI models integrate kar sakte hain.

Simple words mein, agar humein apni Spring Boot application mein ChatGPT jaise AI model ka use karna hai, toh Spring AI humein us integration ko easy banane mein help karta hai.

Example:

Suppose hum ek Spring Boot application bana rahe hain jo user ke questions ka answer deti hai.

- User question poochta hai: "What is Java?"
- Spring Boot application question receive karti hai.
- Spring AI ki help se AI model ko request bheji jaati hai.
- AI model answer generate karta hai.
- Application user ko answer return karti hai.

Q2. Teacher: Kya Spring AI use karna compulsory hai AI integrate karne ke liye?

Student: Nahi. Spring AI use karna compulsory nahi hai. Hum directly AI provider ki REST API call karke bhi AI integrate kar sakte hain.

Lekin Spring AI ek abstraction layer provide karta hai, jisse integration aur AI-related tasks ko manage karna easier ho sakta hai.

Q3. Teacher: AI model kya hota hai?

Student: AI model ek trained system hota hai jo apne task ke according input process karke output generate karta hai.

Examples:

- LLM (Large Language Model): Text generate karna, questions ka answer dena, text summarize karna.
- Image Generation Model: Text prompt se image generate karna.
- Video Generation Model: Text ya other supported input se video generate karna.

Video mein main focus Spring applications ke andar AI models ko integrate karne par hai.

Q4. Teacher: LLM ka full form kya hai?

Student: LLM ka full form Large Language Model hai.

Ye text-related tasks perform kar sakta hai, jaise question answering, content generation aur summarization.

Example:

Input: `Explain Java in simple words.`

Output: AI Java ke baare mein ek understandable explanation generate karega.

## Module 2: Spring Boot and AI Model Communication

Q5. Teacher: Agar hum ChatGPT jaise AI model ko apni Spring Boot application se connect karna chahte hain, toh communication kaise hoga?

Student: AI provider generally APIs expose karta hai. Spring Boot application un APIs ko request bhej sakti hai, aur response receive kar sakti hai.

Basic flow:

User

Question enter karta hai

Spring Boot Application

Request process karti hai

Spring AI / API Integration

AI model ko request bhejta hai

AI Model

Answer generate karta hai

Response to User

Note: Actual implementation mein Spring AI request ko provider-specific integration ke through handle karta hai. Exact flow application ke design par depend karega.

Q6. Teacher: REST API kya hoti hai?

Student: REST API ek common way hai jiske through do applications HTTP ke through communicate kar sakti hain.

For example, hamari Spring Boot application kisi AI provider ke API endpoint ko request bhejti hai aur response receive karti hai.

Example:

```
Spring Boot Application
        |
        | HTTP Request
        v
AI Provider REST API
        |
        | AI Response
        v
Spring Boot Application
```

Q7. Teacher: Kya hum Spring AI ke bina AI use kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan. Hum Java ke HTTP client ya kisi suitable HTTP library se provider ki REST API call kar sakte hain.

Lekin phir request formatting, response parsing, provider-specific details aur related AI features ko manage karne ka kaam humein khud karna pad sakta hai.

Q8. Teacher: Direct REST API call karne mein kya problem aa sakti hai?

Student: Basic request-response implement karna possible hai, lekin real application mein kuch additional challenges hote hain:

1. Har provider ka API format alag ho sakta hai.
2. Request aur response ko properly handle karna padta hai.
3. AI prompts aur different message roles ko manage karna padta hai.
4. Application ke private data ko AI ke context mein dene ka solution chahiye ho sakta hai.
5. Chat history aur conversation context maintain karna pad sakta hai.
6. Embeddings aur RAG jaise features implement karne pad sakte hain.

Spring AI inmein se kai tasks ko standard APIs aur abstractions ke through easier banata hai.

## Module 3: What is Abstraction Layer in Spring AI?

Q9. Teacher: Spring AI mein Abstraction Layer ka kya meaning hai?

Student: Abstraction Layer ka meaning hai ki developer ko har AI provider ke internal implementation details ko directly handle karne ki zaroorat kam ho jaati hai.

Spring AI humein Java classes, interfaces aur APIs provide karta hai. Hum inka use karke AI model ke saath communicate kar sakte hain.

Behind the scenes, Spring AI provider-specific API communication handle karne mein help karta hai.

Real-life example:

Suppose aapko car drive karni hai.

- Aap steering use karte ho.
- Aapko engine ke andar fuel kaise burn ho raha hai, ye har baar manage nahi karna padta.
- Steering aur controls aapke liye driving ko easy banate hain.

Similarly, Spring AI AI model ke saath interaction ko simpler banane ke liye abstraction provide karta hai.

Q10. Teacher: Spring AI ko use karne ka main objective kya hai?

Student: Iska main objective Spring applications mein AI models ko integrate karna easy banana hai.

Important points:

- AI models ke saath communication simplify karna.
- Common programming abstractions provide karna.
- Different AI providers ke integrations support karna.
- Prompts, embeddings, RAG aur chat history jaise AI-related tasks ko manage karne mein help karna.

Q11. Teacher: Spring AI aur direct REST API call mein kya difference hai?

Student: Dono approaches se AI integrate kar sakte hain, lekin implementation ka effort different ho sakta hai.

| Direct REST API                                                       | Spring AI                                                             |
| --------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| HTTP requests ko manually manage karna pad sakta hai.                 | Common AI abstractions provide karta hai.                             |
| Request/response handling developer ko implement karni pad sakti hai. | Supported integrations mein ye kaam simplify hota hai.                |
| Provider change karne par code changes ho sakte hain.                 | Provider abstraction ke through changes reduce ho sakte hain.         |
| AI-specific features manually implement karne pad sakte hain.         | Prompts, embeddings aur RAG jaise features ke liye support milta hai. |

Interview answer: Spring AI is a framework that simplifies integrating AI models into Spring applications by providing abstractions and support for AI-related capabilities.

## Module 4: Different AI Providers and Model Switching

Q12. Teacher: AI provider kya hota hai?

Student: AI provider woh company ya platform hota hai jo AI models aur unhe access karne ke liye APIs provide karta hai.

Video mein examples ke roop mein OpenAI, Azure aur Hugging Face ka mention hai.

Q13. Teacher: Kya Spring AI sirf ek hi AI model support karta hai?

Student: Nahi. Spring AI multiple providers ke saath integration support karta hai. Supported providers aur capabilities Spring AI ki version ke according vary kar sakti hain.

For example, application ki requirements ke according hum ek provider ka model use kar sakte hain aur baad mein kisi doosre supported provider par switch karne ki planning kar sakte hain.

Q14. Teacher: Spring AI ki help se AI provider change karna easy kaise hota hai?

Student: Spring AI common abstractions provide karta hai. Is wajah se provider-specific implementation ko replace karna kai cases mein easier ho sakta hai.

Lekin ek important point: Provider change karne ke liye hamesha zero code changes honge, aisa guarantee nahi hai. Dependency, configuration, model capabilities aur API differences ke according changes karne pad sakte hain.

Example:

```
Initially:
Spring Boot → Spring AI → Provider A

After switching:
Spring Boot → Spring AI → Provider B
```

Agar application common Spring AI abstractions par depend karti hai, toh provider switching mein application code ke changes reduce ho sakte hain.

## Module 5: Prompt Engineering and Message Roles

Q15. Teacher: AI prompt kya hota hai?

Student: Prompt woh instruction ya input hota hai jo hum AI model ko dete hain, taaki model uske according response generate kare.

Example:

```
Explain Spring Boot in simple Hinglish.
```

Yahan ye text hamara prompt hai.

Q16. Teacher: Spring AI prompts ko manage karne mein kaise help karta hai?

Student: Spring AI prompts create aur manage karne ke liye facilities provide karta hai. Iski help se hum fixed prompts, dynamic values aur different message roles ko organize kar sakte hain.

Hum prompt ko Java code mein ya suitable external resource, jaise file, mein rakh sakte hain.

Example:

```
Prompt Template:

Explain {topic} in simple Hinglish.
Give one practical example.
```

Agar `{topic}` ki value `Microservices` hai, toh final prompt hoga:

```
Explain Microservices in simple Hinglish.
Give one practical example.
```

Yahan `{topic}` ek dynamic value hai.

Q17. Teacher: AI messages mein System Role aur User Role kya hote hain?

Student: Video mein do important roles explain kiye gaye hain:

- System Role: AI ke behaviour ya response ke liye overall instructions define karne ke liye use hota hai.
- User Role: User ka actual question ya message represent karta hai.

Example:

```
System:
You are a Java teacher.
Explain concepts in simple Hinglish.

User:
What is HashMap?
```

Is example mein system instruction AI ko batati hai ki response kis style mein dena hai, aur user message actual question hai.

## Module 6: Embeddings and RAG

Q18. Teacher: Embeddings kya hote hain?

Student: Embeddings text ya other supported data ko numerical vectors ke form mein represent karte hain. In vectors ka use semantic similarity aur information retrieval jaise tasks mein kiya ja sakta hai.

Simple example: Hum documents ko embeddings mein convert karke similar meaning wale content ko search kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
Document 1: Java is a programming language.
Document 2: Java is used for backend development.
Document 3: Mango is a fruit.
```

Agar user Java programming ke baare mein question poochta hai, toh semantic retrieval relevant Java documents ko identify karne mein help kar sakta hai.

Note: Embeddings khud se answer generate nahi karte. Ye primarily information ko numerical representation mein convert karne ke kaam aate hain.

Q19. Teacher: RAG ka full form kya hai?

Student: RAG ka full form Retrieval-Augmented Generation hai.

RAG ka use AI model ko relevant external information provide karne ke liye kiya jaata hai, taaki model us information ke basis par answer generate kar sake.

Q20. Teacher: RAG ki zaroorat kyun padti hai?

Student: AI model ko hamari application ka private data, internal documents ya latest company-specific information automatically pata nahi hoti.

Isliye hum relevant information retrieve karke model ko context ke roop mein provide kar sakte hain.

Example: Company Leave Policy Chatbot

Suppose company ke leave policy documents hain aur employee poochta hai:

`How many casual leaves are allowed?`

Basic AI model ko company ki exact internal policy pata hona zaroori nahi hai.

RAG approach mein:

1. Company ke documents store kiye jaate hain.
2. Employee ka question process hota hai.
3. Relevant document content retrieve kiya jaata hai.
4. Retrieved information aur question AI model ko diye jaate hain.
5. Model provided context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.

User Question

Retrieve Relevant Information

Documents / Knowledge Base

AI Model + Retrieved Context

Context-Based Answer

Q21. Teacher: Kya RAG ka use karne se AI hamesha correct answer dega?

Student: Nahi. RAG relevant information provide karne mein help karta hai, lekin correct answer guaranteed nahi hota. Retrieved documents ki quality, relevance aur model ke response par bhi depend karta hai.

## Module 7: Chat History Management

Q22. Teacher: Chat history kya hoti hai?

Student: Chat history previous user messages aur AI responses ka record hota hai.

Conversation ke previous messages ko maintain karne se AI ko conversation ka context samajhne mein help mil sakti hai.

Example:

```
User: What is Java?
AI: Java is a programming language.

User: What are its advantages?
```

Agar previous context maintain kiya gaya hai, toh AI samajh sakta hai ki `its` ka reference Java se hai.

Agar history ya suitable context provide nahi kiya gaya, toh model ko question ka intended reference samajhne mein difficulty ho sakti hai.

Q23. Teacher: Kya Spring AI chat history manage karne mein help karta hai?

Student: Haan. Spring AI conversation history aur memory-related capabilities implement karne ke liye facilities provide karta hai. Application ke design ke according history ko store aur retrieve karne ka mechanism configure karna pad sakta hai.

## Module 8: Complete Revision – Interview Q&A

Q24. Teacher: Spring AI ke main features kaun-kaun se hain?

Student:

1. AI Model Integration – Spring applications ko AI providers se connect karna.
2. Abstraction Layer – AI model communication ko simplify karna.
3. Provider Support – Multiple supported AI providers ke saath integration.
4. Prompt Management – Prompts, templates aur message roles manage karna.
5. Embeddings – Data ko vector representations mein convert karna.
6. RAG – Relevant external data ke context mein AI responses generate karna.
7. Chat History – Conversation context maintain karne ke liye support.

Q25. Teacher: Spring AI ka use real-world projects mein kahan kar sakte hain?

Student:

- Company policy chatbot
- Customer support chatbot
- Java documentation assistant
- PDF/document question-answering system
- Product information assistant
- AI-powered text summarization application

Ye examples Spring AI ki integration aur AI-related capabilities ke practical use cases hain.

Q26. Teacher: Spring Boot aur Spring AI mein kya difference hai?

Student: Spring Boot application ko configure aur develop karna easier banata hai. Spring AI Spring application ke andar AI models integrate karne aur AI-related capabilities implement karne mein help karta hai.

| Spring Boot                                             | Spring AI                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| Application development aur configuration par focus.    | AI integration aur AI-related capabilities par focus.   |
| Web APIs, dependency injection, auto-configuration etc. | Model interaction, prompts, embeddings, RAG etc.        |
| General-purpose Spring applications ke liye.            | Spring applications mein AI features add karne ke liye. |

Q27. Teacher: Is video ka main takeaway kya hai?

Student: Spring AI ek Spring ecosystem project hai jo Java developers ko AI models ke saath work karne ka convenient way provide karta hai. Iski help se AI integration ke saath prompts, embeddings, RAG aur chat history jaise concepts ko implement karna easier ho sakta hai.

## Quick Revision – One-Line Answers

- Spring AI: Spring applications mein AI capabilities integrate karne ke liye project.
- LLM: Large Language Model.
- REST API: Applications ke beech HTTP-based communication ka common approach.
- Abstraction Layer: Complex implementation details ko hide karke interaction simplify karna.
- Prompt: AI model ko diya gaya input ya instruction.
- System Role: AI behaviour ke liye overall instructions.
- User Role: User ka message ya question.
- Embeddings: Data ki numerical vector representation.
- RAG: Retrieved external information ke context mein response generation.
- Chat History: Previous conversation messages ka record.
- Provider Switching: Different supported AI providers par switch karna; required configuration ya code changes ho sakte hain.

# Spring AI – Part 2: Why Use Spring AI? Key Features

Teacher–Student Q&A Notes | Simple Hinglish | Based on Video 2 transcript

Is part mein hum Spring AI ke important features detail mein padhenge. Har concept ke saath example diya hai, taaki aap notes ko text file mein save karke interview se pehle revise kar sako.

## Module 1: Multi-Model and Provider Support

Q1. Teacher: Spring AI use karne ka sabse important benefit kya hai?

Student: Spring AI humein different supported AI providers aur models ke saath integrate karne mein help karta hai.

Market mein multiple AI providers hain. Agar hum har provider ke liye poora integration manually likhen, toh development aur maintenance difficult ho sakta hai.

Spring AI common APIs provide karta hai, jisse provider-specific changes ko manage karna easier ho sakta hai.

Video mein diye gaye provider examples:

- OpenAI
- Microsoft Azure
- Hugging Face
- Ollama
- Amazon Bedrock

Supported providers aur available capabilities Spring AI ki version par depend karti hain.

Q2. Teacher: Provider switching kya hota hai?

Student: Provider switching ka matlab hai application mein use hone wale AI provider ko change karna.

Example:

```
Initially:
Spring Boot → Spring AI → Provider A

After switching:
Spring Boot → Spring AI → Provider B
```

Agar hum Spring AI ki common abstractions use kar rahe hain, toh provider change karne ke liye kai cases mein configuration aur dependency changes sufficient ho sakte hain. Lekin har case mein zero code changes guaranteed nahi hain.

Q3. Teacher: Kya sirf configuration change karke provider switch kar sakte hain?

Student: Video mein bataya gaya hai ki supported cases mein basic configuration change karke provider switch karna possible ho sakta hai.

For example, provider ki API key, model name aur required configuration update karni pad sakti hai.

Actual changes selected provider, Spring AI version aur application implementation par depend karenge.

## Module 2: Spring-Friendly API

Q4. Teacher: Spring AI ki Spring-Friendly API ka kya meaning hai?

Student: Spring AI Spring Framework ke familiar concepts use karta hai, jaise Spring Beans, Dependency Injection, Auto-Configuration aur fluent APIs.

Isliye Spring Boot developers ke liye AI integration comparatively familiar ho sakta hai.

Example:

Normal Spring Boot application mein:

```
@Service
public class EmployeeService {
    // Business logic
}
```

Hum `EmployeeService` ko dependency injection ke through use kar sakte hain.

Spring AI mein bhi supported AI components ko configure karke inject kiya ja sakta hai aur unke methods call kiye ja sakte hain.

Q5. Teacher: Spring Bean kya hota hai?

Student: Spring Bean ek object hota hai jise Spring IoC container manage karta hai.

Example:

```
@Service
public class GreetingService {

    public String greet() {
        return "Hello!";
    }
}
```

Spring is class ka bean create aur manage kar sakta hai.

Spring AI mein bhi configuration ke according AI-related beans available hote hain, jinhe application ke andar inject kiya ja sakta hai.

## Module 3: ChatClient and Dependency Injection

Q6. Teacher: ChatClient kya hota hai?

Student: Spring AI mein `ChatClient` ek high-level API hai jiska use chat model ke saath interact karne ke liye kiya ja sakta hai.

Iski help se hum user ka prompt AI model ko bhej sakte hain aur generated response receive kar sakte hain.

Basic flow:

```
User Question
     |
     v
Spring Boot Controller
     |
     v
Service / ChatClient
     |
     v
AI Model
     |
     v
Generated Response
```

Q7. Teacher: ChatClient ko use karne ke liye kya karna padta hai?

Student: Video ke according, basic process ye hai:

1. Required Spring AI dependency add karna.
2. AI provider ki required configuration set karna.
3. Spring AI ke available `ChatClient.Builder` bean ko inject karna.
4. Builder ki `build()` method se `ChatClient` banana.
5. `ChatClient` ke methods use karke AI model se interact karna.

Example — basic structure:

```
@Service
public class AiService {

    private final ChatClient chatClient;

    public AiService(ChatClient.Builder builder) {
        this.chatClient = builder.build();
    }

    public String ask(String question) {
        return chatClient.prompt()
                .user(question)
                .call()
                .content();
    }
}
```

Note: Ye illustrative code hai. Isse run karne ke liye compatible Spring AI dependency, provider configuration aur required imports chahiye.

Q8. Teacher: ChatClient.Builder aur ChatClient mein kya difference hai?

Student:

- `ChatClient.Builder`: ChatClient ko configure aur construct karne ke liye use hota hai.
- `ChatClient`: Build hone ke baad prompts bhejne aur responses receive karne ke liye use hota hai.

Simple example:

```
ChatClient chatClient = builder.build();
```

Yahan `builder.build()` ek `ChatClient` return karta hai.

Q9. Teacher: Kya ChatClient ko normal service ki tarah use kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan. Hum ise apni service class mein inject karke business logic ke andar use kar sakte hain.

Jaise normal service kisi database ya doosri service ke saath kaam karti hai, waise hi AI service `ChatClient` ki help se AI model ke saath interact kar sakti hai.

## Module 4: Prompt Management

Q10. Teacher: Prompt Management kya hota hai?

Student: Prompt Management ka matlab hai AI model ko diye jaane wale instructions aur input ko properly create, organize aur manage karna.

Achha prompt likhne se model ko expected output samajhne mein help milti hai.

Spring AI prompt management ke liye templates, dynamic values aur different message roles ko handle karne ki facilities provide karta hai.

Q11. Teacher: Prompts ko Java code mein hardcode karne se kya problem hoti hai?

Student: Agar hum poora prompt Java code mein string ke form mein likhte hain, toh usko maintain aur reuse karna difficult ho sakta hai.

Example of hardcoded prompt:

```
String prompt =
    "Hello Rahul, explain Spring Boot in simple words.";
```

Ab agar naam `Rahul` ki jagah `Amit` karna ho ya prompt ko baar-baar use karna ho, toh code modify karna pad sakta hai.

Q12. Teacher: External prompt template kya hota hai?

Student: External prompt template ek separate resource ya file mein stored prompt hota hai, jise application zaroorat ke according read aur use kar sakti hai.

Video mein `.st` file ka example diya gaya hai.

Example template:

```
Hello, my name is {name}.
How can you greet me back nicely?
```

Yahan `{name}` ek dynamic parameter hai.

Run time par hum alag-alag values pass kar sakte hain:

- `name = Rahul`
- `name = Amit`
- `name = Priya`

Isse same prompt ko multiple times reuse kiya ja sakta hai.

Q13. Teacher: Dynamic Prompt kya hota hai?

Student: Dynamic Prompt woh prompt hai jisme kuch values application ke run time par provide karti hai.

Example:

```
Explain {topic} in {language}.
```

Input values:

```
topic = Spring AI
language = Hinglish
```

Final prompt:

```
Explain Spring AI in Hinglish.
```

Q14. Teacher: Prompt Management mein different roles ka kya use hai?

Student: Prompt ke saath different message roles use kiye ja sakte hain, jaise:

- System: Model ke behaviour aur instructions ko define karta hai.
- User: User ka question ya input represent karta hai.
- Assistant: Conversation mein AI ke response ko represent karta hai.

Example:

```
System:
You are a Java teacher.
Explain everything in simple Hinglish.

User:
Explain HashMap.
```

Isse AI ko response ka expected style samajhne mein help milti hai.

## Module 5: Embeddings and Vector Database

Q15. Teacher: Spring AI mein Embedding Support ka kya meaning hai?

Student: Spring AI supported embedding models ke through text ko numerical vectors mein convert karne aur un vectors ko aage use karne mein help karta hai.

Ye vectors text ke semantic meaning ko represent karne ke liye use kiye ja sakte hain.

Example:

```
Text:
"Java is used for backend development."

        |
        v
Embedding Model

        |
        v
[0.12, -0.34, 0.87, ...]
```

Actual vector ki length aur values embedding model par depend karti hain.

Q16. Teacher: Vector Database kya hota hai?

Student: Vector Database aisa database hai jo vector representations ko store aur similarity-based retrieval jaise tasks support karne ke liye design kiya jaata hai.

Iska use documents ya text ke relevant parts retrieve karne mein kiya ja sakta hai.

Video mein examples ke roop mein PostgreSQL, Redis aur doosre vector-storage solutions ka mention hai. Inka vector support specific extensions, modules ya configurations par depend karta hai.

Q17. Teacher: Spring AI ka VectorStore kya hota hai?

Student: `VectorStore` Spring AI ki abstraction hai jo supported vector stores ke saath documents ko store karne aur similarity search jaise operations perform karne ke liye common interface provide karti hai.

Basic flow:

```
Documents
    |
    v
Split into Chunks
    |
    v
Generate Embeddings
    |
    v
Store in Vector Database
    |
    v
Retrieve Relevant Information
```

Spring AI suitable document-processing aur vector-store components provide karta hai. Lekin required models, storage configuration aur integrations ko setup karna padta hai.

## Module 6: RAG – Retrieval-Augmented Generation

Q18. Teacher: RAG ka full form kya hai?

Student: RAG ka full form Retrieval-Augmented Generation hai.

Iska purpose hai relevant external information retrieve karke AI model ko context dena, taaki model us context ke basis par answer generate kar sake.

Q19. Teacher: RAG ki zaroorat kyun padti hai?

Student: AI model ko hamari company ke private documents, internal policies ya application-specific data automatically pata nahi hota.

Agar hum poora data har question ke saath bhejte hain, toh prompt bahut bada ho sakta hai aur token usage badh sakta hai.

RAG mein hum relevant information retrieve karke model ko dete hain, instead of sending the entire knowledge base every time.

Q20. Teacher: RAG practically kaise kaam karta hai?

Student: RAG ke main steps hain:

1. Documents collect karna.
2. Documents ko manageable chunks mein split karna.
3. Chunks ke embeddings generate karna.
4. Embeddings aur associated document information ko vector store mein save karna.
5. User ka question receive karna.
6. Question ke basis par relevant information retrieve karna.
7. Retrieved context aur question ko AI model ko dena.
8. AI-generated response user ko return karna.

Company Documents

Chunks + Embeddings

Vector Store

Documents / Vectors store hote hain

User question aane par retrieval process chalta hai

Relevant Context + User Question

AI-Generated Answer

Q21. Teacher: RAG mein chunks ka kya meaning hai?

Student: Chunks documents ke chhote parts hote hain. Bade documents ko chunks mein divide karne se relevant information retrieve karna easier ho sakta hai.

Example:

```
Company Policy Document
    |
    +-- Chunk 1: Leave Policy
    +-- Chunk 2: Refund Policy
    +-- Chunk 3: Work From Home Policy
```

Agar user refund policy ke baare mein poochta hai, toh retrieval process relevant refund-policy content find karne ki koshish karega.

Q22. Teacher: Kya RAG ke liye vector database compulsory hai?

Student: Video mein RAG ko vector store ke through explain kiya gaya hai. Practical implementations mein vector search ek common approach hai, lekin retrieval ke alternative methods bhi exist karte hain. Isliye har RAG implementation mein vector database mandatory nahi hota.

Q23. Teacher: RAG aur normal LLM request mein kya difference hai?

| Normal LLM Request                                                  | RAG-based Request                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Question model ko bheja jaata hai.                                  | Question ke saath relevant retrieved context bhi diya jaata hai.       |
| Application-specific data model ko pata na ho sakta hai.            | Relevant application-specific data retrieve kiya ja sakta hai.         |
| Answer model ke available knowledge aur input par depend karta hai. | Answer retrieved context aur model ki capability par depend karta hai. |

## Module 7: Chat History and Memory

Q24. Teacher: Chat History aur Chat Memory ka use kya hai?

Student: Inka purpose previous conversation ka context maintain karna hai, taaki chatbot follow-up questions ko samajh sake.

Example:

```
User: What is Spring Boot?
AI: Spring Boot simplifies Spring application development.

User: What are its advantages?
```

Agar previous conversation ka context available hai, toh AI samajh sakta hai ki `its` ka reference Spring Boot hai.

Q25. Teacher: Kya Spring AI automatically har conversation ko permanently remember karta hai?

Student: Nahi. Ye assume nahi karna chahiye ki har conversation automatically aur permanently save hoti hai.

Application ko appropriate chat-memory implementation aur configuration chahiye ho sakti hai. Memory temporary storage ya persistent storage ke through implement ki ja sakti hai, depending on the chosen design.

Video mein configurable conversation history aur storage options ka concept explain kiya gaya hai.

Q26. Teacher: Chat Memory real-world projects mein kahan useful hai?

Student:

- Customer support chatbot
- Personal AI assistant
- Multi-turn question-answering system
- Conversation-based helpdesk

Chat memory ka purpose relevant previous interactions ko available rakhna hai. Isse chatbot ko har baar poori conversation dobara explain karne ki zaroorat kam ho sakti hai.

## Module 8: Streaming Responses

Q27. Teacher: Streaming Response kya hota hai?

Student: Streaming response mein AI ka output poora generate hone ka wait karne ke bajay chunks mein receive kiya ja sakta hai.

Chat interface mein text dheere-dheere appear hota hai, jisse user ko response jaldi dikhna start ho jaata hai.

Normal response:

```
User Question
     |
     v
AI generates complete answer
     |
     v
Complete response displayed
```

Streaming response:

```
User Question
     |
     v
AI generates output
     |
     +--> Chunk 1: Spring
     +--> Chunk 2: Boot is
     +--> Chunk 3: a framework...
     |
     v
User sees progressive output
```

Ye chunks sirf example hain. Actual streaming format provider aur implementation par depend karta hai.

Q28. Teacher: Streaming response ka main benefit kya hai?

Student: Streaming user experience ko improve kar sakta hai, kyunki user response ka initial part jaldi dekh sakta hai, bina poora answer generate hone ka wait kiye.

## Module 9: Spring Ecosystem Integration

Q29. Teacher: Spring AI ka Spring Ecosystem ke saath kya relation hai?

Student: Spring AI, Spring ecosystem ke saath integrate karne ke liye design kiya gaya hai.

Video mein following integrations ka mention hai:

- Spring Boot: Application configuration aur development.
- Spring Web / REST APIs: Application endpoints create karna.
- Spring Security: Authentication aur authorization.
- Spring Data JPA / JDBC: Relational database access.
- Spring Cloud Config: Centralized configuration management.

Exact integration aur setup requirements selected libraries aur versions par depend karte hain.

Q30. Teacher: Spring Cloud Config AI application mein kaise useful ho sakta hai?

Student: Spring Cloud Config centralized configuration management mein help karta hai.

Example ke liye, application ke configuration values ko centrally manage kiya ja sakta hai. Sensitive API keys ko manage karte waqt appropriate secret-management practices follow karni chahiye; unhe plain text mein expose nahi karna chahiye.

## Module 10: Real-World Example – Customer Support Chatbot

Q31. Teacher: Spring AI ki help se company refund-policy chatbot kaise bana sakte hain?

Student: Suppose company ka refund policy document hamare paas hai.

User poochta hai:

`What is your refund policy?`

AI model ko company ki internal refund policy automatically pata nahi hogi.

Hum RAG-based application bana sakte hain:

1. Refund-policy document ko process karenge.
2. Document ko chunks mein divide karenge.
3. Embeddings generate karke suitable vector store mein save karenge.
4. User ka question receive karenge.
5. Relevant refund-policy information retrieve karenge.
6. Retrieved context ko AI model ko provide karenge.
7. Model ke response ko user tak return karenge.

Agar relevant information available nahi hai, toh application ko guess karne ke bajay user ko batana chahiye ki policy information nahi mili.

Q32. Teacher: Is chatbot mein Spring AI ke kaun-kaun se features use honge?

Student:

- `ChatClient` — AI model ke saath interact karne ke liye.
- Prompt Management — Model ko instructions dene ke liye.
- Embeddings — Documents ko vector representations mein convert karne ke liye.
- VectorStore — Relevant information store aur retrieve karne ke liye.
- RAG — Retrieved policy ke context mein answer generate karne ke liye.
- Chat Memory — Follow-up questions ke liye conversation context maintain karne ke liye.
- Streaming — Response ko progressively display karne ke liye, agar application is feature ko implement karti hai.

## Module 11: Interview Revision – Important Questions

Q33. Teacher: Spring AI ke important features list karo.

Student:

1. Multiple supported AI providers ke saath integration.
2. Spring-friendly APIs aur dependency injection.
3. `ChatClient` ke through model interaction.
4. Prompt management aur templates.
5. Embedding model support.
6. Vector store integration.
7. RAG-based retrieval aur generation.
8. Chat history aur memory capabilities.
9. Streaming responses.
10. Spring ecosystem ke saath integration.

Q34. Teacher: Spring AI ka sabse bada advantage kya hai?

Student: Spring AI AI integration ke liye common abstractions aur reusable components provide karta hai. Isse provider integration, prompt handling aur retrieval-based AI applications develop karna easier ho sakta hai.

Q35. Teacher: Spring AI use karte waqt kya yaad rakhna chahiye?

Student:

- Correct dependency aur compatible version use karna.
- Provider configuration aur API keys securely manage karna.
- Prompts ko properly design karna.
- RAG mein relevant aur reliable documents retrieve karna.
- Chat history ke liye suitable memory strategy select karna.
- AI responses ko validate karna, kyunki AI hamesha correct answer guarantee nahi karta.

# Spring AI – Part 3: First Spring Boot Application with OpenAI

Teacher–Student Q&A Notes | Simple Hinglish | Detailed theory + practical code | Based on your uploaded video transcript

Is video mein hum practically seekhenge ki Spring Boot application ko OpenAI model se kaise connect karte hain, API key kaise configure karte hain, `ChatClient` kaise use karte hain aur user ke question ka AI-generated answer kaise return karte hain.

Video ka main practical flow:

1. OpenAI API key create karna.
2. Spring Initializr se Spring Boot project banana.
3. Spring AI OpenAI dependency add karna.
4. API key ko environment variable se configure karna.
5. `ChatClient.Builder` se `ChatClient` create karna.
6. REST endpoint banana.
7. Postman se question bhejna aur AI response receive karna.
8. AI model aur uski supported settings configure karna.

## Module 1: Project ka Use Case

Q1. Teacher: Hum kis type ki application bana rahe hain?

Student: Hum ek Spring Boot application bana rahe hain jo AI model se communicate karegi.

Suppose client kehta hai ki existing application mein ek chatbot add karna hai. User chatbot se questions pooch sake aur AI answers generate kare.

Example:

```
User:
What is Spring Boot?

Spring Boot Application:
Question receive karegi.

Spring AI:
AI model ke saath communication handle karne mein help karega.

AI Model:
Answer generate karega.

Application:
Answer user ko return karegi.
```

Q2. Teacher: Hum OpenAI ko example ke roop mein kyun use kar rahe hain?

Student: Video mein OpenAI ke hosted language models ko example ke roop mein use kiya gaya hai. OpenAI API ke through application request bhej sakti hai aur model se response receive kar sakti hai.

Spring AI humein is integration ko Spring Boot application ke andar manage karne ka convenient way deta hai.

Q3. Teacher: Kya direct REST API call ke bajay Spring AI use karna zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi. Direct API call bhi possible hai. Lekin Spring AI ke through hum `ChatClient` aur doosri AI-related abstractions use kar sakte hain, jo development ko simpler banane mein help karti hain.

## Module 2: OpenAI API Key

Q4. Teacher: API key kya hoti hai?

Student: API key ek credential hoti hai jiske through application API provider ke saamne apni request authenticate kar sakti hai.

OpenAI API use karne ke liye humein provider ki current authentication aur account requirements follow karni padti hain.

Q5. Teacher: OpenAI API key create karne ke basic steps kya hain?

Student: Video ke according, process ye hai:

1. OpenAI Platform website open karo: [OpenAI Platform](https://platform.openai.com/?utm_source=chatgpt.com).
2. Apne account se sign in karo.
3. API keys section open karo.
4. New API key create karne ka option select karo.
5. Key ko securely copy karke secret location mein store karo.
6. Apne project ke environment variable mein key configure karo.

Website ka interface video ke baad change ho sakta hai. Current options account aur platform settings ke according different ho sakte hain.

Q6. Teacher: API key ko Java code ya application.properties mein directly hardcode kyun nahi karna chahiye?

Student: API key secret credential hai. Agar key source code mein hardcode kar di, toh Git repository, shared files ya logs ke through leak ho sakti hai.

Isliye key ko environment variable ya suitable secret-management solution mein rakhna better practice hai.

Wrong approach:

```
spring.ai.openai.api-key=sk-your-secret-key
```

Better approach:

```
spring.ai.openai.api-key=${OPENAI_API_KEY}
```

Yahan `${OPENAI_API_KEY}` ka value environment variable se read kiya jaata hai.

Important: Real API key ko source code, public repository ya shared notes mein kabhi paste mat karna.

## Module 3: OpenAI API Pricing and Model Selection

Q7. Teacher: Kya OpenAI API use karna hamesha free hota hai?

Student: Nahi. Hosted API models use karne par usage-based charges apply ho sakte hain. Actual pricing model aur service ke according vary karti hai.

Video mein bataya gaya hai ki pricing ko samajhna important hai, kyunki model requests mein input aur output tokens consume hote hain.

Q8. Teacher: Token kya hota hai?

Student: Token text ka ek unit hota hai jise language model input aur output process karne ke liye use karta hai.

Token hamesha ek complete word ke equal nahi hota. Ek word multiple tokens mein split ho sakta hai, aur tokenization language aur text par depend karti hai.

Example:

```
Input:
Explain Spring AI.

Model processes:
Input tokens

Model generates:
Output tokens
```

API pricing model ke according input aur output token usage alag-alag charge ho sakta hai. Kuch models mein cached input ya doosre billing categories bhi ho sakte hain.

Q9. Teacher: Model select karte waqt kin baaton ka dhyan rakhna chahiye?

Student: Model select karne se pehle ye points check karne chahiye:

- Capabilities: Model kis type ke tasks support karta hai?
- Quality: Kya model hamare use case ke liye suitable hai?
- Speed: Response kitni jaldi milta hai?
- Pricing: Input aur output usage ka cost kya hai?
- Configuration: Kya model required parameters support karta hai?
- Limits: Kya model ke request, context ya output limits hamare project ke liye suitable hain?

Video mein OpenAI Platform ke model catalog aur pricing documentation ko check karne par emphasis diya gaya hai.

Q10. Teacher: Kya local AI model bhi use kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan. Video mein Ollama aur Docker jaise tools ke through local models run karne ka option mention kiya gaya hai.

Local model use karne se hosted API charges avoid ho sakte hain, lekin hardware, RAM, storage, electricity aur performance jaise factors consider karne padte hain.

## Module 4: Spring Initializr se Project Banana

Q11. Teacher: Spring Initializr kya hai?

Student: Spring Initializr ek tool hai jo Spring Boot project ka initial structure generate karta hai. Isse hum project metadata, Java version aur required dependencies select kar sakte hain.

Website: [Spring Initializr](https://start.spring.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com).

Q12. Teacher: Video mein project ke liye kaun-si settings select ki gayi hain?

Student: Video mein example ke roop mein ye settings use ki gayi hain:

| Setting        | Video mein selected value |
| -------------- | ------------------------- |
| Project        | Maven                     |
| Language       | Java                      |
| Spring Boot    | 3.5.5                     |
| Group          | `com.spring.ai`           |
| Artifact       | `first-project`           |

| Java           | 21                        |
| Web dependency | Spring Web                |
| AI dependency  | OpenAI                    |

Ye video ke example ki settings hain. Naya project banate waqt compatible stable Spring Boot aur Spring AI versions choose karna.

Q13. Teacher: Spring Web dependency kyun add karte hain?

Student: Spring Web ki help se hum REST endpoints create kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
GET /chat?q=What is Java?
```

Ye request hamare Spring Boot controller tak pahunch sakti hai.

Q14. Teacher: Spring AI OpenAI dependency ka kya role hai?

Student: Ye dependency Spring AI ki OpenAI integration provide karti hai, jisse application supported OpenAI models ke saath communicate kar sakti hai.

Video mein selected dependency ka naam `Spring AI OpenAI` starter hai. Current Spring AI version ke according dependency coordinates verify karna chahiye.

Maven dependency ka illustrative example:

```
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.springframework.ai</groupId>
    <artifactId>spring-ai-starter-model-openai</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Spring AI ke versions ke according dependency management setup bhi required ho sakta hai. Isliye compatible version ke liye [Spring AI official documentation](https://docs.spring.io/spring-ai/reference/?utm_source=chatgpt.com) follow karna.

Q15. Teacher: Kya Lombok dependency bhi add karni chahiye?

Student: Lombok optional hai. Video mein suggest kiya gaya hai ki learning ke liye unnecessary dependencies add nahi karni chahiye.

Agar project mein Lombok ki requirement nahi hai, toh use add karna zaroori nahi hai.

## Module 5: Application Properties Configuration

Q16. Teacher: `application.properties` file ka use kya hai?

Student: `application.properties` Spring Boot application ki configuration values define karne ke liye use hoti hai.

Video mein application port aur OpenAI API key property configure ki gayi hai.

Example:

```
server.port=8081

spring.ai.openai.api-key=${OPENAI_API_KEY}
```

Is configuration mein:

- `server.port=8081` application ko port 8081 par run karne ke liye configure karta hai.
- `spring.ai.openai.api-key` OpenAI API key ko environment variable se read karta hai.

Q17. Teacher: Environment variable kaise configure karenge?

Student: Video mein IntelliJ IDEA ke Run Configuration ke through environment variable set kiya gaya hai.

Basic steps:

1. IntelliJ mein Run → Edit Configurations open karo.
2. Apni Spring Boot run configuration select karo.
3. Environment variables ka option open karo.
4. Variable name `OPENAI_API_KEY` enter karo.
5. Value mein apni actual API key securely set karo.
6. Configuration save karke application run karo.

Example:

```
OPENAI_API_KEY=your_actual_secret_key
```

`your_actual_secret_key` sirf placeholder hai; iski jagah apni valid key use karni hoti hai.

Agar aap operating system ke environment variables use karte ho, toh variable set karne ke baad IDE ya terminal ko us value ko read karne layak environment milna chahiye.

Q18. Teacher: Application start hote waqt configuration error kyun aa sakta hai?

Student: Spring AI ki OpenAI integration ko required configuration nahi milti, toh application startup par error aa sakta hai.

Common checks:

- API key missing toh nahi hai?
- Environment variable ka naam sahi hai?
- Property name correct hai?
- Required dependency add hui hai?
- Maven dependencies successfully download hui hain?
- Spring AI aur Spring Boot versions compatible hain?

Important concept: Spring Boot auto-configuration dependencies aur available configuration properties ke basis par required beans create karne ki koshish karti hai.

## Module 6: ChatClient Bean and Dependency Injection

Q19. Teacher: `ChatClient` kya hai aur iska use kyun karte hain?

Student: `ChatClient` Spring AI ki API hai jiska use AI model ko prompts bhejne aur response receive karne ke liye hota hai.

Humein har request ke liye manually HTTP request format karne ki zaroorat nahi hoti. Spring AI ki supported integration request-response handling ko simplify karti hai.

Q20. Teacher: Kya `ChatClient` ko directly `@Autowired` kar sakte hain?

Student: Video mein ek important point explain kiya gaya hai: uske configuration setup mein `ChatClient` ka direct bean available nahi tha. Spring AI ne `ChatClient.Builder` provide kiya tha, jise use karke `ChatClient` build karna tha.

Example:

```
private final ChatClient chatClient;

public ChatController(ChatClient.Builder builder) {
    this.chatClient = builder.build();
}
```

Is code mein:

1. Spring container se `ChatClient.Builder` inject hota hai.
2. `builder.build()` se `ChatClient` create hota hai.
3. Created client ko `chatClient` field mein store kiya jaata hai.

Modern Spring applications mein constructor injection recommended hai. Iske liye constructor par `@Autowired` lagana aam taur par zaroori nahi hota, agar sirf ek constructor hai.

Q21. Teacher: `ChatClient.Builder` aur `ChatClient` ko simple example se samjhao.

Student: Builder ko object create karne ke tool ki tarah samjho.

```
Spring Container
       |
       v
ChatClient.Builder
       |
       | build()
       v
ChatClient
       |
       | prompt(...)
       v
AI Model Request
       |
       v
AI Response
```

Builder se client create hota hai, aur client se hum model ko prompt bhejte hain.

Q22. Teacher: Dependency Injection ka kya benefit hai?

Student: Dependency Injection se Spring required objects ko application classes mein provide kar sakta hai. Isse object creation aur dependency management centralized ho sakta hai.

Example:

```
public ChatController(ChatClient.Builder builder) {
    this.chatClient = builder.build();
}
```

Yahan Spring builder ki dependency provide karta hai.

## Module 7: Create REST Controller

Q23. Teacher: Ab hum AI ke liye REST endpoint kaise banayenge?

Student: Hum ek controller create karenge jo user ka question receive karega, `ChatClient` se AI model ko question bhejega aur generated answer return karega.

Video mein endpoint ka example hai:

```
GET http://localhost:8081/chat?q=How can you help me?
```

Yahan:

- `localhost` ka matlab current computer hai.
- `8081` application port hai.
- `/chat` endpoint path hai.
- `q` query parameter hai.

Q24. Teacher: `@RestController` kya karta hai?

Student: `@RestController` Spring MVC controller define karta hai jiske handler methods ka returned data response body ke roop mein serialize hota hai.

Q25. Teacher: `@GetMapping` aur `@RequestParam` ka kya use hai?

Student:

- `@GetMapping("/chat")` HTTP GET request ko `/chat` path ke saath map karta hai.
- `@RequestParam("q")` request ke query parameter `q` ki value method mein receive karta hai.

Example:

```
/chat?q=What%20is%20Java
```

Controller ko `q` ki value `What is Java` mil sakti hai.

Q26. Teacher: Complete controller ka code dikhao.

Student: Neeche video ke concept par based clean implementation hai:

```
package com.spring.ai.firstproject.controller;

import org.springframework.ai.chat.client.ChatClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
public class ChatController {

    private final ChatClient chatClient;

    public ChatController(ChatClient.Builder builder) {
        this.chatClient = builder.build();
    }

    @GetMapping("/chat")
    public String chat(@RequestParam("q") String question) {

        return chatClient.prompt()
                .user(question)
                .call()
                .content();
    }
}
```

Package name ko apne actual project ke package ke according update karna. Ye example current compatible Spring AI setup assume karta hai.

Q27. Teacher: Is code ka execution flow explain karo.

Student:

1. User `/chat` endpoint ko request bhejta hai.
2. `@RequestParam("q")` question receive karta hai.
3. `chatClient.prompt()` prompt request prepare karta hai.
4. `.user(question)` user ka question set karta hai.
5. `.call()` synchronous model interaction perform karta hai.
6. `.content()` generated response ka text extract karta hai.
7. Controller response ko user ko return karta hai.

Q28. Teacher: `.call()` aur `.content()` ka kya role hai?

Student:

- `.call()` synchronous request-response interaction execute karta hai.
- `.content()` response object se generated text obtain karta hai.

Example:

```
String answer = chatClient.prompt()
        .user("What is Spring Boot?")
        .call()
        .content();
```

Yahan `answer` mein model ka returned text store hoga.

## Module 8: Postman se Application Test Karna

Q29. Teacher: Postman kya hai?

Student: Postman ek API testing tool hai. Iski help se hum HTTP requests send karke API ke responses check kar sakte hain.

Q30. Teacher: Apne chatbot endpoint ko Postman se kaise test karenge?

Student: Ye steps follow karenge:

1. Spring Boot application run karo.
2. Postman open karo.
3. HTTP method `GET` select karo.
4. URL enter karo.
5. Send par click karo.
6. Response body check karo.

Example request:

```
GET http://localhost:8081/chat?q=How%20can%20you%20help%20me?
```

Expected response ka example:

```
I can help you with programming questions,
explanations, summaries, and many other tasks.
```

Ye sirf illustrative response hai. Actual answer model ke according different ho sakta hai.

Q31. Teacher: Agar endpoint se AI ka response aa raha hai, toh iska kya meaning hai?

Student: Iska matlab hai ki application ka basic integration kaam kar raha hai:

- Spring Boot application start ho rahi hai.
- Controller request receive kar raha hai.
- `ChatClient` available hai.
- Provider configuration aur authentication kaam kar rahe hain.
- AI model se response receive ho raha hai.

Lekin ek successful test ka matlab ye nahi ki production ke liye security, error handling aur billing controls bhi complete hain.

## Module 9: AI Model Change Karna

Q32. Teacher: Kya hum application mein AI model change kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan. Spring AI mein provider ke supported configuration options ke through model select kiya ja sakta hai.

Video mein `application.properties` mein model name configure karne ka example diya gaya hai.

Illustrative configuration:

```
spring.ai.openai.chat.options.model=YOUR_MODEL_ID
```

`YOUR_MODEL_ID` ki jagah selected provider ka actual supported model ID use karna hai.

Model ID ko OpenAI Platform ke current model catalog aur documentation se verify karna chahiye.

Q33. Teacher: Model change karne ke baad kya-kya check karna chahiye?

Student: Ye points important hain:

1. Model ID correct hai?
2. API account ko us model ka access hai?
3. Model ki pricing kya hai?
4. Model required input types support karta hai?
5. Configured parameters model support karta hai?
6. API request successfully execute ho rahi hai?

Video mein model switching ke dauran invalid model aur unsupported temperature jaise errors aaye the.

Q34. Teacher: Temperature parameter kya hota hai?

Student: Temperature ek model-generation parameter hai jo, supported models mein, output ki randomness ya variability ko influence kar sakta hai.

- Lower temperature se output comparatively predictable ho sakta hai.
- Higher temperature se output mein zyada variation aa sakta hai.

Lekin temperature ka effect model ke according vary karta hai. Har model is parameter ko support nahi karta.

Example configuration:

```
spring.ai.openai.chat.options.temperature=0.2
```

Ye value sirf tab use karni chahiye jab selected model is parameter ko support karta ho.

Q35. Teacher: Video mein temperature error kyun aaya tha?

Student: Video mein selected model ne default temperature value `0.7` support nahi ki. Isliye request reject ho gayi.

Is situation mein humein model ki supported parameters check karni chahiye aur configuration ko uske according update karna chahiye.

Important: Har error ko solve karne ke liye temperature change karna zaroori nahi hai. Pehle error message aur selected model ki documentation check karo.

## Module 10: AI Ko Application ka Private Data Kaise Pata Chalega?

Q36. Teacher: Agar user AI se poochta hai, "What is my name?", toh kya AI automatically answer de sakta hai?

Student: Zaroori nahi. Agar user ka naam conversation ya application ke provided context mein available nahi hai, toh AI ko naam pata nahi hoga.

Example:

```
User: What is my name?

AI: You haven't told me your name yet.
```

Q37. Teacher: Kya AI model ke paas hamari application ka database automatically available hota hai?

Student: Nahi. Sirf Spring AI aur ChatClient use karne se model ko application ka private database automatically access nahi milta.

Agar model ko company documents, employee details ya application-specific data ke basis par answer dena hai, toh humein suitable mechanism implement karna padta hai.

Possible approaches:

- User-specific context prompt mein provide karna.
- Database se relevant information retrieve karna.
- RAG use karke relevant documents retrieve karna.
- Chat history maintain karna, jab conversational context required ho.

Ye next practical step hai: AI ko sirf general questions answer karne ke bajay application ke data ke saath kaam karna sikhana.

## Module 11: Troubleshooting – Common Errors

| Error / Problem               | Kya check karna hai?                                                        |
| ----------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| API key missing               | `OPENAI_API_KEY` environment variable check karo.                           |
| `ChatClient` bean not found   | `ChatClient.Builder` injection aur Spring AI configuration verify karo.     |
| Invalid model ID              | Correct model ID aur account access verify karo.                            |
| Unsupported temperature       | Selected model ke supported parameters check karo.                          |
| Connection / dependency error | Maven reload, network aur dependency versions check karo.                   |
| Application port error        | Port `8081` already in use toh nahi hai, check karo.                        |
| AI ko private data pata nahi  | Context retrieval, prompt ya RAG implement karne ki requirement check karo. |

Student:

1. OpenAI Platform par account aur API access configure karna.
2. API key securely create aur store karna.
3. Spring Initializr se Maven-based Spring Boot project banana.
4. Spring Web aur OpenAI ki compatible Spring AI dependency add karna.
5. `application.properties` mein port aur API key reference configure karna.
6. Environment variable set karna.
7. `ChatClient.Builder` ko constructor injection se receive karna.
8. `builder.build()` se `ChatClient` create karna.
9. Controller mein `/chat` endpoint banana.
10. `ChatClient` se question bhejna aur response receive karna.
11. Postman se endpoint test karna.
12. Zaroorat ke according model aur supported options configure karna.

Q39. Teacher: Is video se sabse important concept kya seekha?

Student: Humne seekha ki Spring Boot application mein AI integrate karne ke liye Spring AI kaise use karte hain. `ChatClient.Builder` se `ChatClient` create karke question AI model ko bhej sakte hain aur generated response REST endpoint se return kar sakte hain.

## Quick Revision – One-Line Notes

- API Key: Provider API ko authenticate karne ke liye credential.
- Environment Variable: Configuration value ko application ke bahar supply karne ka method.
- Spring Initializr: Spring Boot project generate karne ka tool.
- Spring Web: REST endpoints banane ke liye dependency.
- Spring AI OpenAI Starter: OpenAI integration provide karne wali dependency.
- Auto-Configuration: Spring Boot ka configuration ke basis par beans setup karna.
- Dependency Injection: Required dependencies ko Spring container se receive karna.
- ChatClient.Builder: ChatClient create karne ke liye builder.
- ChatClient: AI model ke saath interaction ke liye API.
- `@RestController`: REST request handlers define karne ke liye annotation.
- `@GetMapping`: HTTP GET endpoint map karne ke liye annotation.
- `@RequestParam`: Query parameter receive karne ke liye annotation.
- `.call()`: Synchronous AI interaction execute karna.
- `.content()`: Generated response text extract karna.
- Model ID: Selected AI model ko identify karne wala identifier.
- Temperature: Supported models mein output variability influence karne wala parameter.
- RAG: Application ke relevant external data ke basis par AI answers generate karne ka approach.

# Spring AI + Ollama — Part 4: Complete Setup Notes

Format: Teacher → Student | Simple Hinglish | Text-file friendly | Based on your uploaded video transcript

Ye notes aapki video “Setup Spring AI Project with Free LLM – Ollama Detail Guide” par based hain. Ismein hum Spring Boot ko Ollama ke local AI model se connect karna, Maven dependencies add karna, configuration karna aur Postman se API test karna seekhenge.&#x20;

Pasted markdown.md



## 1. Is video mein hum kya seekhenge?

Teacher: Aaj hum kya implement karne wale hain?

Student: Sir, hum ek Spring Boot application banayenge jo Ollama ke through locally running AI model se communicate karegi.

Main steps:

1. Ollama install aur run karna.
2. Ek LLM model download karna.
3. Spring Boot project create karna.
4. Spring AI Ollama dependency add karna.
5. `application.properties` mein configuration karna.
6. `ChatClient.Builder` se `ChatClient` banana.
7. REST API create karna.
8. Postman se AI response test karna.

Expected result:

```
User
  |
  v
Postman
  |
  v
Spring Boot REST API
  |
  v
Spring AI ChatClient
  |
  v
Ollama Server
  |
  v
Local LLM (Code Llama)
  |
  v
AI Response
```

## 2. Ollama kya hai?

Teacher: Ollama kya hota hai?

Student: Ollama ek software/tool hai jo supported AI models ko apne computer par locally run karne mein help karta hai.

Teacher: Iska benefit kya hai?

Student: Sir, hum hosted AI service ki API use karne ke bajay local model ke saath experiment kar sakte hain. Isse hosted API usage charges se bach sakte hain.

Important points:

- Ollama: Local models run aur manage karne ka tool.
- LLM: Large Language Model, jo text generate kar sakta hai.
- Local model: Aapke computer par run hone wala model.
- Model download: Model ki files local machine par download hoti hain.

Example: Video mein Code Llama model use kiya gaya hai, jo programming questions aur code generation ke liye use ho sakta hai.

Dhyan rakho: Local model chalane ke liye sufficient RAM, disk space aur processing power chahiye. Model download karne ke liye internet bhi lag sakta hai. Local inference ke liye hosted API billing nahi hoti, lekin computer ki electricity aur hardware resources use hote hain.

## 3. Step 1 — Ollama install aur verify karo

Teacher: Sabse pehle kya karenge?

Student: Ollama install karenge aur verify karenge ki woh run ho raha hai ya nahi.

Official website: [Ollama official website](https://ollama.com/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

Installation ke baad terminal ya PowerShell open karo.

Command 1: Version check

```
ollama --version
```

Command 2: Available local models check

```
ollama list
```

Command 3: Code Llama model run/download karna

```
ollama run codellama:latest
```

Agar model pehle se download nahi hai, to Ollama use download karne ki koshish karega. Download hone ke baad prompt par question pooch sakte ho.

Example:

```
What is a prime number?
```

Java code bhi pooch sakte ho:

```
Write a Java program to check whether a number is prime.
```

Teacher: Agar model terminal mein answer de raha hai, to kya verify hua?

Student: Model Ollama ke through locally run ho raha hai aur prompt ka response de raha hai.

## 4. Step 2 — Spring Boot project create karo

Teacher: Ab Spring Boot project kaise banayenge?

Student: Sir, Spring Initializr se project generate karenge.

Open: [Spring Initializr](https://start.spring.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

Video mein use ki gayi settings:

| Setting     | Value               |
| ----------- | ------------------- |
| Project     | Maven               |
| Language    | Java                |
| Group       | `com.spring.ollama` |
| Artifact    | `spring-ai-ollama`  |
| Packaging   | Jar                 |
| Java        | 21 in the video     |
| Spring Boot | 3.5.5 in the video  |

Note: Ye video mein use ki gayi versions hain. Aap apne installed JDK aur Spring AI version ke saath compatible versions select karna.

### Dependencies add karo

Dependency 1: Spring Web

Isse hum REST API aur controller create kar sakte hain.

Dependency 2: Spring AI Ollama

Isse Spring AI ko Ollama ke saath integrate karne ke liye required support milta hai.

Project generate karo, ZIP extract karo aur IntelliJ IDEA mein open karo. Maven dependencies download hone aur indexing complete hone do.

## 5. Step 3 — Maven dependency samjho

Teacher: Spring AI Ollama starter kyun add kar rahe hain?

Student: Sir, kyunki hum Ollama ko AI model provider ke roop mein use karna chahte hain. Starter relevant integration aur auto-configuration provide karta hai.

Video mein dependency ka naam `spring-ai-starter-model-ollama` bataya gaya hai.

Maven configuration ka example:

```
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.springframework.ai</groupId>
    <artifactId>spring-ai-starter-model-ollama</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Agar aapke project mein Spring AI BOM ya dependency management configured nahi hai, to compatible version management bhi configure karna padega. Sirf dependency name add karna har project mein sufficient nahi hota.

Teacher: Kya hum purane OpenAI project mein bhi Ollama use kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan, compatible dependencies aur configuration ko update karke kar sakte hain. Video mein clarity ke liye ek separate project banaya gaya hai.

## 6. Step 4 — `application.properties` configuration

Teacher: Spring Boot ko kaise pata chalega ki Ollama server kahan run ho raha hai?

Student: Sir, hum configuration file mein Ollama ka base URL aur model name specify karenge.

File location:

```
src/main/resources/application.properties
```

Video ke configuration ka properties-file example:

```
server.port=8081

spring.ai.ollama.base-url=http://localhost:11434

spring.ai.ollama.chat.options.model=codellama:latest
```

### Har property ka meaning

| Property                              | Meaning                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `server.port=8081`                    | Spring Boot application port 8081 par run karegi. |
| `spring.ai.ollama.base-url`           | Ollama server ka base URL.                        |
| `spring.ai.ollama.chat.options.model` | Chat ke liye use hone wala model name.            |

Teacher: Ollama ka default port kya hai?

Student: Video ke according, Ollama ka default port `11434` hai.

```
http://localhost:11434
```

Browser mein ye URL open karke check kar sakte ho ki Ollama server respond kar raha hai ya nahi.

Important: Agar Spring Boot aur Ollama same computer par run ho rahe hain, to `localhost` use karna sahi hai. Agar Spring Boot Docker container ke andar run ho raha hai, to `localhost` container ko refer karega, host machine ko nahi. Us situation mein network ke according base URL change karna padega.

## 7. Step 5 — `ChatClient` aur `ChatClient.Builder`

Teacher: Spring AI mein `ChatClient` kya karta hai?

Student: `ChatClient` ek high-level API hai jiska use karke hum AI model ko prompt bhej sakte hain aur generated response receive kar sakte hain.

Teacher: `ChatClient.Builder` ka kya kaam hai?

Student: Ye `ChatClient` object create karne ke liye builder provide karta hai.

Simple Java example:

```
ChatClient chatClient = chatClientBuilder.build();
```

Ismein:

- `ChatClient.Builder` — object create karne ke liye builder.
- `build()` — `ChatClient` instance return karta hai.
- `ChatClient` — prompt execute karne aur response lene ke liye use hota hai.

### Important interview question

Teacher: Video mein `ChatClient.Builder` ko inject kiya gaya hai, lekin `ChatClient` ko direct inject kyun nahi kiya?

Student: Sir, video mein builder ko inject karke usse `ChatClient` create kiya gaya hai. Isse hum application mein apni required configuration ke according client build kar sakte hain.

Technically, `ChatClient` ko bhi bean ke roop mein configure karke inject kiya ja sakta hai. Isliye direct injection universally incorrect nahi hai.

## 8. Step 6 — Complete `ChatController.java`

Teacher: Ab user ke question ko AI model tak kaise bhejenge?

Student: Sir, ek REST controller banayenge. User query parameter mein question bhejega, aur controller Spring AI ke `ChatClient` se response generate karega.

File location:

```
src/main/java/com/spring/ollama/controller/ChatController.java
```

Complete example:

```
package com.spring.ollama.controller;

import org.springframework.ai.chat.client.ChatClient;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/chat")
public class ChatController {

    private final ChatClient chatClient;

    public ChatController(ChatClient.Builder chatClientBuilder) {
        this.chatClient = chatClientBuilder.build();
    }

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<String> chat(
            @RequestParam("q") String message) {

        String response = chatClient
                .prompt()
                .user(message)
                .call()
                .content();

        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }
}
```

### Code ko step-by-step samjho

1\. `@RestController`

Ye class ko REST controller banata hai. Iske methods ke return values HTTP response body mein serialize hote hain.

2\. `@RequestMapping("/chat")`

Ye controller ka base URL define karta hai.

3\. Constructor injection

```
public ChatController(ChatClient.Builder chatClientBuilder) {
    this.chatClient = chatClientBuilder.build();
}
```

Spring compatible builder bean inject karta hai aur hum usse client create karte hain.

4\. `@GetMapping`

Ye HTTP GET request handle karta hai.

5\. `@RequestParam("q")`

Ye URL se `q` naam ka query parameter read karta hai.

Example:

```
/chat?q=What%20is%20Java
```

Yahan `q` ki value `"What is Java"` hogi.

6\. Prompt execute karna

```
String response = chatClient
        .prompt()
        .user(message)
        .call()
        .content();
```

Is chain ka meaning:

- `prompt()` — prompt request start karta hai.
- `user(message)` — user ka question set karta hai.
- `call()` — model call execute karta hai.
- `content()` — generated response ka text return karta hai.

7\. Response return karna

```
return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
```

Ye HTTP `200 OK` ke saath AI ka answer return karta hai.

## 9. Step 7 — Application run karo

Teacher: Controller ready hai. Ab next step kya hai?

Student: Pehle confirm karunga ki Ollama server run ho raha hai aur selected model downloaded hai. Uske baad Spring Boot application run karunga.

Run karne se pehle checklist:

- Ollama installed hai.
- Ollama server running hai.
- `codellama:latest` model available hai.
- Maven dependencies resolve ho gayi hain.
- `application.properties` mein correct URL aur model name configured hai.
- Controller code compile ho raha hai.

Application run hone par expected port:

```
8081
```

Agar application start nahi hoti, to IntelliJ ke console mein Maven dependency, configuration aur connection errors check karo.

Student: Postman se API call karenge.

Request details

GET

`http://localhost:8081/chat?q=How%20are%20you`

Ab coding-related question bhi test kar sakte hain:

```

```
