# Spring AI Tutorial – Part 19: Managing Multiple Users with Chat Memory

Topic: Conversation ID, User-wise Chat History, `MessageWindowChatMemory`, `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository`, and `Advisor`

Language: Simple Hinglish (Hindi wali English)

Main goal: Ek hi Spring Boot AI application mein multiple users ki chat history ko alag-alag maintain karna seekhna.

## Module 1: Problem samjho — Multiple users ki chat history mix kyun hoti hai?

Teacher: Pichhle lecture mein humne kya seekha tha?

Student: Sir, humne Chat Memory implement ki thi. Iski help se AI previous messages yaad rakh sakta hai aur unke context mein answer de sakta hai.

Teacher: Bilkul! Lekin agar same application ko multiple users use karein, toh kya problem aa sakti hai?

Student: Agar sabhi users ki messages ek hi conversation history mein store ho rahi hain, toh ek user ki information doosre user ko mil sakti hai.

Teacher: Example se samjho.

User 1:

```
User: My name is Harsh.
AI: Hello Harsh! Nice to meet you.
```

Ab User 1 poochta hai:

```
User: What is my name?
AI: Your name is Harsh.
```

Ab User 2 application use karta hai.

```
User: My name is Ankit.
AI: Hello Ankit! Nice to meet you.
```

Agar conversation history properly separate nahi hai, toh User 1 dobara pooch sakta hai:

```
User: What is my name?
AI: Your name is Ankit.
```

Teacher: Kya ye correct behaviour hai?

Student: Nahi sir. Har user ki chat history separate honi chahiye.

Teacher: Exactly! Is problem ko solve karne ke liye hum Conversation ID ka use karenge.

## Module 2: `MessageWindowChatMemory` kya hai?

Teacher: `MessageWindowChatMemory` ka kya kaam hai?

Student: Ye chat history ko manage karta hai aur decide karta hai ki conversation ke kitne recent messages memory mein rakhe jayenge.

Teacher: Video mein default maximum messages kitne bataye gaye hain?

Student: Video mein default 20 messages bataye gaye hain.

Teacher: Kya iska matlab 20 user messages hi hain?

Student: Nahi sir. Chat history mein user messages aur AI ke assistant responses dono ho sakte hain.

Example:

```
1. User: My name is Harsh.
2. Assistant: Hello Harsh!
3. User: What is my name?
4. Assistant: Your name is Harsh.
```

Yahan total 4 messages hain.

Teacher: Purane messages ko limit karna kyun important hai?

Student: Kyunki previous conversation ka context dobara model ko bheja ja sakta hai. Bahut zyada messages bhejne se token usage aur cost badh sakti hai, aur context window bhi fill ho sakti hai.

Important points:

- Chat Memory: Conversation history manage karne ka concept/interface.
- MessageWindowChatMemory: Recent messages ko limited window mein manage karne wali implementation.
- Max messages: History window ki limit.
- Token consumption: Messages model ko bhejne par use hone wale tokens.

Note: Exact defaults aur behaviour Spring AI version ke hisaab se verify karna chahiye.

## Module 3: `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository` internally kaise kaam karta hai?

Teacher: Video mein `MessageWindowChatMemory` ke saath kaunsa repository use ho raha tha?

Student: `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository`.

Teacher: Ye data kahan store karta hai?

Student: Video ke according, ye data ko memory mein ek ConcurrentHashMap ke through store karta hai.

Conceptually, structure kuch aisa samjho:

```
Map<String, List<Message>>
```

Yahan:

- `String` = Conversation ID
- `List<Message>` = Us conversation ki messages list

Example:

```
ConcurrentHashMap
│
├── "harsh-user"
│     ├── User: My name is Harsh.
│     └── Assistant: Hello Harsh!
│
└── "ankit-user"
      ├── User: My name is Ankit.
      └── Assistant: Hello Ankit!
```

Teacher: Is map mein key aur value kya hain?

Student:

- Key: Conversation ID
- Value: Us conversation ke messages ki list

Teacher: `ConcurrentHashMap` kyun use kiya gaya hai?

Student: Ye concurrent access ko support karta hai, isliye multiple threads ke access ke liye useful hai. Lekin ye apne aap user authorization ya session isolation guarantee nahi karta. Uske liye sahi Conversation ID aur security zaroori hai.

Teacher: Kya `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository` permanent storage hai?

Student: Nahi sir. Ye temporary storage hai. Application restart hone par iska stored data normally chala jaata hai.

Example:

```
Application Start
       ↓
Chat messages memory mein store
       ↓
Application Stop / Restart
       ↓
Old in-memory messages lost
```

Revision: Multiple users ki history separate karne ke liye alag Conversation IDs; restart ke baad bhi data chahiye toh persistent repository, jaise supported JDBC-backed storage, use karna hoga.

## Module 4: Conversation ID kya hai?

Teacher: Ab main question — Conversation ID kya hoti hai?

Student: Conversation ID ek unique identifier hoti hai jo ek particular conversation ki history ko identify karne ke liye use hoti hai.

Teacher: Kya har user ke liye alag Conversation ID ho sakti hai?

Student: Haan sir. Video mein user ID ko Conversation ID ki tarah use kiya gaya hai.

Example:

| User  | Conversation ID |
| ----- | --------------- |
| Harsh | `harsh-user`    |
| Ankit | `ankit-user`    |
| Rahul | `rahul-user`    |

Isse repository mein alag-alag messages lists maintain ki ja sakti hain.

Teacher: Agar Harsh dobara message bhejta hai, toh uski purani history kaise milegi?

Student: Jab Harsh ki request ke saath same Conversation ID use hogi, toh chat memory usi conversation ki history retrieve kar sakegi.

Example:

```
Request 1
Conversation ID: harsh-user
Message: My name is Harsh.

Request 2
Conversation ID: harsh-user
Message: What is my name?

Result: Your name is Harsh.
```

Lekin:

```
Request 3
Conversation ID: ankit-user
Message: What is my name?

Result: Ankit ki apni history ke according answer.
```

Important difference: User ID aur Conversation ID conceptually same nahi hain. Video simplicity ke liye user ID ko Conversation ID bana raha hai. Ek user ki multiple separate chats honi chahiye, toh har chat ka alag Conversation ID rakh sakte hain.

## Module 5: Request Header se User ID kaise pass karte hain?

Teacher: Video mein Conversation ID application tak kaise pahunchayi gayi?

Student: HTTP request ke header mein user ID pass karke.

Example Postman request:

```
GET /chat?q=What is my name?
```

Header:

```
userId: harsh-user
```

Doosre user ke liye:

```
userId: ankit-user
```

Teacher: Spring Boot mein request header read karne ke liye kaunsa annotation use hota hai?

Student: `@RequestHeader`.

Example controller:

```
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/chat")
public class ChatController {

    private final ChatService chatService;

    public ChatController(ChatService chatService) {
        this.chatService = chatService;
    }

    @GetMapping
    public String chat(
            @RequestParam("q") String question,
            @RequestHeader("userId") String userId) {

        return chatService.chat(question, userId);
    }
}
```

### Code ko line-by-line samjho

1\. `@RestController`

Class ko REST API controller banata hai.

2\. `@RequestMapping("/chat")`

Controller ka base path `/chat` set karta hai.

3\. `@RequestParam("q")`

URL se question read karta hai.

Example:

```
/chat?q=What is my name?
```

4\. `@RequestHeader("userId")`

Request ke `userId` header se value read karta hai.

5\. `chatService.chat(question, userId)`

Question aur user identifier dono service ko pass karta hai, taaki service correct conversation context use kar sake.

Teacher: Kya is code mein har user ki history separate ho gayi?

Student: Abhi nahi sir. Header read karna sirf pehla step hai. Is identifier ko ChatClient ke memory Advisor tak bhi pahunchana hoga.

Production security warning: Real application mein client se aane wale `userId` header ko bina verify kiye trust nahi karna chahiye. Authenticated user ki identity Spring Security ya trusted backend context se leni chahiye. Warna user doosre user ki ID bhejkar uski chat history access karne ki koshish kar sakta hai.

## Module 6: Service mein Conversation ID kaise set karte hain?

Teacher: Controller se `userId` mil gaya. Ab next step kya hai?

Student: Service mein ChatClient call ke saath memory Advisor ko Conversation ID deni hai.

Video ka main concept hai:

```
.advisors(advisorSpec ->
    advisorSpec.param(
        "chat_memory_conversation_id",
        userId
    )
)
```

Yahan `chat_memory_conversation_id` Spring AI ke memory Advisor ka parameter key hai. Ye string video mein hard-code ki gayi hai; supported constant available ho toh apne Spring AI version ke according use kar sakte hain.

Example service:

```
@Service
public class ChatService {

    private final ChatClient chatClient;

    public ChatService(ChatClient chatClient) {
        this.chatClient = chatClient;
    }

    public String chat(String question, String userId) {

        return chatClient.prompt()
                .user(question)
                .advisors(advisorSpec ->
                    advisorSpec.param(
                        "chat_memory_conversation_id",
                        userId
                    )
                )
                .call()
                .content();
    }
}
```

### Ab poora code samjho

Step 1 — `chatClient.prompt()`

AI request banane ke liye fluent API start hoti hai.

Step 2 — `.user(question)`

Current user ka question prompt mein add hota hai.

Step 3 — `.advisors(...)`

Memory Advisor ko current request ke liye configure karte hain.

Step 4 — `.param(...)`

Advisor ko Conversation ID provide karte hain. Is example mein `userId` ki value Conversation ID ke roop mein use ho rahi hai.

Step 5 — `.call()`

Non-streaming model call execute hoti hai.

Step 6 — `.content()`

AI ka text response return hota hai.

Note: Is service example ke work karne ke liye ChatClient par `MessageChatMemoryAdvisor` pehle se configured hona chahiye, jaise previous lecture mein kiya tha. Actual Advisor API signatures Spring AI version ke hisaab se vary kar sakte hain.

## Module 7: End-to-end flow samjho

Teacher: Ab batao, request aane se lekar AI response milne tak kya hota hai?

Student: Flow kuch aisa hota hai:

1\. User / Postman

Question + trusted user/conversation identifier

2\. Spring Boot Controller

Question aur identifier read karta hai

3\. Chat Service + ChatClient

Question ke saath Conversation ID Advisor ko pass karta hai

4\. MessageChatMemoryAdvisor

Us Conversation ID ki history retrieve karta hai aur request ke context mein use karta hai

5\. LLM / AI Model

Current question aur available conversation context ke basis par response generate karta hai

6\. Memory Update + Response

Configured memory mechanism conversation messages maintain karta hai; response user ko milta hai

Teacher: Kya humein khud `ConcurrentHashMap` create karke messages store karne padenge?

Student: Nahi sir. Spring AI ki provided memory implementation aur repository ye kaam manage karti hain. Humein correct configuration aur Conversation ID pass karni hoti hai.

## Module 8: Postman mein practical testing

Video mein do users ko test karke verify kiya gaya tha ki unki histories separate hain.

### Test 1 — Harsh

Request header:

```
userId: harsh-user
```

Message:

```
My name is Harsh.
```

Expected response:

```
Hello Harsh! Nice to meet you.
```

Ab same header ke saath next request:

```
What is my name?
```

Expected answer:

```
Your name is Harsh.
```

### Test 2 — Ankit

Ab header change karo:

```
userId: ankit-user
```

Message:

```
My name is Ankit.
```

Ab same header ke saath poochho:

```
What is my name?
```

Expected answer:

```
Your name is Ankit.
```

### Test 3 — Dono histories ko dobara verify karo

| Header       | Question         | Expected answer |
| ------------ | ---------------- | --------------- |
| `harsh-user` | What is my name? | Harsh           |
| `ankit-user` | What is my name? | Ankit           |

Teacher: Is practical se kya prove hua?

Student: Har request mein same identifier use karne par us conversation ki history alag maintain ho sakti hai. Do different identifiers use karne par histories ek doosre ke saath mix nahi honi chahiye.

Actual output model response, configured memory, aur request mein sahi ID pass hone par depend karega.

## Module 9: Debugger se memory kaise verify karte hain?

Teacher: Video mein debugger ka use kyun kiya gaya?

Student: Internally check karne ke liye ki messages repository mein kaise store ho rahe hain.

Video mein `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository` ke internal store ko inspect kiya gaya. Conceptually structure aisa dikhta hai:

```
chatMemoryStore
│
├── Key: harsh-user
│     └── List<Message>
│           ├── UserMessage: My name is Harsh.
│           ├── AssistantMessage: Hello Harsh!
│           ├── UserMessage: What is my name?
│           └── AssistantMessage: Your name is Harsh.
│
└── Key: ankit-user
      └── List<Message>
            ├── UserMessage: My name is Ankit.
            └── AssistantMessage: Hello Ankit!
```

Teacher: `UserMessage` aur `AssistantMessage` kya hain?

Student:

- UserMessage: User ne jo message send kiya.
- AssistantMessage: AI assistant ne jo response diya.

Agar user do questions poochta hai aur AI dono ke answers deta hai, toh conversation history mein inke corresponding messages store ho sakte hain.

Teacher: Debugger mein map ki size `2` dikhne ka kya matlab hai?

Student: Agar map mein do distinct Conversation IDs hain, toh map mein do keys ho sakti hain — ek Harsh ke liye aur ek Ankit ke liye.

Dhyan rahe: Map ki size conversation keys count karti hai, total messages nahi.

## Module 10: Is approach ki limitation kya hai?

Teacher: Kya ab humari saari problems solve ho gayi hain?

Student: Nahi sir. User-wise session separation ho gayi, lekin storage abhi bhi in-memory hai.

Teacher: Iska kya disadvantage hai?

Student: Application restart hone par `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository` ka data normally lost ho jaata hai.

Example:

```
Application running
      ↓
Harsh aur Ankit ki histories stored
      ↓
Application restart
      ↓
In-memory histories lost
```

Teacher: Permanent history ke liye kya karna hoga?

Student: Persistent storage use karna hoga, jaise Spring AI version aur configuration ke hisaab se supported JDBC-backed Chat Memory Repository.

Isse history ko application ke restart ke baad bhi preserve kiya ja sakta hai, provided database persistence properly configured ho.

Teacher: Kya database use karne se token usage problem bhi solve ho jaati hai?

Student: Nahi sir. Storage persistent ho jaata hai, lekin model ko bheje jaane wale context ki length phir bhi manage karni padti hai. Isi liye message window aur token usage par dhyan dena zaroori hai.

## Module 11: Interview Questions and Answers

Q1. What is Conversation ID in Spring AI?

Answer: Conversation ID is an identifier used by chat memory to retrieve and maintain the message history for a particular conversation. Different IDs allow different conversation histories to be maintained separately.

Q2. Why do we need separate Conversation IDs for multiple users?

Answer: Without separate conversation identifiers, users may share the same memory context. Using the correct ID helps ensure each user's conversation history is retrieved separately.

Q3. What is `MessageWindowChatMemory`?

Answer: It is a Spring AI chat memory implementation that manages a limited window of conversation messages. Limiting history can help control token consumption.

Q4. What is `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository`?

Answer: It stores chat memory data in application memory. The video describes its internal storage using a `ConcurrentHashMap` keyed by Conversation ID.

Q5. What is the role of `ConcurrentHashMap` in the video?

Answer: The map uses a Conversation ID as the key and the corresponding message list as the value, allowing different conversation histories to be stored under different keys.

Q6. How do you read a user identifier from an HTTP header in Spring Boot?

Answer: We can use the `@RequestHeader` annotation to read a header value and pass it from the controller to the service.

Q7. How is Conversation ID passed to the memory Advisor?

Answer: In the video's example, the ChatClient Advisor configuration uses the parameter key `chat_memory_conversation_id` and passes the selected conversation identifier as its value.

Q8. What is the difference between User ID and Conversation ID?

Answer: A User ID identifies a user, while a Conversation ID identifies a conversation. One user can have multiple conversations, so the two identifiers do not always have to be the same.

Q9. Does in-memory chat history survive an application restart?

Answer: Normally, no. In-memory data is temporary. A persistent repository is needed when conversation history must survive restarts.

Q10. Why should we limit chat history?

Answer: Sending too many previous messages can increase token usage and cost, and can consume the model's context window.

Q11. Is using a different Conversation ID enough to guarantee security?

Answer: No. The application must also authenticate users and authorize access to conversations. A client-supplied identifier must not be trusted without verification.

Q12. How would you maintain multiple conversations for one user?

Answer: I would use a separate Conversation ID for each chat session and associate each conversation with the authenticated user. This allows separate histories for the same user.

## Module 12: Quick Revision Notes

- Chat Memory: Maintains conversation context across requests.
- MessageWindowChatMemory: Manages a limited window of messages.
- InMemoryChatMemoryRepository: Stores memory temporarily in application memory.
- ConcurrentHashMap: Conceptually maps Conversation IDs to message lists in the implementation described by the video.
- Conversation ID: Identifies which conversation history should be used.
- `@RequestHeader`: Reads an HTTP header in a Spring Boot controller.
- `chat_memory_conversation_id`: The Advisor parameter key used in the video.
- MessageChatMemoryAdvisor: Connects configured chat memory with the ChatClient request flow.
- Different IDs: Help separate different conversation histories.
- Persistent repository: Needed when history must survive application restarts.
- Security: Derive or validate user identity through trusted authentication; don't blindly trust client-provided IDs.
- Token management: Keep history within a reasonable limit to control token usage.

Final concept yaad rakho:

```
Same Conversation ID
        ↓
Same conversation history

Different Conversation IDs
        ↓
Separate conversation histories

InMemoryChatMemoryRepository
        ↓
Temporary storage

Persistent Chat Memory Repository
        ↓
History can survive restarts when correctly configured
```

Next part ka connection: Is lecture mein user-wise conversation separation solve hui. Video ke end mein next topic hai database-backed chat memory, taaki application restart hone par purani chat history delete na ho.
========================================================================================================================
# Spring AI Tutorial – Part 20: Database-Backed Chat Memory with JDBC

Is video ka main topic hai Spring AI mein chat history ko database mein permanently store karna, taaki application restart hone ke baad bhi AI purani conversation ka context use kar sake.

Notes video ke transcript par based hain. Main concepts ko beginner level se explain karunga, practical code aur request flow ke saath. Jahan transcript mein exact code ya API details available nahi hain, wahan unhe clearly identify karunga.

## 1. Sabse pehle problem samjho

Teacher: Chat Memory kya hoti hai?

Student: Chat Memory ka matlab hai user aur AI ke beech hui purani conversation ko yaad rakhna, taaki AI agle question ka jawab purani baaton ko dhyan mein rakhkar de sake.

Example:

QUESTION 1

User: My name is Ankit.

AI: Nice to meet you, Ankit!

QUESTION 2

User: What is my name?

AI: Your name is Ankit.

AI tabhi sahi jawab de payega jab uske paas pehle wali conversation ka relevant context available ho.

Teacher: Kya AI automatically har purani API request yaad rakhta hai?

Student: Nahi. Generally, har nayi model request mein required conversation history ya context provide karna padta hai. Spring AI ki Chat Memory facility is process ko manage karne mein help karti hai.

Teacher: Pichhle implementation mein problem kya thi?

Student: Hum `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository` use kar rahe the. Ismein messages application ki RAM mein store hote hain. Application restart hone par ye data normally lost ho jata hai.

Teacher: Iska solution kya hai?

Student: Conversation history ko database mein store karna, jaise MySQL ya PostgreSQL. Iske liye video mein `JdbcChatMemoryRepository` ka use dikhaya gaya hai.

## 2. In-Memory vs Database-Backed Chat Memory

| Feature                    | In-Memory                         | Database-Backed JDBC                  |
| -------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------- |
| Data kahan store hota hai? | Application RAM mein              | Relational database mein              |
| Restart ke baad history    | Normally lost                     | Database data available reh sakta hai |
| Setup                      | Simple                            | Database aur configuration chahiye    |
| Suitable use               | Learning, temporary data, testing | Persistent chat history               |
| Example                    | `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository`    | `JdbcChatMemoryRepository`            |

Teacher: Kya database use karne ka matlab AI ab automatically sab kuch yaad rakhega?

Student: Nahi. Database purane messages store karta hai. Chat Memory aur uska Advisor relevant history ko conversation ke context mein model request ke saath provide karte hain. Model ka jawab ab bhi diye gaye context par depend karta hai.

## 3. Chat Memory ke important components

Teacher: JDBC implementation samajhne se pehle kaun se components samajhna zaroori hai?

Student: Chaar main concepts samajhna zaroori hai.

### 3.1 `ChatMemory`

Teacher: `ChatMemory` kya hai?

Student: Ye Spring AI ka abstraction hai jo conversation messages ki memory manage karne ke liye use hota hai.

Simple example: Socho humein decide karna hai ki conversation ke kitne purane messages AI ko bhejne hain. Ye memory strategy ka part hai.

### 3.2 `MessageWindowChatMemory`

Teacher: `MessageWindowChatMemory` ka kya role hai?

Student: Ye memory ke liye ek window strategy provide karta hai. Iska matlab hai ki conversation ke kitne recent messages maintain ya context ke liye use karne hain.

Video mein instructor 20 messages ki default window ka zikr karta hai aur baad mein use 10 messages karne ka example deta hai.

Important: Message window ka size aur database mein physically kitne records stored hain, ye do alag cheezein hain. Window chhoti karne ka matlab zaroori nahi ki database ki saari purani rows permanently delete ho jaayen. Exact retention behaviour implementation aur version par depend kar sakta hai.

### 3.3 `ChatMemoryRepository`

Teacher: Repository ka simple meaning kya hai?

Student: Repository ek component ya interface hai jo data ko save karne, retrieve karne aur delete karne jaise storage operations define karta hai.

`ChatMemoryRepository` Spring AI mein chat messages ki storage ke liye abstraction hai. Iske different implementations alag storage systems use kar sakte hain.

### 3.4 `JdbcChatMemoryRepository`

Teacher: JDBC ka full form kya hai?

Student: Java Database Connectivity (JDBC). Ye Java application ko database ke saath communicate karne mein help karta hai.

`JdbcChatMemoryRepository` chat memory repository ki JDBC-based implementation hai. Video mein ise relational database mein chat messages store karne ke liye use kiya gaya hai.

Simple flow:

`ChatMemory → ChatMemoryRepository → JDBC implementation → Database`

Is flow mein `ChatMemory` memory strategy manage karta hai aur repository storage ka kaam karti hai.

## 4. JDBC Chat Memory kaise kaam karti hai?

Teacher: Abhi tak humne concepts samjhe. Lekin complete working flow kya hai?

Student: Jab user question bhejta hai, Spring AI relevant conversation history repository se retrieve kar sakta hai. Chat Memory Advisor us history ko model request mein include karne mein help karta hai. Model response generate karta hai, aur conversation ke messages repository mein save hote hain.

1\. User

Question bhejta hai

2\. Controller / Service

Question aur conversation ID handle karte hain

3\. ChatMemory Advisor

Relevant history ko manage karta hai

4\. ChatMemoryRepository

Database se messages read/save karta hai

5\. MySQL Database

Conversation records persist karta hai

Ye conceptual flow hai. Actual execution order aur save/retrieve operations Spring AI ki version aur Advisor implementation ke hisaab se vary kar sakte hain.

Teacher: `conversationId` itna important kyun hai?

Student: Kyunki database mein ek saath bahut saare users ki conversations ho sakti hain. `conversationId` batata hai ki kaun se messages ek conversation se related hain.

Example:

- Conversation `user-101` → Ankit ki history.
- Conversation `user-202` → Harsh ki history.

Agar har request mein sahi conversation ID pass hoti hai, to application correct history retrieve kar sakti hai.

Important security point: Production application mein sirf client se aayi ID par trust mat karo. Authenticated user ko us conversation ka access hai ya nahi, ye server ko verify karna chahiye.

## 5. Project setup: kaun si dependencies chahiye?

Teacher: Video mein kaun si main dependencies use ki gayi hain?

Student: Do important dependencies hain:

1. Spring AI JDBC Chat Memory Repository starter.
2. MySQL JDBC driver.

Pehli dependency Spring AI ki JDBC repository implementation provide karti hai. Doosri dependency Java application ko MySQL database se connect karne mein help karti hai.

Video mein instructor Maven project ke `pom.xml` mein dependencies add karta hai.

Illustrative Maven configuration:

```
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.springframework.ai</groupId>
    <artifactId>spring-ai-starter-model-chat-memory-repository-jdbc</artifactId>
</dependency>

<dependency>
    <groupId>com.mysql</groupId>
    <artifactId>mysql-connector-j</artifactId>
    <scope>runtime</scope>
</dependency>
```

Teacher: Kya ye dependency versions apne aap add kar sakte hain?

Student: Ye project ke Spring AI BOM aur dependency management par depend karta hai. Agar BOM configured hai, to compatible versions manage ho sakte hain. Agar nahi hai, to required versions explicitly specify karne pad sakte hain.

Note: Transcript mein dependency ka naam isi tarah diya gaya hai. Apne project ki Spring AI version ke liye exact artifact name official documentation se verify karna chahiye, kyunki starter names version ke saath change ho sakte hain.

## 6. MySQL database configure kaise karte hain?

Teacher: Dependency add karne ke baad kya karna hai?

Student: MySQL server run hona chahiye. Phir application ko database URL, username aur password provide karna hota hai.

Video mein instructor MySQL Workbench open karke ek schema create karta hai, jiska naam `spring_ai_yt` hai.

Schema ko simple language mein database ke andar ek logical container samajh sakte ho, jisme tables aur related database objects rakhe jaate hain.

Example `application.properties`:

```
server.port=8082

spring.datasource.url=jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/spring_ai_yt
spring.datasource.username=root
spring.datasource.password=${DB_PASSWORD}
```

Code explanation:

- `server.port=8082`: Spring Boot application port `8082` par run karegi.
- `spring.datasource.url`: Batata hai ki database kahan hai aur kaunsa database use karna hai.
- `localhost`: Database isi machine par run ho raha hai.
- `3306`: MySQL ka commonly used default port.
- `spring_ai_yt`: Database ka naam.
- `spring.datasource.username`: Database login username.
- `spring.datasource.password`: Environment variable se password read kiya ja raha hai.

`DB_PASSWORD` environment variable set karna zaroori hai. Apna real database password source code ya public repository mein commit mat karna.

Teacher: Agar main PostgreSQL use kar raha hoon to kya karun?

Student: PostgreSQL ke liye PostgreSQL JDBC driver aur uske according datasource URL aur credentials use karne honge. Saath hi check karo ki tumhari Spring AI version ka JDBC memory repository us database aur uske dialect ko support karti hai ya nahi.

Video mein PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB, SQL Server aur HSQLDB jaise supported database/dialect options ka zikr hai. Current compatibility ko documentation se verify karna chahiye.

## 7. Database table automatically create kaise hoti hai?

Teacher: Instructor ne application run ki, lekin database mein table nahi bani. Aisa kyun hua?

Student: JDBC connection establish hone aur required table initialize hone mein difference hai. Database connection successful hone ka matlab ye nahi ki chat memory ki table bhi automatically create ho gayi.

Video mein instructor documentation se schema initialization property check karta hai aur phir application configuration update karta hai.

Illustrative property:

```
spring.ai.chat.memory.repository.jdbc.initialize-schema=always
```

Is property ka purpose JDBC chat memory repository ke schema initialization ko control karna hai. Exact property name aur accepted values apne Spring AI version ki documentation se confirm karo.

Teacher: Schema initialization kya hota hai?

Student: Schema initialization ka matlab required database structure, jaise table aur columns, create ya initialize karna hai.

Teacher: Iske baad kya hua?

Student: Instructor ne application dobara run ki aur MySQL Workbench refresh kiya. Ab chat memory ki table dikhne lagi.

Video mein table ka naam `SPRING_AI_CHAT_MEMORY` dikhaya gaya hai. Exact table name aur columns implementation/version ke hisaab se vary kar sakte hain.

Production caution: `initialize-schema=always` development aur learning ke liye convenient ho sakta hai. Production mein schema changes ko controlled migrations ke through manage karna zyada appropriate ho sakta hai. Application ko database tables modify karne ki permission bhi carefully manage karo.

## 8. Database table ke andar kya store hota hai?

Teacher: Jab user AI ko message bhejta hai, database mein kya save hota hai?

Student: Video mein instructor table ke columns dikhata hai:

- `conversation_id`
- `content`
- `type`
- `timestamp`

Inka simple meaning samjho:

| Column            | Meaning                             |
| ----------------- | ----------------------------------- |
| `conversation_id` | Kis conversation ka message hai     |
| `content`         | Message ka text                     |
| `type`            | Message kis role/type ka hai        |
| `timestamp`       | Message se related time information |

Ye video mein dikhaye gaye fields ka conceptual explanation hai. Exact schema mein additional fields ya different data types ho sakte hain.

Teacher: `type` column ki kya importance hai?

Student: Application ko samajhna hota hai ki message user ne bheja tha ya AI ne generate kiya tha.

Example:

| conversation_id | content                 | type      |
| --------------- | ----------------------- | --------- |
| `user-101`      | My name is Ankit        | user      |
| `user-101`      | Nice to meet you, Ankit | assistant |

Ye sirf samjhane ke liye simplified example hai, actual database dump nahi.

Agar roles aur message order sahi maintain nahi honge, to conversation history ko reconstruct karna mushkil ho sakta hai.

## 9. Practical testing: kya application sach mein memory use kar rahi hai?

Teacher: Database-backed Chat Memory ko kaise test karenge?

Student: Video mein instructor user ke messages bhejta hai aur phir database mein records check karta hai.

### Test 1: User ka naam store karna

Request:

```
My name is Ankit.
```

Expected AI response, example:

```
Nice to meet you, Ankit!
```

Database mein conversation ke user aur assistant messages store hone chahiye, agar memory integration correctly configured hai.

### Test 2: Naam yaad hai ya nahi?

Same conversation ID ke saath doosri request:

```
What is my name?
```

Expected answer:

```
Your name is Ankit.
```

### Test 3: Application restart karna

1. Conversation ID `user-101` ke saath naam bhejo.
2. Database mein messages verify karo.
3. Spring Boot application stop karo.
4. Application dobara start karo.
5. Same conversation ID ke saath poochho: `What is my name?`

Agar database-backed repository aur memory Advisor correctly configured hain, to purane stored messages retrieve karke AI answer de sakta hai.

Important: Restart ke baad successful answer aana persistence test ka useful evidence hai. Lekin ye test tabhi meaningful hai jab request mein same conversation ID use ho aur relevant history model ko provide ki ja rahi ho.

## 10. `maxMessages(20)` ka kya meaning hai?

Teacher: Video mein 20 messages ki baat kyun ho rahi hai?

Student: Chat Memory ko bounded window dene se model request mein unnecessary old history bhejne se bach sakte hain. Isse context window par pressure aur token usage kam ho sakta hai.

Example configuration:

```
MessageWindowChatMemory.builder()
    .chatMemoryRepository(chatMemoryRepository)
    .maxMessages(20)
    .build();
```

Is code ka purpose hai memory ko repository ke saath configure karna aur window size ko 20 messages set karna.

Agar application mein 10 messages ki window chahiye, to video mein dikhaya gaya approach:

```
MessageWindowChatMemory.builder()
    .chatMemoryRepository(chatMemoryRepository)
    .maxMessages(10)
    .build();
```

Teacher: Kya `maxMessages(10)` ka matlab database mein maximum 10 rows hi hongi?

Student: Zaroori nahi. Ye distinction bahut important hai:

- Memory window: Kitne messages memory context ke liye maintain/use kiye jaate hain.
- Database retention: Database mein kitne records physically rakhe jaate hain.

In dono ka behaviour repository aur Spring AI implementation par depend karta hai. Sirf window size ko 10 karne se assume mat karo ki database ki saari purani rows delete ho jayengi.

Teacher: Token usage par kya effect ho sakta hai?

Student: Agar baaki sab conditions same hon, to kam conversation history bhejne se prompt tokens kam ho sakte hain. Lekin history bahut chhoti karne se AI important purani baatein bhool bhi sakta hai.

Isliye window size ko application ke use case ke hisaab se choose karna chahiye.

## 11. Apna `ChatMemory` bean kaise banayein?

Teacher: Agar default configuration ke badle humein apni memory window set karni hai to kya karenge?

Student: Video mein instructor Spring configuration class mein `ChatMemory` bean create karta hai aur JDBC repository inject karta hai.

Representative example:

```
@Configuration
public class ChatMemoryConfig {

    @Bean
    public ChatMemory chatMemory(
            ChatMemoryRepository chatMemoryRepository) {

        return MessageWindowChatMemory.builder()
                .chatMemoryRepository(chatMemoryRepository)
                .maxMessages(10)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Line-by-line explanation:

1. `@Configuration` Spring ko batata hai ki class configuration ke liye hai.
2. `@Bean` method ke returned object ko Spring container mein register karne ke liye use hota hai.
3. `ChatMemoryRepository` method parameter hai. Spring compatible repository bean available hone par use inject kar sakta hai.
4. `MessageWindowChatMemory.builder()` memory configuration build karna start karta hai.
5. `.chatMemoryRepository(chatMemoryRepository)` batata hai ki storage ke liye kaunsi repository use karni hai.
6. `.maxMessages(10)` window size set karta hai.
7. `.build()` configured `ChatMemory` object return karta hai.

Teacher: Is code se JDBC repository automatically mil jayegi?

Student: Tabhi jab required JDBC starter, compatible configuration, datasource aur auto-configuration properly set up hon. Sirf method parameter likhne se har project mein repository bean automatically available nahi ho jaati.

Version note: Ye representative code hai. Builder methods aur configuration APIs tumhari Spring AI version ke according verify karni chahiye.

## 12. ChatClient mein Chat Memory Advisor kaise use hota hai?

Teacher: `ChatMemory` bean banane ke baad AI request ko purani history kaise milti hai?

Student: ChatClient configuration mein Chat Memory Advisor use kiya ja sakta hai.

Representative example:

```
@Configuration
public class ChatClientConfig {

    @Bean
    public ChatClient chatClient(
            ChatClient.Builder builder,
            ChatMemory chatMemory) {

        MessageChatMemoryAdvisor memoryAdvisor =
                MessageChatMemoryAdvisor.builder(chatMemory)
                        .build();

        return builder
                .defaultAdvisors(memoryAdvisor)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Explanation:

- `ChatClient.Builder` ChatClient configure karne ke liye use hota hai.
- `ChatMemory` bean memory strategy aur repository ko use karti hai.
- `MessageChatMemoryAdvisor` conversation messages ko model interaction ke saath integrate karta hai.
- `.defaultAdvisors(memoryAdvisor)` is Advisor ko client ke default Advisor setup mein add karta hai.
- `.build()` configured ChatClient return karta hai.

Agar tumhare existing project mein doosre Advisors bhi hain, to unhe remove mat karna. Sabhi required Advisors ko compatible configuration ke saath preserve karo.

Teacher: Advisor ki zaroorat kyun hai?

Student: Database mein history save karna ek kaam hai. Us history ko correct conversation ke saath model request mein include karna doosra kaam hai. Memory Advisor is integration ko manage karne mein help karta hai.

Important: Ye configuration example concept samjhane ke liye hai. Exact API aur defaults Spring AI version ke hisaab se verify karna zaroori hai.

## 13. Conversation ID aur multiple users

Teacher: Agar do users same application use kar rahe hain to unki memory alag kaise rahegi?

Student: Har conversation ke liye correct, distinct conversation ID use karna zaroori hai.

Example:

- Ankit: `conversation-101`
- Harsh: `conversation-202`

Ankit ne kaha:

```
My company name is ABC Technologies.
```

Harsh ne kaha:

```
My company name is XYZ Solutions.
```

Agar dono conversations correctly isolate ki gayi hain, to Ankit ke question par Harsh ki company history use nahi honi chahiye.

Video mein instructor different IDs ke saath messages store hote hue dikhata hai.

Teacher: Kya sirf different IDs bana dena sufficient security hai?

Student: Nahi. Application ko verify karna hoga ki authenticated user us conversation ID ka owner hai ya usko access karne ki permission hai. Arbitrary ID accept karne se data leakage ka risk ho sakta hai.

## 14. Agar database ka support nahi milta to kya karein?

Teacher: Video mein custom `ChatMemoryRepository` implement karne ka bhi zikr hai. Kab zaroorat padti hai?

Student: Agar tumhara required storage system supported built-in repository implementation provide nahi karta, to custom implementation ki zaroorat ho sakti hai.

Conceptual example:

```
public class MyChatMemoryRepository
        implements ChatMemoryRepository {

    // Required repository methods
    // Implement according to the Spring AI version
}
```

Ye sirf conceptual skeleton hai, complete runnable implementation nahi.

Video mein JDBC implementation ko reference ke roop mein dekhkar custom repository banane ka suggestion hai.

Transcript mein `findByConversationId`, `saveAll` aur `deleteByConversationId` jaise operations ka zikr hai. Exact interface methods aur signatures apni Spring AI version ke source/documentation se confirm karna chahiye.

Custom implementation mein tumhe correctly handle karna hoga:

- Messages ko save karna.
- Conversation ID ke according messages retrieve karna.
- Required deletion operations.
- Message roles aur ordering.
- Concurrent requests aur database errors.
- Data access aur user isolation.

Teacher: Kya custom repository har baar banana zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi. Agar built-in JDBC repository tumhare database aur use case ko support karti hai, to custom implementation likhne ki zaroorat nahi ho sakti.

## 15. Custom schema kab chahiye?

Teacher: Video mein custom schema file ka zikr kyun aata hai?

Student: Kuch databases ke liye Spring AI required schema automatically initialize kar sakta hai. Agar database/dialect ke liye built-in schema support available nahi hai, to custom schema provide karne ki zaroorat pad sakti hai.

Conceptual steps:

1. Database ke liye required table structure define karo.
2. SQL schema file create karo.
3. Application configuration mein us schema file ka path set karo.
4. Verify karo ki SQL statements tumhare database ke syntax ke compatible hain.
5. Application start karke table creation aur message storage test karo.

Video mein custom schema file ke liye `spring.ai.chat.memory.repository.jdbc.schema` property ka zikr hai. Is property ka exact naam, supported values aur file path format tumhari Spring AI version ke official documentation se verify karo.

Important: Transcript custom database support ka concept samjhata hai, lekin har database ke liye complete custom SQL schema nahi deta. Isliye kisi specific unsupported database ke liye SQL invent karke video ka original code mat samajhna.

## 16. Common mistakes aur troubleshooting

Teacher: Agar application run ho rahi hai lekin chat memory database mein store nahi ho rahi, to kya check karna chahiye?

Student: Ye checklist follow karo:

1. Dependencies: JDBC chat memory starter aur database driver sahi hain?
2. Datasource: Database URL, username aur password correct hain?
3. Database availability: Database server running hai?
4. Schema: Required table create hui hai?
5. Initialization: Schema initialization configuration sahi hai?
6. Repository: Expected JDBC repository bean use ho rahi hai?
7. Memory configuration: `ChatMemory` bean correct repository use kar rahi hai?
8. Advisor: ChatClient mein required memory Advisor configured hai?
9. Conversation ID: Same conversation ke requests mein expected ID pass ho rahi hai?
10. Window size: Required history memory window mein available hai?
11. Logs: Application logs mein database, SQL ya bean configuration errors hain?

Teacher: Agar restart ke baad AI naam bhool gaya to iska matlab database persistence fail hai?

Student: Zaroori nahi. Database records check karo, phir verify karo ki request same conversation ID use kar rahi hai aur Advisor relevant history model ko provide kar raha hai. Problem persistence, configuration, conversation ID ya context retrieval mein se kahin bhi ho sakti hai.

## 17. Important concepts ka comparison

| Concept                        | Main responsibility                                                  |
| ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ChatMemory`                   | Conversation memory manage karna                                     |
| `MessageWindowChatMemory`      | Memory window strategy define karna                                  |
| `ChatMemoryRepository`         | Message storage ka contract define karna                             |
| `InMemoryChatMemoryRepository` | RAM mein messages store karna                                        |
| `JdbcChatMemoryRepository`     | JDBC ke through relational database mein messages store karna        |
| `MessageChatMemoryAdvisor`     | Chat Memory ko ChatClient/model interaction ke saath integrate karna |
| `conversationId`               | Conversation ke messages identify karna                              |

Teacher: In sab ko ek saath kaise yaad rakhein?

Student: Is simple analogy se yaad rakho:

- `ChatMemory` = memory manage karne ka system.
- `MessageWindowChatMemory` = kitni recent history use karni hai, uski strategy.
- `ChatMemoryRepository` = storage ke liye contract.
- `JdbcChatMemoryRepository` = database storage implementation.
- `MessageChatMemoryAdvisor` = model interaction ke saath history integrate karne wala component.
- `conversationId` = kis conversation ki history chahiye, uski pehchaan.

## 18. Interview Questions and Answers

Q1. What is Chat Memory in Spring AI?

Answer: Chat Memory manages conversation messages so that relevant previous interactions can be included in subsequent AI requests.

Q2. Why use JDBC-based chat memory?

Answer: JDBC-based chat memory stores conversation messages in a relational database, allowing them to remain available across application restarts when persistence is configured correctly.

Q3. What is the difference between `ChatMemory` and `ChatMemoryRepository`?

Answer: `ChatMemory` manages the conversation memory strategy, while `ChatMemoryRepository` defines the operations used to store and retrieve conversation messages.

Q4. What is `JdbcChatMemoryRepository`?

Answer: It is a JDBC-based implementation of the chat memory repository abstraction that stores messages in a supported relational database.

Q5. What is the role of `MessageWindowChatMemory`?

Answer: It provides a bounded message-window strategy for conversation memory. Its configuration affects how much recent history is maintained or used, depending on the implementation.

Q6. Does database-backed memory mean the AI automatically remembers every old message?

Answer: No. The database stores messages, but the application must retrieve and provide relevant conversation history to the model.

Q7. What is a conversation ID?

Answer: A conversation ID identifies a conversation so that the application can retrieve the corresponding message history.

Q8. What is the difference between in-memory and JDBC chat memory?

Answer: In-memory chat memory stores data in application memory and normally loses it on restart. JDBC chat memory stores data in a relational database and can preserve it across restarts.

Q9. Why is schema initialization required?

Answer: Schema initialization creates the database structures required by the repository, such as its message table, when automatic initialization is configured and supported.

Q10. Does `maxMessages(10)` guarantee only 10 database rows remain?

Answer: No. It configures the memory window size. Physical database retention depends on the repository implementation and its deletion/retention behaviour.

Q11. Why might an AI forget a previous answer after an application restart?

Answer: The history might not have been persisted, the wrong conversation ID may have been used, or the memory Advisor may not be supplying the required history to the model.

Q12. When would you implement a custom `ChatMemoryRepository`?

Answer: When the required storage system or custom data model is not adequately supported by an available repository implementation.

Q13. Why should conversation IDs be secured?

Answer: To prevent one user from accessing another user's conversation history. The server should authorize access to each conversation.

Q14. Why should we limit conversation history?

Answer: A bounded history can reduce prompt size and token usage, but the window must be large enough to preserve the context required by the application.

Q15. What is the role of a Chat Memory Advisor?

Answer: It integrates conversation history with ChatClient interactions so that relevant messages can be used during model calls.

## 19. Final Quick Revision

- Problem: In-memory conversation history is normally lost when the application restarts.
- Solution: Store chat messages in a database using a supported repository implementation.
- `ChatMemory`: Manages conversation memory.
- `MessageWindowChatMemory`: Defines the bounded memory-window strategy.
- `ChatMemoryRepository`: Storage abstraction.
- `JdbcChatMemoryRepository`: JDBC-based relational database implementation.
- `MessageChatMemoryAdvisor`: Connects chat memory with ChatClient interactions.
- `conversationId`: Identifies the conversation whose history is needed.
- Datasource configuration: Connects Spring Boot to the database.
- Schema initialization: Creates the required database structure when configured and supported.
- `maxMessages(10)`: Configures the memory window; it does not automatically guarantee database retention of only 10 rows.
- Custom repository: Useful when existing implementations do not meet storage requirements.
- Security: Authorize access to conversations and protect database credentials.
- Version compatibility: Confirm dependency names, configuration properties and method signatures for the project's Spring AI version.

## 20. Complete Concept Connection Flow

```
User sends a question
        |
        v
Controller / Service
        |
        v
ChatClient
        |
        v
Chat Memory Advisor
        |
        v
ChatMemory
        |
        v
ChatMemoryRepository
        |
        v
JDBC Repository
        |
        v
Relational Database
        |
        v
Relevant history used in model request
        |
        v
AI generates response
        |
        v
Conversation messages persisted
```

Teacher: Is video ka sabse important takeaway kya hai?

Student: Database mein chat history save karna aur us history ko AI ke context mein provide karna do related lekin alag responsibilities hain. Spring AI mein `ChatMemory`, repository implementation, conversation ID aur memory Advisor ko sahi tarah configure karna zaroori hai, taaki conversation history reliably use ho sake.

# PART 1: RAG ki zarurat kyu padi?

## Q1. Humne abhi tak Spring AI mein kya seekha tha?

Teacher: Pichle lectures mein humne kya implement kiya tha?

Student: Humne Chat Memory ka use karke conversation history database mein store karna seekha tha.

Teacher: Bilkul sahi!

Example ke liye, maan lo humne Spring Boot application banayi hai jisme user AI chatbot se baat karta hai.

User:

```
My name is Rahul.
I am a Java developer.
```

AI:

```
Nice to meet you, Rahul!
```

Ab user puchta hai:

```
What is my name?
```

AI ko pata hona chahiye ki user ne pehle apna naam Rahul bataya tha.

Iske liye hum Chat Memory use karte hain.

### Chat Memory kaise kaam karti hai?

1. User application ko message send karta hai.
2. Application user message ko database mein store karti hai.
3. AI ka response bhi conversation history mein maintain hota hai.
4. Jab user next question send karta hai, application previous conversation history retrieve karti hai.
5. History aur new question LLM ko send kiye jaate hain.
6. LLM previous conversation samajhkar response generate karta hai.

Simple flow:

```
User
  |
  v
Spring Boot Application
  |
  v
Chat Memory
  |
  v
Database
  |
  v
Previous Conversation Retrieve
  |
  v
LLM
  |
  v
Response
```

### Q2. Kya Chat Memory har situation mein sufficient hai?

Student: Nahi sir!

Chat Memory limited conversation history maintain karne ke liye useful hai. Lekin agar humein bahut saare documents ya company data ke basis par answers generate karne hain, toh problem aa sakti hai.

Example:

Maan lo hum ek company chatbot develop kar rahe hain.

Company ke paas:

- 500 PDF documents.
- 100 company policies.
- Employee handbook.
- HR guidelines.
- Technical documentation.
- Company-related information.

Ab employees chatbot se questions puchenge:

```
What is the company's leave policy?

How many paid leaves are available?

What is the work-from-home policy?
```

Aise case mein Chat Memory sufficient nahi hai.

Kyunki company ka complete data har baar LLM ko send nahi kar sakte.

Yahan par RAG ki requirement aati hai.

# PART 2: Chat Memory ki limitations

## Q3. Chat Memory approach mein kya problems aa sakti hain?

Teacher: Agar hum company ka complete data LLM ko send karna chahein toh kya problems hongi?

Student: Sir, bahut saara data send karna difficult ho sakta hai.

Teacher: Correct! Video mein instructor ne kuch important problems explain ki hain.

### 1. Large Amount of Data

Maan lo company ke paas 10,000 PDF documents hain.

Har document mein company-related information available hai.

Agar user ek simple question puchta hai:

```
What is the leave policy?
```

Toh kya humein 10,000 documents ka complete data LLM ko send karna chahiye?

Nahi!

Humein sirf leave policy se related information send karni chahiye.

### 2. High Token Consumption

Token kya hota hai?

Token text ka ek chhota unit hota hai jise LLM process karta hai.

Example:

```
I am learning Java.
```

LLM is text ko tokens mein process karta hai.

Agar hum bahut saara data LLM ko send karenge toh tokens ki quantity increase hogi.

Iska result:

- Token consumption increase hoga.
- API cost increase ho sakti hai.
- Processing mein zyada time lag sakta hai.

### 3. Context Window Limitation

Teacher: Context Window kya hoti hai?

Student: Sir, LLM ek request mein jitne maximum tokens process kar sakta hai, uski limit ko Context Window kehte hain.

Example:

Maan lo kisi model ki context window 100,000 tokens hai.

Aur hum 150,000 tokens send karte hain.

Toh complete input process nahi ho payega.

Har model ki apni context window limit hoti hai.

### 4. Private Company Data

LLM models generally publicly available aur training ke samay available data se train kiye jaate hain.

Example:

Maan lo company ki apni private information hai:

- Internal HR policies.
- Employee salary structure.
- Internal project documentation.
- Company-specific rules.

Ye information necessarily LLM ki training mein available nahi hogi.

Toh LLM ko company ki private information kaise milegi?

Yahan RAG useful hota hai.

### 5. Latest Information

LLM ki training ek particular cutoff date tak ke data par based ho sakti hai.

Example:

Maan lo LLM ko 2024 tak ki information par train kiya gaya hai.

Lekin company ne 2026 mein new leave policy introduce ki.

Ab LLM ko automatically 2026 ki new policy pata nahi hogi.

RAG ke through hum latest company data provide kar sakte hain.

### 6. Hallucination

Teacher: Hallucination ka kya meaning hota hai?

Student: Jab AI kabhi-kabhi incorrect ya fabricated information ko confident answer ki tarah present karta hai, use hallucination kehte hain.

Example:

User:

```
How many paid leaves does our company provide?
```

AI:

```
Your company provides 30 paid leaves.
```

Lekin actual policy mein 20 paid leaves hain.

Yeh incorrect answer hallucination ka example ho sakta hai.

RAG relevant documents se factual information retrieve karke aisi mistakes ko reduce karne mein help karta hai.

Chat Memory vs RAG – Basic Difference

| Chat Memory                             | RAG                                           |
| --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- |
| Previous conversation store karta hai   | External knowledge retrieve karta hai         |
| Conversation context maintain karta hai | Documents aur large data se context laata hai |
| Chat history ke liye useful             | Company chatbot ke liye useful                |
| User aur AI ke previous messages        | Relevant document information                 |

# PART 3: What is RAG?

## Q4. RAG ka full form kya hai?

Student: RAG ka full form hai:

Retrieval-Augmented Generation.

Ismein three important words hain:

1. Retrieval
2. Augmented
3. Generation

Inko individually samajhte hain.

### 1. Retrieval

Retrieval ka meaning hai existing data source se relevant information retrieve karna.

Example:

Company ke database mein 1,000 documents hain.

User asks:

```
What is the company's leave policy?
```

System complete 1,000 documents retrieve nahi karega.

Sirf leave policy se related information retrieve karega.

Isi process ko Retrieval kehte hain.

### 2. Augmented

Augmented ka meaning hai existing information ko additional information ke saath improve ya enhance karna.

Example:

User ka question:

```
What is the leave policy?
```

Database se retrieved information:

```
Employees are eligible for 20 paid leaves annually.
```

Ab application user ke question ke saath retrieved information attach karegi.

Matlab:

```
User Question + Retrieved Context
```

Is process ko Augmentation kehte hain.

### 3. Generation

Ab question aur retrieved context LLM ko send kiya jayega.

LLM dono ko understand karke final answer generate karega.

Example:

```
Question:
What is the leave policy?

Context:
Employees are eligible for 20 paid leaves annually.
```

LLM Response:

```
Employees are eligible for 20 paid leaves annually.
```

Ye Generation hai.

## Q5. RAG exactly kya karta hai?

Student: RAG ek technique hai jisme hum external data source se user ke question ke according relevant information retrieve karte hain, us information ko user ke prompt ke saath combine karte hain aur LLM ko send karte hain.

LLM retrieved context ko use karke answer generate karta hai.

Interview Answer:

RAG stands for Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It is a technique that retrieves relevant information from an external knowledge source and adds it to the user's prompt before sending it to the LLM. This helps the LLM generate more relevant and context-aware answers using external data.

# PART 4: RAG ka complete working flow

## Q6. RAG kaise work karta hai?

Teacher: Ab hum RAG ka complete flow samajhte hain.

RAG ko samajhne ke liye ek company chatbot ka example lete hain.

Maan lo hum Spring Boot aur Spring AI use karke chatbot bana rahe hain.

[AI Context Engineering Workshop | Vizuara AI Labs](https://images.openai.com/static-rsc-4/w8N6O8YyMcu9XRBlH_6lGxwiNGGljLcPY_lQz78emO1X8vjnkoIxUTxolorYXTHYbcikB8VCJ-Ae8rYl3yrFkZZ7sWfrsi5EYkEaieewthVdc-vUBx_YHmbMg9MT6qtdNlPbbkZMaXgCfPR8V9SzFIoPcrGi5rqEj8QsvCTnlpE?purpose=inline)

[context-engineering.vizuara.ai](https://context-engineering.vizuara.ai/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

RAG ka flow samajhne ke liye do phases samajhna important hai:

1. Data Ingestion Phase – Database mein data prepare aur store karna.
2. Query and Retrieval Phase – User ke question ke according data retrieve karke answer generate karna.

## Phase 1: Data Ingestion

### Q7. Data Ingestion kya hota hai?

Student: Data Ingestion ka meaning hai source se data collect karke database mein store karne ke liye prepare karna.

Example:

Company ke paas documents hain:

```
company-policy.pdf
employee-handbook.pdf
leave-policy.pdf
work-from-home.pdf
```

Hum in documents ka data apne knowledge database mein store karenge.

Video mein instructor Vector Database ka use explain karta hai.

### Q8. Vector Database kya hota hai?

Teacher: Vector Database ek special type ka database hai jo vector representations ko store aur search karne ke liye use hota hai.

Vector kya hota hai?

Vector numbers ki ek list hoti hai jo kisi text ya information ka numerical representation provide karti hai.

Example:

```
"Java is a programming language"
```

Is text ko embedding model numerical vector mein convert kar sakta hai.

Illustrative example:

```
[0.21, 0.45, 0.78, 0.12]
```

Ye actual embedding nahi hai, sirf samajhne ke liye example hai.

Vector Database ka use similar meaning wali information efficiently search karne ke liye hota hai.

### Q9. Data ko chunks mein kyu divide karte hain?

Student: Maan lo ek PDF document mein 100 pages hain.

Agar hum complete PDF ko ek single unit ki tarah process karenge toh relevant information find karna difficult ho sakta hai.

Isliye document ko small-small pieces mein divide karte hain.

In pieces ko Chunks kehte hain.

Example:

```
Document: Company Policy

Chunk 1:
Company introduction.

Chunk 2:
Employee leave policy.

Chunk 3:
Work from home policy.

Chunk 4:
Employee benefits.
```

Ab agar user leave policy ke baare mein question puchta hai toh system relevant chunk retrieve kar sakta hai.

Important: Transcript mein chunking aur Vector Database ka concept explain kiya gaya hai. Actual chunk size, overlap aur embedding implementation next lecture ke liye chhoda gaya hai.

### Phase 1 Flow

```
Company Documents
       |
       v
Read Documents
       |
       v
Divide into Small Chunks
       |
       v
Convert Text into Vectors
       |
       v
Store in Vector Database
```

Har step ka meaning:

1. Company documents collect karo.
2. Documents ka text read karo.
3. Text ko small chunks mein divide karo.
4. Chunks ko vector representations mein convert karo.
5. Vectors aur associated information Vector Database mein store karo.

## Phase 2: Query and Retrieval

### Q10. User question send karega toh kya hoga?

Maan lo user question send karta hai:

```
What is the company's leave policy?
```

Ab complete company data LLM ko send nahi kiya jayega.

Instead, application Vector Database se relevant information search karegi.

### Step 1: User sends query

```
User
  |
  v
Spring Boot Application
```

User apna question Spring Boot application ko send karta hai.

### Step 2: Similarity Search

Teacher: Similarity Search kya hota hai?

Student: Similarity Search ka meaning hai kisi query ke meaning ke saath similar information search karna.

Example:

User query:

```
Tell me about employee holidays.
```

Database mein stored information:

```
Chunk 1: Company introduction.
Chunk 2: Annual paid leave policy.
Chunk 3: Employee salary policy.
Chunk 4: Work from home policy.
```

System meaning ke basis par relevant chunk find karega.

Yahan exact words match hona compulsory nahi hai.

Vector Database mein similarity search ka use karke semantically similar information retrieve ki ja sakti hai.

### Step 3: Relevant Context Retrieve

Vector Database relevant chunks application ko return karega.

Example:

```
Retrieved Context:

Employees are eligible for
20 paid leaves annually.
```

Ab application ke paas relevant information available hai.

### Step 4: Augment the Prompt

Ab Spring Boot application user query aur retrieved context combine karegi.

Example:

```
You are a company assistant.

Context:
Employees are eligible for
20 paid leaves annually.

Question:
What is the company's leave policy?
```

Yahan important baat hai:

LLM ko ab company ka complete database nahi bhejna hai.

Sirf relevant information bhejni hai.

### Step 5: Send to LLM

Ab augmented prompt LLM ko send hoga.

LLM:

1. User question understand karega.
2. Retrieved context understand karega.
3. Context ke basis par response generate karega.

### Step 6: Generate Response

Example:

```
Employees are eligible for
20 paid leaves annually.
```

### Step 7: Return Response to User

Spring Boot application generated response receive karegi.

Uske baad application response user ko return kar degi.

### Complete RAG Flow Diagram

## RAG – Complete Working Flow

User Question

What is the leave policy?

Spring Boot Application

Vector Database

Similarity Search

Relevant Context Retrieved

20 paid leaves annually

Augmented Prompt

User Query + Retrieved Context

LLM

Understands context and generates answer

Spring Boot Response → User

Interview Answer:

RAG works in two major phases. First, documents are loaded, divided into chunks, converted into embeddings and stored in a vector database. Second, when a user sends a query, the application retrieves relevant chunks using similarity search, combines them with the user query and sends the augmented prompt to the LLM. The LLM generates a context-aware response.

# PART 5: Why do we need RAG?

## Q11. RAG ki requirement kyu hai?

Video mein instructor ne RAG ke important benefits aur problems explain ki hain.

| Problem                          | RAG ka solution                                                                       |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Limited model knowledge          | External data provide kar sakte hain                                                  |
| Private company data unavailable | Private documents se context retrieve kar sakte hain                                  |
| Context window limitation        | Sirf relevant information send karte hain                                             |
| Large knowledge base             | Relevant chunks search karte hain                                                     |
| Hallucination                    | Relevant factual context provide karke hallucination reduce karne mein help karta hai |
| Latest information missing       | Updated external data retrieve kar sakte hain                                         |

## Q12. RAG hallucination ko kaise reduce karta hai?

Student: RAG user ke question se related factual information database se retrieve karta hai.

Us information ko prompt ke saath LLM ko send kiya jaata hai.

Isse LLM ko answer generate karne ke liye relevant reference milta hai.

Example:

Without RAG:

```
Question:
What is our company leave policy?

LLM:
May generate an incorrect answer
because it doesn't know the private policy.
```

With RAG:

```
Question:
What is our company leave policy?

Context:
20 paid leaves annually.

LLM:
Employees receive 20 paid leaves annually.
```

Important: RAG hallucination ko reduce karta hai, completely eliminate nahi karta. Agar retrieved data incorrect ya irrelevant hai toh answer bhi incorrect ho sakta hai.

# PART 6: Real-world applications of RAG

## Q13. RAG ka use real-world applications mein kahan hota hai?

### 1. Company Chatbot

Company apne internal documents aur policies ke basis par chatbot develop kar sakti hai.

### 2. Customer Support System

Maan lo company ke paas previous customer complaints aur solutions ka database hai.

User apni problem describe karta hai.

RAG relevant previous solutions retrieve karke LLM ko provide kar sakta hai.

LLM user ko solution suggest kar sakta hai.

### 3. PDF Chatbot

User PDF upload karta hai aur questions puchta hai.

RAG PDF se relevant information retrieve karta hai aur answer generate karta hai.

### 4. Technical Documentation Chatbot

Maan lo company ke paas Spring Boot, Java aur Microservices documentation available hai.

Developer question puch sakta hai:

```
How does our API Gateway work?
```

RAG internal technical documentation se relevant information retrieve karega.

# PART 7: Important interview questions

## Q14. What is RAG?

Answer: RAG stands for Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It retrieves relevant information from external data sources and adds that information to the user's prompt before sending it to the LLM. This helps generate context-aware answers.

## Q15. Why do we need RAG?

Answer: We need RAG to provide external, private and updated information to LLMs without sending the entire knowledge base. It also helps reduce token consumption and hallucinations.

## Q16. What is a Vector Database?

Answer: A Vector Database stores and searches numerical vector representations of data. It is useful for finding semantically similar information using similarity search.

## Q17. What is a Chunk?

Answer: A chunk is a small piece of a larger document. Documents are divided into chunks so that the system can retrieve only relevant information instead of processing the entire document.

## Q18. What is Similarity Search?

Answer: Similarity Search is a technique used to find information that is semantically similar to a given query. In RAG, it helps retrieve relevant document chunks from a vector database.

## Q19. Does RAG train the LLM?

Answer: No. RAG does not normally train or modify the LLM. It provides relevant external information as context during inference.

## Q20. Does RAG eliminate hallucinations?

Answer: No. RAG can reduce hallucinations by providing relevant information, but it cannot guarantee completely accurate answers.

## Q21. What is the difference between Chat Memory and RAG?

Answer: Chat Memory stores previous conversation history to maintain conversational context, whereas RAG retrieves relevant information from external knowledge sources to answer user questions.

## Q22. Can we implement RAG using Spring Boot?

Answer: Yes. We can use Spring AI with a vector database and an LLM to implement RAG-based applications in Spring Boot.

# PART 8: Quick Revision

Teacher: Chalo ab complete topic revise karte hain.

| Term              | Simple meaning                                              |
| ----------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| RAG               | External data retrieve karke LLM se answer generate karwana |
| Retrieval         | Relevant information search karna                           |
| Augmented         | Context ko prompt ke saath add karna                        |
| Generation        | LLM se answer generate karwana                              |
| Vector            | Numerical representation of information                     |
| Vector Database   | Vectors store aur search karne wala database                |
| Chunk             | Document ka small piece                                     |
| Similarity Search | Similar meaning wala data find karna                        |
| Context Window    | Model ki input processing limit                             |
| Hallucination     | AI ka incorrect ya fabricated answer dena                   |
| Embedding         | Text ko numerical vector mein convert karna                 |

## RAG ka one-minute revision

```
1. Documents collect karo.
2. Documents ko chunks mein divide karo.
3. Chunks ko embeddings mein convert karo.
4. Vector Database mein store karo.
5. User query receive karo.
6. Similarity Search se relevant chunks retrieve karo.
7. Retrieved context ko user prompt ke saath combine karo.
8. Augmented prompt LLM ko send karo.
9. LLM answer generate karega.
10. Spring Boot response user ko return karega.
```

Next learning topic: RAG ke andar humne Vector Database, Embeddings aur Chunking ka naam suna. In concepts ko properly samajhne ke baad hi practical Spring AI RAG implementation easy hoga.

Is video se sabse important baat yaad rakho:

Chat Memory AI ko batati hai ki user ne pehle kya baat ki thi. RAG AI ko batata hai ki external documents aur data mein relevant information kya available hai.
