22. DETAIL UNDERSTANDING ABOUT VECTORS | VECTOR DATABASES | EMBEDDINGS IN AI | SPRING AI
Complete Self-Study Notes – Easy Hinglish (Hindi + English)

SOURCE
YouTube video: “Detail Understanding about Vectors | Vector Databases | Embeddings in AI | Spring AI Tutorials”
Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mOKD9rP6DZk

IMPORTANT NOTE
These notes follow the supplied transcript and its teaching sequence. The video is mainly conceptual. It does not demonstrate Java/Spring AI code, Maven dependencies, database installation commands, or a complete runnable project. Those details are therefore not invented here.

============================================================
1. IS VIDEO MEIN HUM KYA SEEKHENGE?
============================================================

Teacher: Is video ka main objective kya hai?

Student: Is video mein hum Vector, Embedding, Vector Database, Similarity Search aur RAG ke andar inka use samjhenge.

Teacher: Ye concepts seekhna kyun zaroori hai?

Student: Kyunki RAG implement karne ke liye humein samajhna padega ki:
1. Text ya doosri information ko numbers mein kaise represent kiya jaata hai.
2. Ye numerical representation database mein kaise store hoti hai.
3. User ki query se meaning ke basis par related information kaise search hoti hai.
4. Search se mili information LLM ko kaise di jaati hai.

Video ka central idea:
RAG mein user ki query ko embedding/vector mein convert kiya jaata hai. Us vector ki help se Vector Database mein similar information search hoti hai. Retrieved information aur original query LLM ko di jaati hain. LLM us context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.

============================================================
2. VECTOR KYA HOTA HAI?
============================================================

Teacher: AI/ML ke context mein Vector kya hota hai?

Student: Vector numbers ki ek array hoti hai jo kisi information ko mathematical/numerical form mein represent karti hai.

Simple definition:
Vector = Numbers ki ordered list/array, jo kisi data ya information ko represent karti hai.

Example:
[0.25, 0.80, 0.12, 0.65]

Ye numbers apne aap mein human-readable sentence nahi hain. Lekin AI system mein ye kisi text, image, audio ya doosri information ki numerical representation ho sakte hain.

Teacher: Array ka matlab kya hota hai?

Student: Array ek collection hota hai jisme multiple values ek saath rakhi jaati hain. Jaise Java mein int[] ya double[].

Java example:
double[] vector = {0.25, 0.80, 0.12, 0.65};

Yahan vector variable mein numbers ki array store hai. Ye sirf vector ko samjhane wala Java example hai; video mein Java code demonstrate nahi hua.

Teacher: Kya vector ka relation Mathematics ke vector se hai?

Student: Basic idea similar hai. Mathematics mein vector ko values/direction/magnitude ke form mein samjha ja sakta hai. AI/ML mein hum yahan numerical values ki array ki baat kar rahe hain jo information represent karti hai.

IMPORTANT:
Video mein instructor matrix aur N-dimensional array ka reference deta hai. Beginner ke liye itna yaad rakhein:
- 1D array: values ki ek line/list.
- 2D array: rows aur columns jaisa structure.
- N-dimensional array: ek se zyada dimensions wala general data structure.
- AI embeddings aam taur par ek numerical vector hoti hain jisme multiple dimensions/values hoti hain.

============================================================
3. VECTOR KIS TARAH KI INFORMATION REPRESENT KAR SAKTA HAI?
============================================================

Teacher: Vector sirf words ke liye hota hai?

Student: Nahi. Video ke according, different types ki information ko numerical form mein represent kiya ja sakta hai, jaise:
- Text
- Image
- Audio
- Video
- Other supported data

Example 1 – Text:
Sentence: “Java is a very popular language.”

AI system is text ko numerical values ke vector ke form mein represent kar sakta hai.

Example 2 – Word:
Word: “Apple”

Sirf samjhane ke liye hum ise aise imagine kar sakte hain:
[0.1, 0.4, 0.8, 0.2]

Example 3 – Image:
Ek dog ki image ko bhi numerical representation/vector ke form mein represent kiya ja sakta hai.

Example 4 – Audio:
Audio file ki information ko bhi suitable embedding/vector representation mein convert kiya ja sakta hai.

IMPORTANT CLARIFICATION:
Video mein Apple ke liye ek chhota 1D array example sirf understanding ke liye dikhaya gaya hai. Real embedding mein dimensions kaafi zyada ho sakti hain. Video exact dimension count ya kisi particular embedding model ka naam nahi batata.

============================================================
4. EMBEDDING KYA HOTI HAI?
============================================================

Teacher: Embedding ka simple meaning kya hai?

Student: Embedding kisi information ki numerical representation hoti hai. AI system text, image ya doosre supported input ko numbers ke vector mein represent karta hai. Is numerical representation ko embedding kaha jaata hai.

Simple way:
Original information → Embedding process → Numbers ka vector

Example:
Text: “Java is a popular programming language.”
        |
        v
Embedding representation
        |
        v
[0.12, 0.45, 0.78, 0.31, ...]

Teacher: Kya Embedding aur Vector alag cheezein hain?

Student: Is video ke context mein instructor in terms ko closely related tarike se use karta hai:
- Vector: numbers ki array.
- Embedding: information ko represent karne wala numerical vector.

Easy memory trick:
Embedding batata hai ki information ko numerical form mein represent kiya gaya hai.
Vector us numerical representation ki values/array ko refer karta hai.

Note:
Technically, “embedding” representation ko describe karta hai aur “vector” us representation ka numerical structure hota hai. Video in dono ko conversationally almost interchangeably use karta hai.

Teacher: Embedding kyun chahiye?

Student: Computer ko human sentence ka meaning directly waise samajh nahi aata jaise insaan samajhta hai. Numerical representation ki help se AI systems information ke beech mathematical comparison kar sakte hain. Isi se similarity search possible hoti hai.

============================================================
5. VECTOR DATABASE KYA HOTA HAI?
============================================================

Teacher: Vector Database kya hai?

Student: Vector Database ek special type ka database hai jo vector embeddings ko store karta hai aur un par similarity search perform karne ke liye optimized hota hai.

Simple definition:
Vector Database = Aisa database jo embeddings/vectors store karta hai aur similar vectors search kar sakta hai.

Teacher: Normal database aur Vector Database mein kya difference hai?

Student:
Normal relational databases, jaise MySQL aur PostgreSQL, structured data ko tables, rows aur columns mein store karte hain. Unmein hum columns/properties aur conditions ke basis par search kar sakte hain.

Vector Database ka important use embeddings ke beech similarity search karna hai.

Example – Normal database:
Employee table mein:
- employee_id = 101
- name = Rahul
- department = IT

Query:
department = 'IT'

Yahan hum exact property/value ke basis par record search kar rahe hain.

Example – Vector Database:
Stored information:
- Company leave policy
- Company work-from-home policy
- Company reimbursement policy
- Company security policy

User query:
“What is the work-from-home policy?”

Query ko embedding mein convert karke vector database mein similar meaning wali information search ki ja sakti hai.

IMPORTANT:
Video normal database ko useless nahi bolta. Dono ka purpose alag hai:
- Normal database: structured data aur property/condition-based queries.
- Vector search: numerical embeddings ke basis par similarity-oriented retrieval.

Video mention karta hai ki PostgreSQL aur kuch doosre databases vector capabilities support karte hain. Iska matlab ye nahi ki har database/version mein same feature ya same setup hota hai. Exact support version aur extension/configuration par depend karega.

============================================================
6. SIMILARITY SEARCH KYA HAI?
============================================================

Teacher: Similarity Search ka matlab kya hai?

Student: Similarity Search ka matlab hai query ke meaning/representation se milte-julte vectors ya information ko search karna.

Simple example:
Aap search karte ho: “King”

System aisi information dhoondhne ki koshish karega jo “King” se meaning ke level par related ho. Exact output embedding model, stored data aur search method par depend karega.

Ek aur example:
User query: “Company ki work-from-home policy kya hai?”

Database mein policy documents ke embeddings stored hain. Similarity search query embedding ke close/similar vectors dhoondhegi. Un vectors se linked documents retrieve kiye ja sakte hain.

Teacher: Kya similarity search exact word matching hi hoti hai?

Student: Nahi. Iska main idea meaning/representation ke basis par related information dhoondhna hai, sirf exact same word dhoondhna nahi.

Example:
Query: “How can I work from home?”
Stored text: “Remote work guidelines for employees.”

Exact words alag hain, lekin meaning related ho sakta hai. Embedding-based search aisi similarity identify karne mein help kar sakti hai.

IMPORTANT:
Similarity ka result guaranteed perfect understanding nahi hota. Results embedding model, data quality, chunking, similarity metric, index aur search configuration par depend karte hain. Video in factors ka detailed implementation nahi karta.

============================================================
7. SIMILARITY SEARCH KE LIYE MATHEMATICS
============================================================

Teacher: Database kaise decide karta hai ki kaunse vectors similar hain?

Student: Vectors ke beech mathematical calculations ki help se similarity ya distance measure ki jaati hai.

Video do examples mention karta hai:
1. Cosine Similarity
2. Euclidean Distance

A) Cosine Similarity

Simple meaning:
Cosine Similarity do vectors ke beech unki direction kitni similar hai, ye measure karne ka ek mathematical method hai.

Easy analogy:
Socho do arrows hain. Agar dono arrows lagbhag same direction mein point kar rahe hain, to unki cosine similarity high ho sakti hai.

B) Euclidean Distance

Simple meaning:
Euclidean Distance do points/vectors ke beech straight-line distance measure karti hai.

Easy analogy:
Map par do locations ke beech seedhi line ka distance socho. Points jitne paas honge, distance utna kam hoga.

Basic difference:
- Cosine Similarity: direction/orientation ki similarity dekhti hai.
- Euclidean Distance: vectors ke beech distance measure karti hai.

IMPORTANT:
Similarity score ya distance ko interpret karne ka tareeka selected metric aur database implementation par depend karta hai. Har metric mein “bada score = zyada similar” rule same nahi hota. Euclidean distance mein generally chhota distance closer points ko show karta hai.

Video mathematical formulas ya code-level implementation detail mein nahi jaata. Isliye yahan focus concept par hai.

============================================================
8. 3D SPACE ANALOGY – SIMILAR VECTORS PAAS KYUN HOTE HAIN?
============================================================

Teacher: Instructor ne X, Y, Z axes ka example kyun diya?

Student: High-dimensional vectors ko imagine karna difficult hota hai. Isliye instructor 3D space ka simple example use karta hai.

3D space mein teen axes hoti hain:
- X-axis
- Y-axis
- Z-axis

Socho is space mein multiple points/objects rakhe hain.

Agar kuch points ek jaise/similar hain, to analogy mein unhe ek doosre ke paas imagine kar sakte hain.
Agar kuch points dissimilar hain, to unhe door imagine kar sakte hain.

Example:
- Group A: Dog-related images ke vectors.
- Group B: Car-related images ke vectors.

Conceptually, similar content ke vectors embedding space mein paas ho sakte hain, aur dissimilar content ke vectors door ho sakte hain.

Teacher: Kya actual vector database sirf 3D use karta hai?

Student: Nahi. Instructor clearly kehta hai ki 3D sirf samjhane ke liye hai. Actual embeddings N-dimensional hoti hain. N-dimensional space ko normal human visualization se samajhna difficult hota hai.

IMPORTANT:
“Similar items always physically paas hi honge” ko literal physical rule mat samajhna. Ye mathematical vector space ki analogy hai. Actual closeness selected embedding representation aur distance/similarity metric ke through calculate hoti hai.

============================================================
9. TOP-K / TOP-FIVE SIMILAR RESULTS
============================================================

Teacher: Agar database mein bahut saare vectors hain, to kya sabhi results return karne chahiye?

Student: Usually humein sirf most relevant results chahiye hote hain. Isliye hum top results retrieve kar sakte hain, jaise Top 5 ya Top 10.

Example:
User query: “Company policy”

Vector search ko 10,000 stored embeddings mein se relevant results dhoondhne hain.

System similarity/distance ke basis par best matching results return kar sakta hai:
1. Work-from-home policy
2. Leave policy
3. Employee conduct policy
4. Attendance policy
5. Security policy

Ye example samjhane ke liye hai; exact results stored documents aur query par depend karenge.

Top-K ka meaning:
K = kitne results retrieve karne hain.
- Top 5: 5 results
- Top 10: 10 results

Teacher: Top 5 hi kyun?

Student: Application ki requirement par depend karta hai. Humein jitna context chahiye, uske according top 5, top 10 ya koi aur count select kar sakte hain.

IMPORTANT:
Video “similarity index” aur percentage jaise 90%/50% ka informal example deta hai. Har vector database similarity ko percentage ke form mein show nahi karta. Actual score metric-specific hota hai. Isliye score ko bina metric samjhe percentage of correctness mat samajhna.

============================================================
10. VECTOR DATABASE KA RAG MEIN ROLE
============================================================

Teacher: Ab tak Vector aur Vector Database samjha. RAG mein inka use kyun hota hai?

Student: RAG ka full form Retrieval-Augmented Generation hai.
- Retrieval: Relevant information search/retrieve karna.
- Augmented: LLM ko extra context dena.
- Generation: LLM se answer generate karwana.

RAG ka basic idea:
LLM ko sirf apni pre-trained knowledge par depend nahi karna. Application pehle apne documents/data mein se relevant information retrieve karti hai, phir woh information LLM ko user query ke saath deti hai.

Video ka example:
Company ke paas bahut saari policies hain. User poochta hai:
“What are the top five policies of your company?”

Application company policy information ko vector database mein embeddings ke form mein store karti hai. User ki query ko bhi embedding mein convert kiya jaata hai. Similarity search se relevant policy information retrieve hoti hai. Phir retrieved knowledge aur user query LLM ko di jaati hai.

============================================================
11. RAG – COMPLETE REQUEST-TO-RESPONSE FLOW
============================================================

Teacher: RAG mein complete flow step by step samjhao.

Student:

STEP 1 – User query send karta hai
User Spring Boot application ko question bhejta hai.

Example:
“What is the company work-from-home policy?”

STEP 2 – Query ko embedding mein convert karna
Application user query ko embedding/vector mein convert karti hai.

Original query:
“What is the company work-from-home policy?”

Query embedding:
[0.12, 0.45, 0.78, 0.31, ...]

Ye numbers sirf illustration hain. Video exact vector values nahi deta.

STEP 3 – Vector Database mein similarity search
Application query embedding ko Vector Database ke paas bhejti hai.

Vector Database stored document embeddings ke saath similarity/distance compare karke related information search karta hai.

STEP 4 – Top relevant data retrieve karna
Search ke result mein top 5, top 10 ya required count ke relevant records/documents retrieve kiye jaate hain.

Video is retrieved information ko “Knowledge” kehta hai.

STEP 5 – Query + Retrieved Knowledge LLM ko dena
Application LLM ko do important cheezein provide karti hai:
1. Original user query.
2. Vector search se mila relevant knowledge/context.

Example:
Query: “What is the company work-from-home policy?”
Knowledge: Retrieved work-from-home policy content.

STEP 6 – LLM answer generate karta hai
LLM provided context ko read/understand karke user ke question ka response generate karta hai.

STEP 7 – Response user ko return hota hai
Spring Boot application generated answer ko user/client ko return karti hai.

User

DIAGRAM KO SAMJHO:
- User → Spring Boot: user question bhejta hai.
- Spring Boot → Embedding: query ko numerical vector mein convert karwata hai.
- Embedding → Vector Database: query vector search ke liye bheja jaata hai.
- Vector Database → Knowledge: relevant documents retrieve hote hain.
- Knowledge + Query → LLM: LLM ko context aur question milta hai.
- LLM → User: final answer return hota hai.

============================================================
12. DOCUMENTS KO VECTOR DATABASE MEIN STORE KARNE KA IDEA
============================================================

Teacher: User ki query aane se pehle company ka data database mein kaise available hota hai?

Student: Video ka conceptual flow ye hai:
1. Company se related information/documents available hain.
2. Un information ko embeddings/vector representation mein convert kiya jaata hai.
3. Embeddings ko Vector Database mein store kiya jaata hai.
4. Jab user query karta hai, query embedding ke basis par stored embeddings search hoti hain.

Simple conceptual flow:

Company Documents
      |
      v
Embedding Representation
      |
      v
Vector Database mein storage

User Query
      |
      v
Query Embedding
      |
      v

      |

Relevant Information

IMPORTANT LIMITATION:
Video data ingestion ke exact steps, document splitting/chunking, embedding model selection, metadata schema, database index creation ya code nahi dikhata. Isliye ye notes sirf wahi conceptual process explain karte hain jo transcript support karta hai.

============================================================
13. NORMAL DATABASE VS VECTOR DATABASE
============================================================

Teacher: Dono mein basic difference kya hai?

Student:

NORMAL / RELATIONAL DATABASE
- Structured data store karne ke liye use hota hai.
- Data tables, rows aur columns mein ho sakta hai.
- SQL conditions ya properties ke basis par records search kiye ja sakte hain.
- Example: employee_id, name, department.

VECTOR DATABASE
- Embeddings/vectors store karne ke liye use hota hai.
- Similarity-oriented search ke liye optimized hota hai.
- Query vector ke similar vectors retrieve kar sakta hai.
- Example: policy documents ke embeddings mein query se related policy content dhoondhna.

Why does the difference matter?
Agar humein employee ID exact match karni hai, normal database query suitable hai.
Agar humein user ke natural-language question se meaning ke basis par related document dhoondhna hai, vector similarity search useful ho sakti hai.

Note:
Video PostgreSQL aur doosre databases mein vector support ka mention karta hai. Kuch databases traditional relational features ke saath vector search capabilities bhi provide kar sakte hain. Isliye “normal database kabhi vector search nahi kar sakta” aisa absolute conclusion nahi nikalna chahiye.

15. IMPORTANT TERMS – QUICK REVISION
============================================================

1. Vector
Numbers ki array jo information ko represent karti hai.

2. Embedding
Text/image/audio jaise data ki numerical representation.

3. N-dimensional array
Aisa array structure jisme multiple dimensions ho sakti hain.

4. Vector Database
Embeddings store aur similarity search karne ke liye special/optimized database.

5. Similarity Search
Query ke similar meaning/representation wale vectors ya records dhoondhna.

6. Cosine Similarity
Do vectors ki direction/orientation ki similarity measure karne ka mathematical method.

7. Euclidean Distance
Do vectors/points ke beech straight-line distance measure karne ka method.

8. Query
User ka question ya search request.

9. Top-K
Search se return karne wale top K relevant results. Example: Top 5.

10. Knowledge / Context
RAG mein retrieved relevant information jo LLM ko answer banane ke liye di jaati hai.

11. LLM
Large Language Model. AI model jo input query aur supplied context ke basis par response generate karta hai.

12. RAG
Retrieval-Augmented Generation. Relevant external information retrieve karke LLM ko context dene aur answer generate karwane ka approach.

16. COMMON BEGINNER CONFUSIONS
============================================================

Q1. Kya Vector aur Embedding same hain?
Answer: Video mein dono terms closely/interchangeably use hui hain. Simple understanding ke liye embedding information ki numerical representation hai aur vector us representation ki numerical array hai.

Q2. Kya vector sirf text ko represent karta hai?
Answer: Nahi. Video text, image, audio aur video jaise data ka mention karta hai.

Q3. Kya Vector Database sirf numbers store karta hai?
Answer: Video ka focus embeddings/vectors store karne par hai. Practical vector database mein vector ke saath original text, ID aur metadata jaise associated fields bhi ho sakte hain, lekin unka schema video mein explain nahi hua.

Q4. Kya Similarity Search exact matching hai?
Answer: Nahi. Iska focus similar numerical representations ke basis par related information retrieve karna hai.

Q5. Kya 3D space actual embedding ka size hai?
Answer: Nahi. X, Y, Z ka example sirf visualization ke liye hai. Actual vectors N-dimensional ho sakte hain.

Q6. Kya Top 5 ka matlab hamesha 5 hi results?
Answer: Nahi. Top 5 example hai. Application requirement ke according Top 5, Top 10 ya doosra K select kiya ja sakta hai.

Q7. RAG mein LLM ko direct user query hi kyun nahi dete?
Answer: Video ke RAG flow mein pehle relevant company knowledge retrieve hota hai. Phir query ke saath retrieved knowledge LLM ko diya jaata hai, taaki response relevant context par based ho.

Q8. Kya Vector Database khud final answer generate karta hai?
Answer: Video ke flow mein Vector Database relevant information retrieve karta hai. Final natural-language answer LLM generate karta hai.

Q9. Kya similarity score hamesha percentage hota hai?
Answer: Nahi. Video percentage ka informal example deta hai. Actual similarity/distance score selected mathematical metric par depend karta hai.

Q10. Kya video mein Spring AI ka code diya gaya hai?
Answer: Nahi. Ye video conceptual explanation deta hai. Instructor kehta hai ki next videos mein database setup aur RAG implementation dekhenge.

============================================================
17. INTERVIEW QUESTIONS AND ANSWERS
============================================================

Q1. What is a vector in AI/ML?
Answer:
A vector is an array of numerical values used to represent information such as text, images, or audio in a mathematical form.

Q2. What is an embedding?
Answer:
An embedding is a numerical representation of data. It converts information such as text or images into a vector so that an AI system can perform mathematical comparisons and similarity search.

Q3. What is a Vector Database?
Answer:
A Vector Database is a database designed to store vector embeddings and perform similarity searches to retrieve vectors that are close or related to a query vector.

Q4. What is similarity search?
Answer:
Similarity search finds stored vectors that are mathematically similar to a query vector. It helps retrieve information related to the meaning of a user's query rather than relying only on exact keyword matching.

Q5. Why do we use a Vector Database in RAG?
Answer:
In RAG, the user's query is converted into an embedding. The Vector Database searches for similar stored embeddings and retrieves relevant information. That information is passed to the LLM along with the original query to generate a context-aware response.

Q6. What is the difference between a normal database and a Vector Database?
Answer:
A normal relational database commonly searches structured records using columns and conditions. A Vector Database stores embeddings and supports similarity-based retrieval. They solve different kinds of search requirements, and some traditional databases can also add vector capabilities.

Q7. What is Cosine Similarity?
Answer:
Cosine Similarity is a mathematical measure that compares the direction of two vectors. It is used to estimate how similar their vector representations are.

Q8. What is Euclidean Distance?
Answer:
Euclidean Distance measures the straight-line distance between two points or vectors. A smaller distance generally means the points are closer under this metric.

Q9. What is Top-K search?
Answer:
Top-K search returns the K best matching results from a search. For example, Top 5 returns the five highest-ranked results according to the selected search method.

Q10. Explain the RAG flow using a Vector Database.
Answer:
First, the user's query is converted into an embedding. The query embedding is searched in the Vector Database. The database returns the top relevant documents. The application sends those documents along with the original query to the LLM. The LLM generates an answer using the supplied context, and the application returns it to the user.

Q11. Why does the video use a 3D example to explain vectors?
Answer:
Actual embeddings can have N dimensions, which are difficult to visualize. The instructor uses X, Y and Z axes as a simple analogy to explain that similar vectors can be close in vector space and dissimilar vectors can be farther apart.

Q12. Does the Vector Database generate the final answer?
Answer:
No. In the flow explained in the video, the Vector Database retrieves relevant information. The LLM uses the retrieved information and the user's query to generate the final answer.

# Spring AI – RAG Implementation: MariaDB Vector Store

Complete Teacher–Student Q&A Notes | Hinglish | Beginner Friendly

Ye notes aapke provided YouTube transcript ke basis par banaye gaye hain. Important concepts, practical implementation, commands, dependencies aur code examples ko sequence mein explain kiya hai.

Part 1 – Introduction & Understanding

Q1. Pichhle video mein humne kya seekha tha?

Teacher: Pichhle video mein humne kaun-kaun se concepts cover kiye the?

Student:

Humne ye concepts seekhe the:

1. RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation): LLM ko external/private data ke basis par answer generate karwana.
2. Vector Database: Aisa database jo vectors (embeddings) store aur search kar sakta hai.
3. Vector: Data ka numerical representation.
4. Embedding: Text ko numerical vector mein convert karna.
5. Chunking: Bade document ko chhote-chhote parts mein divide karna.

Ab hum practical RAG implementation start karenge.

Q2. RAG implement karne ke liye Vector Database ki zarurat kyun hoti hai?

Student:

RAG implement karne ke liye humein apna private data store karna hota hai.

Example:

Maan lo company ke paas ek document hai:

```
Our company uses Java Spring Boot.
We develop microservices.
We use MySQL for database.
We use Kafka for messaging.
```

Humein kya karna hoga?

1. Document read karna.
2. Document ko small chunks mein divide karna.
3. Har chunk ka embedding generate karna.
4. Embeddings ko Vector Database mein store karna.
5. User ke question ke according similar data search karna.
6. Retrieved data LLM ko provide karna.

Is tarah LLM apne general knowledge ke bajaye retrieved private data ke basis par answer de sakta hai.

Q3. RAG mein Vector Database ka main purpose kya hai?

Student:

Vector Database ka main purpose hai:

- Embeddings store karna.
- Similarity Search perform karna.
- Relevant documents retrieve karna.
- Private data ko efficiently search karna.

Example:

User question:

```
Which programming language does our company use?
```

Database se relevant information retrieve hogi:

```
Our company uses Java Spring Boot.
```

Ye information LLM ko provide ki jayegi.

Q4. Spring AI kaun-kaun se Vector Databases support karta hai?

Student:

Spring AI multiple Vector Databases support karta hai.

Video mein kuch examples diye gaye hain:

- MariaDB
- Redis
- Chroma
- Neo4j
- Oracle
- PGVector
- Pinecone
- Qdrant
- Azure AI services

Hum apne practical implementation mein MariaDB Vector Store use karenge.

Baad mein video creator Qdrant bhi explain karne wale hain.

Q5. Hum MariaDB Vector Store hi kyun use kar rahe hain?

Student:

Video mein MariaDB select karne ke kuch reasons bataye gaye hain:

1. MariaDB mein Vector Store support available hai.
2. Docker ke through easily run kar sakte hain.
3. MariaDB 11.7 onwards vector support available hai, as explained in the video.
4. Spring AI MariaDB Vector Store integration provide karta hai.

Important: MariaDB version 11.7 ya uske baad ka compatible version use karna hai.

Part 2 – MariaDB Installation Using Docker

Q6. MariaDB ko Docker se kaise install karenge?

Teacher: Kya humein MariaDB manually install karna padega?

Student:

Nahi. Hum Docker container ke through MariaDB run kar sakte hain.

Docker ka benefit:

- Manual installation ki zarurat nahi.
- Container isolated environment mein run hota hai.
- Easily start aur stop kar sakte hain.
- Different database versions run kar sakte hain.

Step 1: Docker Image Download

```
docker pull mariadb:11.8
```

Explanation:

- `docker pull` – Docker image download karta hai.
- `mariadb` – Image name.
- `11.8` – Image version.

Step 2: MariaDB Container Run

Video mein following configuration use ki gayi hai:

```
docker run -d \
--name mariadb1 \
-e MARIADB_ROOT_PASSWORD=root1234 \
-p 3308:3306 \
mariadb:11.8
```

Explanation:

| Command                 | Meaning                              |
| ----------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `docker run`            | Container create aur start karta hai |
| `-d`                    | Detached mode mein run karta hai     |
| `--name mariadb1`       | Container ka naam                    |
| `-e`                    | Environment variable set karta hai   |
| `MARIADB_ROOT_PASSWORD` | Root password                        |
| `-p 3308:3306`          | Port mapping                         |
| `mariadb:11.8`          | MariaDB Docker image                 |

Q7. Docker mein Port Mapping 3308:3306 ka kya meaning hai?

Student:

Iska meaning hai:

```
Host Machine Port : Container Port
```

```
3308 : 3306
```

- Host machine par MariaDB port 3308 par accessible hoga.
- Container ke andar MariaDB port 3306 par run karega.

Port 3308 kyun use kiya?

Video creator ke system mein MySQL already port 3306 par running tha.

Isliye port conflict avoid karne ke liye host port 3308 use kiya.

Example:

```
MySQL    → localhost:3306
MariaDB  → localhost:3308
```

Q8. Environment Variables kya hote hain?

Student:

Environment Variables ka use application ya container ki configuration provide karne ke liye hota hai.

MariaDB Docker image mein kuch important environment variables hain:

| Variable                            | Purpose                             |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- |
| `MARIADB_ROOT_PASSWORD`             | Root user ka password               |
| `MARIADB_ALLOW_EMPTY_ROOT_PASSWORD` | Empty root password allow karna     |
| `MARIADB_RANDOM_ROOT_PASSWORD`      | Random root password generate karna |

Example:

```
-e MARIADB_ROOT_PASSWORD=root1234
```

Isse root user ka password set hota hai.

Q9. Kaise check karenge ki MariaDB Container running hai?

Student:

Following command execute karenge:

```
docker ps
```

Isse running containers ki list mil jayegi.

Agar container stop ho gaya hai toh:

```
docker ps -a
```

Container logs check karne ke liye:

```
docker logs mariadb1
```

Uske baad Test Connection click karenge.

Connection successful hone ke baad Finish click karenge.

Q12. Kaise check karenge ki MariaDB Vector support karta hai?

DBeaver mein table create karte time column datatype check karenge.

    id INT PRIMARY KEY,

Yahan:

- `VECTOR(3)` – 3 dimensions ka vector.

Actual Spring AI implementation mein embedding dimensions 1536 use kiye jayenge.

Q17. Database mein kaunsa data store karenge?

Java is a programming language.

```

Ye data initially Java List ke andar rakha jayega.

Q18. Helper Class kya hoti hai aur kyun banayi gayi?

Student:

Helper Class ek utility class hoti hai jo reusable methods aur common operations perform karne mein help karti hai.

Video mein `Helper` class create ki gayi hai.

Iske andar `getData()` method banaya gaya hai.

Example:

```
public class Helper {

    public static List<String> getData() {

        return List.of(
            "Java is a programming language.",
            "Spring Boot is used to develop Java applications.",
            "Java supports object-oriented programming.",
            "MySQL is a relational database.",
            "Kafka is used for messaging."
        );
    }
}
```

Explanation:

- `static` – Object create kiye bina method call kar sakte hain.
- `List<String>` – String values ki list return karega.
- `getData()` – Sample data return karega.

Part 6 – VectorStore Interface

Q20. Spring AI mein VectorStore kya hota hai?

Student:

VectorStore Spring AI ka interface hai jo Vector Database mein documents manage aur query karne ke operations define karta hai.

Iski help se hum:

1. Documents add kar sakte hain.
2. Documents delete kar sakte hain.
3. Similarity Search perform kar sakte hain.
4. Relevant documents retrieve kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
private final VectorStore vectorStore;
```

Spring AI auto-configuration ki help se MariaDB VectorStore ka implementation provide kar sakta hai.

Q21. VectorStore mein kaun-kaun se important methods hote hain?

Student:

Video mein kuch important methods explain kiye gaye hain:

| Method               | Purpose                        |
| -------------------- | ------------------------------ |
| `add()`              | Documents store karna          |
| `delete()`           | Documents delete karna         |
| `similaritySearch()` | Similar documents search karna |

Q22. add() method ka use kya hai?

Student:

`add()` method ka use documents ko Vector Database mein insert karne ke liye hota hai.

Example:

```
vectorStore.add(documents);
```

Yahan `documents` ek `List<Document>` hai.

Q23. similaritySearch() kya karta hai?

Student:

`similaritySearch()` user ke query ke according similar documents search karta hai.

Example:

```
List<Document> documents =
        vectorStore.similaritySearch("What is Java?");
```

Ye relevant documents return karta hai.

Iska practical use hum next stage mein RAG implementation ke time karenge.

Part 7 – String to Document Conversion

Q24. Spring AI mein Document kya hota hai?

Student:

Spring AI ka Document ek object hai jo text content aur metadata represent karta hai.

Simple String ko Document object mein convert karke VectorStore mein store kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
Document document =
        new Document("Java is a programming language.");
```

Document ke andar content aur metadata hota hai.

Q25. List of String ko List of Document mein kaise convert karenge?

Student:

Java Stream API ka use karenge.

Example:

```
List<String> data = Helper.getData();

List<Document> documents = data.stream()
        .map(Document::new)
        .toList();
```

Step-by-step explanation:

1. `data.stream()` – List ko Stream mein convert karta hai.
2. `map()` – Har String element par operation perform karta hai.
3. `Document::new` – Har String se Document object create karta hai.
4. `toList()` – Converted elements ko List mein collect karta hai.

Example:

Input:

```
List<String> data = List.of(
    "Java is a programming language.",
    "Spring Boot is a Java framework."
);
```

Output conceptually:

```
List<Document>
    |
    |-- Document("Java is a programming language.")
    |
    |-- Document("Spring Boot is a Java framework.")
```

Q26. Method Reference `Document::new` kya hai?

Student:

`Document::new` Java ka Constructor Reference hai.

Ye lambda expression ka short form hai.

Normal Lambda:

```
.map(text -> new Document(text))
```

Constructor Reference:

```
.map(Document::new)
```

Dono ka purpose same hai.

Part 8 – Saving Data into Vector Database

Q27. Data ko Vector Database mein save karne ke liye service method kaise banayenge?

Student:

Video mein creator `saveData()` method create karte hain.

Example:

```
@Service
public class ChatService {

    private final VectorStore vectorStore;

    public ChatService(VectorStore vectorStore) {
        this.vectorStore = vectorStore;
    }

    public void saveData(List<String> data) {

        List<Document> documents = data.stream()
                .map(Document::new)
                .toList();

        vectorStore.add(documents);
    }
}
```

Explanation:

1. `VectorStore` inject kiya.
2. `saveData()` method create kiya.
3. String list receive ki.
4. Stream API se Document list banayi.
5. `vectorStore.add()` se database mein documents insert kiye.

Q28. VectorStore ko manually create karna padta hai kya?

Student:

Nahi.

Spring AI auto-configuration ki help se VectorStore bean provide kar sakta hai.

Constructor injection se use kar sakte hain.

```
public ChatService(VectorStore vectorStore) {
    this.vectorStore = vectorStore;
}
```

Q29. Data insert karne ke liye Test Class ka use kyun kiya gaya?

Student:

Video mein creator data insert karne ke liye Spring Boot Test ka use karte hain.

Reason:

- Test method directly run kar sakte hain.
- Spring Application Context load hota hai.
- Service method execute kar sakte hain.
- Database mein sample data insert kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
@SpringBootTest
class ChatServiceTest {

    @Autowired
    private ChatService chatService;

    @Test
    void saveDataToVectorDatabase() {

        chatService.saveData(Helper.getData());

        System.out.println("Data saved successfully");
    }
}
```

Q30. `@SpringBootTest` kya karta hai?

Student:

`@SpringBootTest` Spring Boot Application Context load karta hai.

Iski help se test ke andar:

- Spring Beans inject kar sakte hain.
- Services access kar sakte hain.
- Repositories access kar sakte hain.
- Application configuration test kar sakte hain.

Part 9 – MariaDB Configuration

Q31. First time data insert karte waqt error kyun aaya?

Student:

Video mein first attempt par SQL error aaya.

Reason:

MariaDB ke liye proper database configuration nahi ki gayi thi.

Application mein pehle MySQL/JDBC configuration available thi.

Lekin ab humein MariaDB ke according datasource URL configure karna tha.

Q32. MariaDB Datasource URL kaise configure karenge?

Student:

`application.properties` file mein following configuration karenge.

```
spring.datasource.url=jdbc:mariadb://localhost:3308/spring_ai
spring.datasource.username=root
spring.datasource.password=root1234
```

Explanation:

| Property                     | Purpose                 |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| `spring.datasource.url`      | Database connection URL |
| `spring.datasource.username` | Database username       |
| `spring.datasource.password` | Database password       |

URL structure:

```
jdbc:mariadb://HOST:PORT/DATABASE_NAME
```

Q33. MariaDB Vector Store Schema Initialize kaise karenge?

Student:

Spring AI MariaDB Vector Store ko apna required database schema create karna hota hai.

Iske liye following property use karenge:

```
spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.initialize-schema=true
```

Meaning:

Application start hone par MariaDB Vector Store ka required schema initialize kiya jayega.

Q34. Distance Type kya hota hai?

Student:

Vector Database mein similarity calculate karne ke liye distance metrics use kiye jaate hain.

Video mein creator Cosine Distance use karte hain.

Common distance metrics:

1. Cosine Distance
2. Euclidean Distance

Cosine Distance:

Do vectors ke beech direction similarity measure karne ke liye use hota hai.

Example:

```
Vector A = [1, 2, 3]
Vector B = [1, 2, 4]
```

Cosine-based comparison se pata chalega ki dono vectors kitne similar hain.

Q35. Embedding Dimensions kya hoti hain?

Student:

Embedding Dimensions ka meaning hai vector ke andar kitne numerical values hain.

Example:

```
[0.12, 0.45, 0.78]
```

Is vector mein 3 dimensions hain.

Video mein creator 1536 dimensions configure karte hain.

```
spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.distance-type=COSINE
spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.dimensions=1536
```

Important:

Embedding Model aur Vector Database ke dimensions match hone chahiye.

Agar embedding model 1536-dimensional vectors generate karta hai, toh database mein bhi 1536 dimensions configure karna chahiye.

spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.initialize-schema=true
```

Ab application start karne par Spring AI required Vector Store table initialize kar sakta hai.

vector_store
```

Q37. Vector Store table mein kaun-kaun se columns create hote hain?

Video mein creator database ke andar following columns show karte hain:

| Column    | Data Type | Purpose                         |
| --------- | --------- | ------------------------------- |
| id        | UUID      | Document ki unique identity     |
| content   | TEXT      | Original text content           |
| metadata  | LONGTEXT  | Additional document information |
| embedding | VECTOR    | Numerical embedding             |

Q38. Metadata kya hota hai?

Student:

Metadata additional information hoti hai jo document ke saath store hoti hai.

Example:

```
{
  "category": "Java",
  "source": "java-notes.txt"
}
```

Isse document ke baare mein extra information milti hai.

Metadata filtering jaise use cases mein helpful hota hai.

Q39. Embedding column ka datatype VECTOR kyun hai?

Student:

Embedding numerical values ka collection hota hai.

Isliye MariaDB mein embedding ko `VECTOR` datatype ke andar store kiya jata hai.

Example:

```
[0.12, 0.45, 0.78, 0.91, ...]
```

Video mein creator batate hain ki configured dimensions 1536 hain.

Part 11 – Final Data Insertion and Verification

Q40. Database mein data successfully insert hua ya nahi kaise check karenge?

Student:

Following steps follow karenge:

1. Spring Boot application start karenge.
2. Schema initialize hoga.
3. Test method run karenge.
4. `saveData()` method execute hoga.
5. String data Document objects mein convert hoga.
6. `vectorStore.add()` call hoga.

DBeaver open karke `vector_store` table refresh karenge.

Q41. Database mein embeddings kaise generate hote hain?

Flow:

```
Original Text
     |
     v
Document Object
     |
     v
Embedding Model
     |
     v
Numerical Vector
     |
     v
MariaDB Vector Store
```

Original text, metadata aur embedding database mein store hote hain.

Q42. Kya embeddings normal text ki tarah readable hoti hain?

Student:

Nahi.

Embeddings numerical vectors hote hain.

Example:

```
[0.023, 0.145, -0.321, 0.456, ...]
```

Ye normal human-readable sentences nahi hote.

Ye text ka mathematical representation hota hai.

Q43. Kya hum multiple programming languages ka data store kar sakte hain?

Haan.

Example:

Java is an object-oriented programming language.

Python is a high-level programming language.

JavaScript is used for web development.

Spring Boot is used to build Java applications.
```

Ye sab documents Vector Database mein store kiye ja sakte hain.

Part 13 – Important Interview Questions

Q46. What is a Vector Database?

A Vector Database stores numerical vector representations of data called embeddings and supports similarity search to retrieve relevant information.

Q47. What is an Embedding?

Embedding is a numerical representation of text or other data that captures its semantic meaning.

Q48. What is the purpose of Spring AI VectorStore?

VectorStore provides APIs to add, delete and search documents in a Vector Database.

Q49. What is Similarity Search?

Similarity Search finds documents whose vector representations are closest to the query vector.

Q50. What is the purpose of `vectorStore.add()`?

It adds a collection of documents to the configured Vector Database.

Q51. What is the purpose of `similaritySearch()`?

It retrieves documents that are semantically similar to the given query.

Q52. Why do we use Document objects in Spring AI?

Document objects represent text content along with metadata and can be processed by Spring AI components.

Q53. Why do embedding dimensions need to match?

The database vector column must be compatible with the dimensionality of vectors generated by the embedding model.

Q54. What is the purpose of `@SpringBootTest`?

It loads the Spring Boot application context for integration testing.

Q55. Why do we use MariaDB Java Client?

It provides JDBC connectivity between a Java application and MariaDB.

Q56. What is Cosine Distance?

Cosine Distance is a vector distance metric used to measure how different two vector directions are.

Q57. What is Metadata in Spring AI Document?

Metadata contains additional information associated with a document, such as its source, category or other attributes.

========================================================================================================================
SPRING AI RAG IMPLEMENTATION WITH OLLAMA + MARIADB VECTOR STORE
Hinglish Self-Study / Re-implementation Notes
===============================================================

1. PROJECT GOAL
---------------
Is project ka goal ek simple RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) application banana hai.

Application flow:
1. Company-related text documents prepare/receive hote hain.
2. Spring AI embedding model har document ka vector banata hai.
3. Spring AI VectorStore original content, metadata aur embeddings MariaDB mein store karta hai.
4. User question poochta hai.
5. VectorStore question se similar documents search karta hai.
6. Retrieved documents ka text context banaya jata hai.
7. Context aur question Ollama chat model ko bheje jate hain.
8. Chat model answer generate karta hai.
9. API answer aur retrieved source documents return karti hai.

Models:
- Chat model: codellama:latest
- Embedding model: mxbai-embed-large:latest

Database: MariaDB
Backend: Spring Boot + Spring AI
RAG style: Manual RAG (similarity search + manually constructed prompt)


2. HIGH-LEVEL ARCHITECTURE
--------------------------

POST /api/rag/ingest
    |
    v
RagController
    |
    v
RagService.ingestDocuments()
    |
    v
String documents -> Spring AI Document objects
    |
    v
VectorStore.add(documents)
    |
    +--> Embedding model (Ollama mxbai-embed-large)
    |        |
    |        v
    |     Embedding vectors
    |
    v
MariaDB vector_store table


POST /api/rag/ask
    |
    v
RagController
    |
    v
RagService.askQuestion(question)
    |
    v
VectorStore.similaritySearch(question)
    |
    v
Top 4 relevant documents from MariaDB
    |
    v
Retrieved document text -> Context
    |
    v
ChatClient (Ollama codellama)
    |
    v
Generated answer
    |
    v
AskResponse (question + answer + source documents)


3. TECHNOLOGY / VERSION DETAILS
-------------------------------

From the supplied project configuration:
- Java: 17
- Spring Boot: 4.1.2
- Spring AI: 2.0.1
- Ollama: running inside Docker
- Chat model: codellama:latest
- Embedding model: mxbai-embed-large:latest
- MariaDB JDBC driver: mariadb-java-client
- MariaDB database: springai
- Application port: 8081
- Ollama API port: 11434
- MariaDB host port: 3308

Ollama models:
- codellama:latest -> generates natural-language answers.
- mxbai-embed-large:latest -> converts text into numerical embeddings.

Embedding dimensions:
- mxbai-embed-large -> 1024 dimensions.
- MariaDB vector column and Spring AI vector-store dimensions must also be 1024.

Do not confuse this with nomic-embed-text, which has a different embedding dimension.


4. MAVEN DEPENDENCIES
---------------------

Core dependencies for this manual implementation:

A. spring-boot-starter-webmvc
   REST APIs/controllers ke liye.

B. spring-ai-starter-model-ollama
   Ollama chat aur embedding models integrate karne ke liye.

C. spring-ai-starter-vector-store-mariadb
   MariaDB vector store mein documents add/search karne ke liye.

D. spring-boot-starter-jdbc
   JdbcTemplate aur JDBC auto-configuration ke liye.
   MariaDB Vector Store ko JdbcTemplate chahiye.

E. mariadb-java-client
   Java application aur MariaDB ke beech JDBC connectivity ke liye.

Spring AI BOM:
- spring-ai.version = 2.0.1
- Spring AI dependencies ko same BOM se manage karo.

IMPORTANT DEPENDENCY CLEANUP:
Supplied pom.xml mein ye extra dependencies bhi thi:
- spring-ai-vector-store-advisor
- spring-ai-rag
- spring-ai-advisors-vector-store with explicit version 2.0.0-M8

Current code manually similarity search karta hai aur prompt banata hai. Ye Advisor/RAG modules use nahi karta. Is manual implementation ke liye ye extra dependencies required nahi hain. Especially Spring AI 2.0.1 BOM ke saath older 2.0.0-M8 dependency mix mat karo. Advisor-based RAG implement karoge tab compatible version ka relevant module add karna.

Clean core dependency section:

<dependencies>

        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>

    <dependency>

dependencyManagement mein spring-ai-bom import karo:
- groupId: org.springframework.ai
- artifactId: spring-ai-bom
- version: ${spring-ai.version}
- type: pom
- scope: import

Supplied project versions: Spring Boot 4.1.2, Java 17, Spring AI 2.0.1. Apne actual project mein versions aligned rakho.


5. APPLICATION.PROPERTIES
-------------------------

Configuration based on the supplied implementation:

spring.application.name=spring_ai_rag_app
server.port=8081

# Ollama connection
spring.ai.ollama.base-url=http://localhost:11434

# Chat model
spring.ai.ollama.chat.options.model=codellama:latest
spring.ai.ollama.chat.options.temperature=0.7

# MariaDB connection
spring.datasource.url=jdbc:mariadb://localhost:3308/springai
spring.datasource.username=root
spring.datasource.password=root
spring.datasource.driver-class-name=org.mariadb.jdbc.Driver

# Embedding model
spring.ai.ollama.embedding.model=mxbai-embed-large

# MariaDB Vector Store
spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.initialize-schema=true
spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.distance-type=COSINE
spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.dimensions=1024

Properties explanation:
- base-url: Ollama server URL accessible from Spring Boot.
- chat.options.model: LLM that generates the final answer.
- chat.options.temperature: controls response randomness.
- datasource.url: MariaDB host, port and database.
- embedding.model: model used to create embeddings.
- initialize-schema=true: Spring AI initializes vector-store schema.
- distance-type=COSINE: cosine distance is used for similarity comparison.
- dimensions=1024: must match embedding model output and database vector column.

Docker networking:
The supplied properties use localhost:11434. This works when the Ollama Docker container publishes port 11434 to the host and Spring Boot runs on the host. If Spring Boot also runs inside Docker, localhost refers to the Spring Boot container itself; use the Ollama container/service hostname on the shared Docker network.

The password above reproduces the user's supplied properties. Use the actual password configured in your environment.


6. DTO CLASSES
---------------

A. AskRequest.java

package com.example.dto;

public record AskRequest(String question) {
}

Purpose:
- /ask API se user question receive karta hai.
- Example JSON:
  {
    "question": "Which database does the company use?"
  }


B. AskResponse.java

package com.example.dto;

import java.util.List;

public record AskResponse(
        String question,
        String answer,
        List<String> retrievedDocuments
) {
}

Purpose:
- Original question return karta hai.
- LLM-generated answer return karta hai.
- Retrieved source documents ki list return karta hai.


C. IngestRequest.java

package com.example.dto;

import java.util.List;

public record IngestRequest(List<String> documents) {
}

Purpose:
- Documents ki list represent karta hai.
- Current controller mein request object internally create hota hai; endpoint request body se documents receive nahi karta.
- Agar future mein Postman/client se documents bhejne hain, controller ko @RequestBody IngestRequest request accept karna hoga.


7. CONTROLLER
--------------

File: RagController.java

package com.example.controller;

import com.example.dto.AskRequest;
import com.example.dto.AskResponse;
import com.example.dto.IngestRequest;
import com.example.service.RagService;

import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/rag")
public class RagController {

    private final RagService ragService;

    public RagController(RagService ragService) {
        this.ragService = ragService;
    }

    // API 1: Ingest documents
    @PostMapping("/ingest")
    public Map<String, Object> ingest() {

        List<String> documents = List.of(
                "Our company name is ABC Technologies.",
                "Our company uses Java as its primary programming language.",
                "We use Spring Boot to develop backend microservices.",
                "Our primary relational database is MySQL.",
                "We use Apache Kafka for asynchronous communication between microservices.",
                "Our backend applications follow microservices architecture.",
                "We use Redis for caching frequently accessed data.",
                "Docker is used to containerize our applications.",
                "Kubernetes is used for container orchestration.",
                "Our applications are deployed on AWS cloud infrastructure."
        );

        IngestRequest request = new IngestRequest(documents);

        int count = ragService.ingestDocuments(request.documents());

        return Map.of(
                "message", "Documents stored successfully",
                "totalDocuments", count
        );
    }

    // API 2: Ask question
    @PostMapping("/ask")
    public AskResponse ask(@RequestBody AskRequest request) {
        return ragService.askQuestion(request.question());
    }
}

Controller explanation:

@RestController:
- Class REST API endpoints expose karta hai.
- Returned Java objects JSON response mein convert hote hain.

@RequestMapping("/api/rag"):
- Dono endpoints ka common URL prefix.

@PostMapping("/ingest"):
- Hardcoded company documents ingest karta hai.
- IngestRequest object create karke documents service ko deta hai.
- Service se inserted document count receive karta hai.
- Success message aur count return karta hai.

@PostMapping("/ask"):
- @RequestBody JSON ko AskRequest record mein map karta hai.
- Question RagService ko pass karta hai.
- AskResponse return karta hai.

IMPORTANT:
Current /ingest method mein @RequestBody nahi hai. Documents Java code mein hardcoded hain. Isliye Postman se request body bhejne par woh use nahi hogi. IngestRequest DTO abhi internally use ho raha hai.


8. SERVICE CLASS - CORE RAG LOGIC
---------------------------------

File: RagService.java

package com.example.service;

import com.example.dto.AskResponse;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import org.springframework.ai.chat.client.ChatClient;
import org.springframework.ai.document.Document;
import org.springframework.ai.vectorstore.SearchRequest;
import org.springframework.ai.vectorstore.VectorStore;

import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Service
public class RagService {

    private static final Logger log =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(RagService.class);

    private final VectorStore vectorStore;
    private final ChatClient chatClient;

    public RagService(
            VectorStore vectorStore,
            ChatClient.Builder chatClientBuilder) {

        this.vectorStore = vectorStore;
        this.chatClient = chatClientBuilder.build();
    }

    // STEP 1: Store documents in MariaDB Vector Store
    public int ingestDocuments(List<String> data) {

        log.info("Starting document ingestion...");

        // Convert String into Document objects
        List<Document> documents = data.stream()
                .map(text -> new Document(
                        text,
                        Map.of("source", "company-documents")
                ))
                .toList();

        log.info("Total documents: {}", documents.size());

        // Generate embeddings and store documents
        vectorStore.add(documents);

        log.info("Documents successfully stored!");

        return documents.size();
    }

    // STEP 2: Search relevant documents and generate answer
    public AskResponse askQuestion(String question) {

        log.info("User question: {}", question);

        // Search similar documents from MariaDB
        List<Document> retrievedDocuments =
                vectorStore.similaritySearch(
                        SearchRequest.builder()
                                .query(question)
                                .topK(4)
                                .build()
                );

        log.info(
                "Retrieved documents: {}",
                retrievedDocuments.size()
        );

        // If no relevant documents found
        if (retrievedDocuments.isEmpty()) {
            return new AskResponse(
                    question,
                    "I don't have relevant information in my documents.",
                    List.of()
            );
        }

        // Convert retrieved documents into context
        String context = retrievedDocuments.stream()
                .map(Document::getText)
                .reduce((a, b) -> a + "\n\n" + b)
                .orElse("");

        log.info("Retrieved Context:\n{}", context);

        // Send context + question to Ollama
        String answer = chatClient.prompt()

                .system("""
                        You are a company knowledge assistant.

                        Answer the user's question ONLY using
                        the provided context.

                        Rules:
                        1. Do not use outside knowledge.
                        2. Do not invent information.
                        3. If the answer is not available in
                           the context, say:
                           "I don't know based on the provided documents."
                        4. Give clear and simple answers.
                        """)

                .user("""
                        Context:
                        %s

                        Question:
                        %s
                        """.formatted(context, question))

                .call()
                .content();

        log.info("Generated Answer: {}", answer);

        // Return answer + retrieved documents
        List<String> sources = retrievedDocuments.stream()
                .map(Document::getText)
                .toList();

        return new AskResponse(
                question,
                answer,
                sources
        );
    }
}


9. RAGSERVICE - LINE-BY-LINE CONCEPT
------------------------------------

A. Dependency injection

private final VectorStore vectorStore;
private final ChatClient chatClient;

VectorStore:
- Spring AI abstraction hai.
- Documents add karne aur similarity search karne ke liye use hota hai.
- Is project mein MariaDBVectorStore implementation auto-configure hoti hai.

ChatClient:
- Chat model ko prompt bhejne aur response lene ka fluent API.
- Is project mein Ollama chat model use hota hai.

Constructor:
- Spring VectorStore bean inject karta hai.
- ChatClient.Builder inject karta hai.
- Builder se ChatClient build hota hai.


B. Ingestion process

Method:
ingestDocuments(List<String> data)

Step 1:
Input String documents receive hote hain.

Step 2:
Stream ke through har String ko Spring AI Document object mein convert karte hain.

new Document(
    text,
    Map.of("source", "company-documents")
)

Document ke parts:
- Text/content: actual document information.
- Metadata: additional information, jaise source name.

Step 3:
vectorStore.add(documents) call hota hai.

Behind the scenes:
1. Spring AI har Document ke text ko embedding model ko deta hai.
2. Ollama mxbai-embed-large embedding generate karta hai.
3. Embedding vector aur document content/metadata vector store ko diye jate hain.
4. MariaDB vector_store table mein records save hote hain.

Step 4:
Method documents.size() return karta hai.

Note:
Returned count input list ka size hai. Ye database mein independently verify kiya gaya row count nahi hai.


C. Retrieval process

Code:
vectorStore.similaritySearch(
    SearchRequest.builder()
        .query(question)
        .topK(4)
        .build()
);

Meaning:
- query(question): user question ke basis par similar documents search.
- topK(4): maximum 4 matching documents return karne ki request.
- similaritySearch: vector store se relevant documents retrieve.

Important:
Top 4 ka matlab maximum four results; zaroori nahi har query par exactly four results milen.


D. Empty result handling

if (retrievedDocuments.isEmpty()) {
    return new AskResponse(
        question,
        "I don't have relevant information in my documents.",
        List.of()
    );
}

Agar vector store se koi document retrieve nahi hota, toh LLM call nahi hota. Application fallback response return karti hai.


E. Context building

String context = retrievedDocuments.stream()
        .map(Document::getText)
        .reduce((a, b) -> a + "\n\n" + b)
        .orElse("");

Meaning:
- Har retrieved Document ka text nikala jata hai.
- Documents ko double newline se join kiya jata hai.
- Combined string LLM prompt ka context banti hai.


F. Prompt construction

.system(...):
- Model ko role aur rules deta hai.
- Is example mein model company knowledge assistant hai.
- Answer only provided context se dena hai.
- Outside knowledge/invention avoid karna hai.
- Context mein answer na ho toh "I don't know..." bolna hai.

.user(...):
- Actual retrieved context aur user question send karta hai.

.call():
- Chat model ko request execute karta hai.

.content():
- Generated response ka text receive karta hai.


G. Response construction

AskResponse contains:
1. question
2. answer
3. retrievedDocuments

Isse API caller ko answer ke saath source context bhi milta hai.


10. POSTMAN TESTING
-------------------

A. Ingest API

Method: POST
URL: http://localhost:8081/api/rag/ingest

Current implementation:
- Request body required nahi hai.
- Controller ke andar 10 company documents hardcoded hain.

Expected response shape:

{
  "message": "Documents stored successfully",
  "totalDocuments": 10
}

B. Ask API

Method: POST
URL: http://localhost:8081/api/rag/ask

Header:
Content-Type: application/json

Body -> raw -> JSON:

{
  "question": "Which programming language does our company use?"
}

Expected response shape:

{
  "question": "Which programming language does our company use?",
  "answer": "Our company uses Java as its primary programming language.",
  "retrievedDocuments": [
    "Our company uses Java as its primary programming language."
  ]
}

Exact answer and retrieved documents similarity search/model output par depend karte hain. Upar illustrative response hai.


11. DATABASE CHECKS
-------------------

Connect to configured database:
- Host: localhost
- Port: 3308
- Database: springai
- Username: root
- Password: configured password

Check tables:
SHOW TABLES;

Check vector table structure:
DESCRIBE vector_store;

Check table DDL:
SHOW CREATE TABLE vector_store;

Check stored rows:
SELECT id, content, metadata
FROM vector_store;

For mxbai-embed-large, expected vector dimension is 1024. Vector column should be compatible:

embedding VECTOR(1024)

IMPORTANT DIMENSION MISMATCH FOUND DURING THIS PROJECT:
Earlier, the supplied MariaDB table structure showed VECTOR(768), while the selected embedding model is mxbai-embed-large (1024 dimensions). These settings do not match.

Before ingesting with mxbai-embed-large:
- spring.ai.vectorstore.mariadb.dimensions must be 1024.
- MariaDB embedding column must be VECTOR(1024).
- Existing vectors from a 768-dimensional model cannot be reused as 1024-dimensional vectors.

If the table already contains important data, take a backup and follow the appropriate schema migration/recreation process. Do not drop a table with important data without backup.


12. TROUBLESHOOTING HISTORY
---------------------------

Issue 1: Ollama model not found

Error:
HTTP 404 - model 'mxbai-embed-large' not found

Cause:
Ollama could not find the configured embedding model.

Check models inside the Ollama environment/container:
ollama list

If missing:
ollama pull mxbai-embed-large

The user's Docker Ollama model list later showed:
- mxbai-embed-large:latest
- codellama:latest


Issue 2: JdbcTemplate bean missing

Error:
No qualifying bean of type JdbcTemplate available.

Fix:
Add Spring Boot JDBC starter:

<dependency>
    <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
    <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-jdbc</artifactId>
</dependency>

Keep MariaDB JDBC driver dependency too.


Issue 3: MariaDB vector insert error

Error:
Incorrect vector value for column springai.vector_store.embedding

Observed details:
- MariaDB version: 13.0.2-MariaDB-ubu2604
- JDBC driver shown in stack trace: mariadb-java-client 3.5.10
- Existing table: embedding VECTOR(768)
- Configured embedding model: mxbai-embed-large
- mxbai-embed-large dimension: 1024

First investigate and align vector dimensions/schema.

A JDBC URL option was suggested as a diagnostic experiment:
jdbc:mariadb://localhost:3308/springai?useBulkStmts=false

This option is not a confirmed universal fix. If the same error persists after dimensions/schema are aligned, investigate the exact MariaDB JDBC driver and Spring AI MariaDB Vector Store compatibility for the versions being used. Do not assume the bulk-statement option alone solves every vector binding problem.
