14. DESIGN NOTES / LIMITATIONS
------------------------------

1. Current ingestion data is hardcoded in RagController.
   Real application mein documents request body, files, database records, ya another source se aa sakte hain.

2. IngestRequest currently internally create hota hai.
   It is not currently used as @RequestBody.

3. Current RAG manual retrieval + prompt construction use karta hai.
   It does not use RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor or QuestionAnswerAdvisor.

4. topK(4) up to four relevant documents retrieve karta hai.
   Ye guarantee nahi karta ki retrieved documents mein answer zaroor ho.

5. System prompt model ko context-only answer dene ko kehta hai, lekin LLM phir bhi mistakes kar sakta hai. Missing-information questions se test karo.

6. Example one input String ko one Document banata hai.
   Ismein PDF parsing, file upload, text splitting/chunking, chunk overlap, document update/delete, authentication ya access control implement nahi hai.

7. Large documents ke liye chunking strategy ki zaroorat ho sakti hai. Ye sample basic RAG demonstration hai, production ingestion pipeline nahi.

8. Metadata currently:
   source = company-documents

9. Document IDs explicitly assign nahi kiye gaye. Repeated ingestion par update/duplicate behavior ko assume mat karo; actual vector-store ID behavior verify karo.


15. QUICK REVISION - INTERVIEW STYLE
------------------------------------

Q1. What is RAG?
RAG means Retrieval-Augmented Generation. Pehle knowledge source se relevant information retrieve hoti hai, phir woh information LLM ko answer generate karne ke liye di jati hai.

Q2. Why do we need embeddings?
Embeddings text ko numerical vectors mein convert karte hain, jisse semantically similar text search kiya ja sakta hai.

Q3. What is VectorStore?
Spring AI VectorStore ek abstraction hai jo document add karne aur embeddings ke basis par similarity search karne ke liye use hota hai.

Q4. Why are Chat Model and Embedding Model separate?
Embedding model text ko vectors mein convert karta hai. Chat model prompt/context ko use karke natural-language answer generate karta hai.

Q5. What does topK(4) mean?
Vector store se maximum four similar documents retrieve karne ki request.

Q6. Why do we build context?
Retrieved document text ko combine karke LLM ko diya jata hai, taaki woh stored knowledge ke basis par answer de.

Q7. What is metadata?
Metadata Document ke saath attached additional information hai, jaise source, category ya document identifier.

Q8. What is the purpose of the system prompt?
System prompt model ka role aur response rules define karta hai, jaise supplied context se hi answer dena.

Q9. What happens if no documents are retrieved?
Is implementation mein fallback AskResponse return hota hai aur chat model call nahi hota.

Q10. Why must vector dimensions match?
Vector column ko embedding model ke output ke same number of dimensions wale vectors store karne hote hain.

END OF NOTES
========================================================================================================================
Bilkul bhai! Ab hum is official documentation ko properly study notes mein convert karenge. Main sirf aapke diye hue Spring AI documentation ko base bana raha hoon. Koi external video ya document refer nahi karunga.

Is baar hum Theory + Practical Code + Line-by-Line Explanation + Real-life Examples + Interview Questions cover karenge. Koshish rahegi ki aap ye notes padhkar topic independently samajh aur implement kar sako.

# Spring AI – Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG)

Chapter: Advisors – QuestionAnswerAdvisor | Complete Hinglish Study Notes

# PART 1 – Introduction to RAG

## 1. Retrieval Augmented Generation kya hai?

Teacher: Sabse pehle batao, RAG ka full form kya hai?

Student: Sir, RAG ka full form hai Retrieval Augmented Generation.

Teacher: Very good! Ab batao, RAG ki zarurat kyu padti hai?

Student: Sir, Large Language Models (LLMs) ki kuch limitations hoti hain. For example:

1. Long-form content ko handle karne mein difficulty ho sakti hai.
2. Factual accuracy maintain karne mein problem ho sakti hai.
3. User ke specific context ko properly understand karna difficult ho sakta hai.

In limitations ko overcome karne ke liye RAG technique use hoti hai.

Teacher: Ek practical example se samjhao.

Student: Suppose hum ek company ke liye AI chatbot bana rahe hain.

Company ke paas apne private documents hain:

- Employee policies.
- Leave policies.
- Project documentation.
- Internal technical documents.
- Company-related information.

Ab humara LLM automatically company ke private documents nahi jaanta.

Toh hum RAG use karenge. RAG relevant documents retrieve karega aur unka context LLM ko provide karega.

### RAG ka basic working flow

Company Documents

Private / External Information

Vector Database

Documents stored for retrieval

User Question

Retrieval

Relevant documents search karna

LLM + Retrieved Context

Final Answer

Teacher: RAG ke three words ka meaning batao.

Student:

- Retrieval: Relevant information search karna.
- Augmented: User question ke saath additional context add karna.
- Generation: LLM se final answer generate karwana.

## 2. Spring AI RAG ko kaise support karta hai?

Teacher: Kya Spring AI mein RAG implement karne ke liye humein sab kuch manually develop karna padta hai?

Student: Nahi sir! Spring AI RAG implement karne ke liye Modular Architecture provide karta hai.

Iski help se hum do approaches use kar sakte hain:

1\. Custom RAG Flow

Apni requirement ke according khud RAG workflow develop kar sakte hain.

2\. Out-of-the-box RAG Flow

Spring AI ke ready-made components aur Advisor API use kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Is documentation mein hum kis Advisor ko study karne wale hain?

Student: Sir, `QuestionAnswerAdvisor`.

# PART 2 – Advisors

## 3. Advisors kya hote hain?

Teacher: Spring AI mein Advisors ka use kyu karte hain?

Student: Advisors ki help se hum ChatClient ke request processing flow mein additional functionality add kar sakte hain.

RAG ke case mein Advisor Vector Database se relevant documents retrieve karne aur unka context LLM ko provide karne mein help karta hai.

Teacher: Kya QuestionAnswerAdvisor use karne ke liye dependency add karni padegi?

Student: Yes sir!

Documentation ke according, `QuestionAnswerAdvisor` aur `VectorStoreChatMemoryAdvisor` use karne ke liye ye Maven dependency add karni hoti hai.

### 3.1 Maven Dependency

```
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.springframework.ai</groupId>
    <artifactId>spring-ai-vector-store-advisor</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Line-by-line explanation:

| Configuration                    | Meaning                                     |
| -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- |
| `groupId`                        | Spring AI organization                      |
| `artifactId`                     | Advisor module ka naam                      |
| `spring-ai-vector-store-advisor` | Vector Store related Advisors ki dependency |

Teacher: Is dependency ka use kya hai?

Student: Is dependency ki help se hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor aur VectorStoreChatMemoryAdvisor jaise components use kar sakte hain.

# PART 3 – QuestionAnswerAdvisor

## 4. QuestionAnswerAdvisor kya hai?

Teacher: Ab hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor ko detail mein samajhte hain. Iska main purpose kya hai?

Student: Sir, QuestionAnswerAdvisor ek Spring AI Advisor hai jo user ke question ke according Vector Database mein relevant documents search karta hai.

Uske baad retrieved documents ko user ke text ke saath append karta hai, taaki AI model additional context use karke answer generate kar sake.

Teacher: Vector Database mein data kyu store karte hain?

Student: Sir, Vector Database mein aisa data store kar sakte hain jiske baare mein AI model ko pehle se information nahi hoti.

For example, company ke private documents.

Teacher: Kya QuestionAnswerAdvisor use karne se pehle documents VectorStore mein load hone chahiye?

Student: Yes sir! Documentation mein assume kiya gaya hai ki data already `VectorStore` mein loaded hai.

### 4.1 QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka working flow

User Question

QuestionAnswerAdvisor

Vector Database

Similarity Search

Retrieved Documents

User Question + Retrieved Context

AI Model

Final Response

Teacher: Is process mein similarity search kaun perform karta hai?

Student: QuestionAnswerAdvisor, VectorStore ki help se similarity search perform karta hai.

Teacher: Retrieved documents ka kya hota hai?

Student: Retrieved documents user ke text ke saath append hote hain aur LLM ko context provide karte hain.

## 5. Practical Implementation – QuestionAnswerAdvisor

Official documentation ka code:

```
ChatResponse response = ChatClient.builder(chatModel)
        .build()
        .prompt()
        .advisors(
            QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore).build()
        )
        .user(userText)
        .call()
        .chatResponse();
```

Ab hum is code ko ek-ek line samajhte hain.

### Step 1 – ChatClient.builder(chatModel)

```
ChatClient.builder(chatModel)
```

Teacher: Yahan `chatModel` kya hai?

Student: Sir, ye configured ChatModel hai jo AI model ke saath communication handle karta hai.

### Step 2 – build()

```
.build()
```

ChatClient ka instance create karta hai.

### Step 3 – prompt()

```
.prompt()
```

New prompt request prepare karne ke liye use hota hai.

### Step 4 – advisors()

```
.advisors(
    QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore).build()
)
```

Teacher: Is line mein kya ho raha hai?

Student: Sir, hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka instance create karke ChatClient request ke saath attach kar rahe hain.

Yahan:

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
```

Advisor ko VectorStore provide kar raha hai.

Aur:

```
.build()
```

Advisor ka instance create kar raha hai.

### Step 5 – user(userText)

```
.user(userText)
```

User ka actual question provide karta hai.

Example:

```
.user("What is Spring Boot?")
```

### Step 6 – call()

```
.call()
```

ChatClient request execute karta hai.

### Step 7 – chatResponse()

```
.chatResponse()
```

Complete `ChatResponse` object return karta hai.

Teacher: Ab humein manually similarity search likhna padega?

Student: Nahi sir! QuestionAnswerAdvisor automatically VectorStore mein similarity search perform karta hai.

Important learning: Is implementation mein hum directly Advisor attach kar rahe hain. Manual similarity search aur context append karne ka code humein khud nahi likhna padta.

# PART 4 – SearchRequest Customization

## 6. Default Similarity Search kaise work karti hai?

Teacher: Abhi jo QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka example dekha, usmein kaunse documents search hote hain?

Student: Documentation ke according, default example mein QuestionAnswerAdvisor Vector Database ke sabhi documents par similarity search perform karta hai.

Teacher: Suppose mujhe sirf specific type ke documents search karne hain. Kya possible hai?

Student: Yes sir! Iske liye hum `SearchRequest` aur Filter Expression use kar sakte hain.

Documentation ke according, Filter Expression SQL-like syntax use karta hai aur different VectorStores ke beech portable hai.

## 7. SearchRequest kya hai?

Teacher: SearchRequest ka kya use hai?

Student: SearchRequest ki help se hum VectorStore search ko customize kar sakte hain.

Is documentation mein do important configurations hain:

1. Similarity Threshold.
2. TopK.

### 7.1 TopK kya hai?

Teacher: TopK ka meaning kya hai?

Student: TopK define karta hai ki similarity search se maximum kitne documents retrieve karne hain.

Example:

```
.topK(6)
```

Iska meaning maximum 6 search results retrieve karna hai.

### 7.2 Similarity Threshold kya hai?

Teacher: Similarity Threshold kya define karta hai?

Student: Ye minimum similarity score define karta hai jiske basis par document search result mein qualify kar sakta hai.

Example:

```
.similarityThreshold(0.8d)
```

Iska meaning minimum similarity threshold `0.8` set karna hai.

Teacher: Agar threshold increase karenge toh kya hoga?

Student: Search condition stricter ho jayegi. Comparatively fewer documents qualify kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Aur threshold decrease karenge toh?

Student: Lower similarity score wale documents bhi qualify kar sakte hain.

### 7.3 TopK vs Similarity Threshold

| TopK                                     | Similarity Threshold                       |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ |
| Maximum document count control karta hai | Minimum similarity score control karta hai |
| Example: 6 documents                     | Example: 0.8                               |
| Quantity control                         | Relevance threshold control                |

## 8. Practical Code – Custom SearchRequest

Official documentation ka code:

```
var qaAdvisor = QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
        .searchRequest(
            SearchRequest.builder()
                .similarityThreshold(0.8d)
                .topK(6)
                .build()
        )
        .build();
```

Line-by-line explanation:

1\. QuestionAnswerAdvisor Builder

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
```

QuestionAnswerAdvisor create karne ke liye builder start karte hain.

2\. searchRequest()

```
.searchRequest(...)
```

Advisor ko search configuration provide karte hain.

3\. SearchRequest.builder()

```
SearchRequest.builder()
```

SearchRequest ka builder create karta hai.

4\. similarityThreshold()

```
.similarityThreshold(0.8d)
```

Minimum similarity threshold configure karta hai.

5\. topK()

```
.topK(6)
```

Maximum 6 documents retrieve karne ki configuration karta hai.

6\. build()

```
.build()
```

Pehle SearchRequest aur phir QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka instance create hota hai.

Teacher: Kya ye configuration associated ChatClient requests par apply hogi?

Student: Yes sir! Documentation ke according, Advisor create karte waqt configure ki gayi search settings us Advisor se associated ChatClient requests par apply hoti hain.

# PART 5 – Dynamic Filter Expressions

## 9. Filter Expression kya hai?

Teacher: Suppose VectorStore mein multiple types ke documents stored hain:

- Spring documents.
- Java documents.
- Hibernate documents.

Mujhe sirf Spring-related documents search karne hain. Kya karunga?

Student: Sir, hum Filter Expression use karenge.

Example:

```
"type == 'Spring'"
```

Iska meaning hai ki document ka metadata field `type` Spring hona chahiye.

Teacher: Kya filter expression ko fixed rakhna zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi sir! Documentation ke according hum filter expression ko runtime par bhi update kar sakte hain.

## 10. Static Filter vs Dynamic Filter

Static Filter: Advisor create karte waqt filter configure karna.

Dynamic Filter: Har individual request ke according runtime par filter provide karna.

| Static Filter                 | Dynamic Filter                       |
| ----------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| Advisor creation time par set | Request time par set                 |
| Fixed condition               | Request-specific condition           |
| Associated requests par apply | Individual request ke liye customize |

## 11. Dynamic Filter Expression – Practical Code

Documentation ka example:

```
ChatClient chatClient = ChatClient.builder(chatModel)
    .defaultAdvisors(
        QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
            .searchRequest(SearchRequest.builder().build())
            .build()
    )
    .build();
```

Teacher: Is code mein `defaultAdvisors()` ka kya purpose hai?

Student: ChatClient ke liye default Advisor configure karna.

Yahan hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor ko default Advisor ke roop mein attach kar rahe hain.

Ab runtime par filter expression update karte hain.

```
String content = chatClient.prompt()
    .user("Please answer my question XYZ")
    .advisors(a -> a.param(
        QuestionAnswerAdvisor.FILTER_EXPRESSION,
        "type == 'Spring'"
    ))
    .call()
    .content();
```

Step 1 – prompt()

New ChatClient request start karte hain.

Step 2 – user()

User ka question provide karte hain.

Step 3 – advisors()

```
.advisors(a -> a.param(...))
```

Current request ke liye Advisor context parameter provide karte hain.

Step 4 – FILTER_EXPRESSION

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor.FILTER_EXPRESSION
```

Ye QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka constant hai jo dynamic filter parameter identify karta hai.

Step 5 – Filter Value

```
"type == 'Spring'"
```

Sirf Spring type metadata wale documents search karne ke liye filter expression provide karta hai.

Step 6 – call().content()

Request execute hoti hai aur response ka text return hota hai.

Important: FILTER_EXPRESSION

Dynamic filtering ke liye ye constant use hota hai:

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor.FILTER_EXPRESSION
```

Is parameter ki help se runtime par search results ko metadata expression ke according filter kar sakte hain.

Code note: Official snippet mein ChatClient ko `chatClient` naam se declare kiya gaya hai. Isliye same instance ko call karte waqt `chatClient.prompt()` use karenge. Agar aapke code mein field ka naam alag hai, toh usi naam ka reference use karein.

# PART 6 – Custom Template

## 12. Default PromptTemplate kya hai?

Teacher: QuestionAnswerAdvisor retrieved documents ko user question ke saath append karta hai. Ye kaam kaise karta hai?

Student: Sir, QuestionAnswerAdvisor internally ek default template use karta hai.

Is template ka purpose retrieved documents aur user question ko combine karke AI model ko context provide karna hai.

Teacher: Kya hum default template customize kar sakte hain?

Student: Yes sir! Documentation ke according, hum apna custom `PromptTemplate` provide kar sakte hain.

Iske liye:

```
.promptTemplate()
```

builder method use hota hai.

## 13. PromptTemplate kya hai?

Teacher: Simple language mein PromptTemplate samjhao.

Student: PromptTemplate ek predefined structure hai jisme hum placeholders define karte hain.

Runtime par actual values placeholders mein insert hoti hain aur final prompt prepare hota hai.

Teacher: QuestionAnswerAdvisor ke Custom PromptTemplate mein kitne mandatory placeholders hain?

Student: Do mandatory placeholders hain:

1. `query`
2. `question_answer_context`

### 13.1 query placeholder

```
<query>
```

Ismein user ka actual question provide hota hai.

Example:

```
What is Spring Boot?
```

### 13.2 question_answer_context placeholder

```
<question_answer_context>
```

Ismein VectorStore se retrieved documents ka context provide hota hai.

Teacher: Kya hum placeholder names apni marzi se change kar sakte hain?

Student: Documentation ke according, ye dono placeholder names required hain. Custom template mein inhe include karna zaroori hai.

## 14. TemplateRenderer kya hai?

Teacher: Documentation mein TemplateRenderer ka bhi mention hai. Ye kya karta hai?

Student: TemplateRenderer ka purpose template ko render karna hai, yani placeholders ko actual values ke saath resolve karna.

Documentation ke according, custom PromptTemplate kisi bhi `TemplateRenderer` implementation ko use kar sakta hai.

Default implementation:

`StPromptTemplate`

Ye StringTemplate engine par based hai.

Teacher: StTemplateRenderer kya hai?

Student: Ye StringTemplate-based rendering ke liye use hone wala renderer hai.

## 15. Practical Implementation – Custom PromptTemplate

Official documentation ka complete example:

```
PromptTemplate customPromptTemplate = PromptTemplate.builder()
    .renderer(
        StTemplateRenderer.builder()
            .startDelimiterToken('<')
            .endDelimiterToken('>')
            .build()
    )
    .template("""
            <query>

            Context information is below.

            ---------------------
            <question_answer_context>
            ---------------------

            Given the context information and no prior knowledge,
            answer the query.

            Follow these rules:

            1. If the answer is not in the context, just say that you don't know.
            2. Avoid statements like "Based on the context..."
               or "The provided information...".
            """)
    .build();
```

Ab code ko detail mein samajhte hain.

### Step 1 – PromptTemplate.builder()

```
PromptTemplate.builder()
```

Custom PromptTemplate create karne ke liye builder start hota hai.

### Step 2 – renderer()

```
.renderer(...)
```

Template render karne ke liye renderer configure karte hain.

### Step 3 – StTemplateRenderer.builder()

```
StTemplateRenderer.builder()
```

StringTemplate-based renderer create karte hain.

### Step 4 – startDelimiterToken()

```
.startDelimiterToken('<')
```

Placeholder ka starting delimiter define karta hai.

### Step 5 – endDelimiterToken()

```
.endDelimiterToken('>')
```

Placeholder ka ending delimiter define karta hai.

Ab humare placeholders ka format hoga:

```
<query>
<question_answer_context>
```

### Step 6 – template()

```
.template("""
    <query>

    Context information is below.

    ---------------------
    <question_answer_context>
    ---------------------
    """)
```

Yahan hum actual prompt structure define kar rahe hain.

Teacher: Is template mein `<query>` ka role kya hai?

Student: Ismein user ka question aayega.

Teacher: Aur `<question_answer_context>` ka role?

Student: Ismein retrieved documents ka context aayega.

### Step 7 – Prompt Instructions

```
Given the context information and no prior knowledge,
answer the query.
```

Is instruction se model ko bataya ja raha hai ki provided context aur bina prior knowledge par depend karke answer dena hai.

### Step 8 – Rules

```
1. If the answer is not in the context, just say that you don't know.

2. Avoid statements like "Based on the context..."
   or "The provided information...".
```

Teacher: Pehla rule kya bolta hai?

Student: Agar context mein answer available nahi hai, toh model ko clearly bolna hai ki use answer nahi pata.

Teacher: Dusra rule kya bolta hai?

Student: Model ko unnecessary introductory phrases avoid karne hain, jaise:

- Based on the context...
- The provided information...

### Step 9 – build()

```
.build();
```

Complete Custom PromptTemplate create ho jaata hai.

## 16. Custom PromptTemplate ko QuestionAnswerAdvisor mein attach karna

Teacher: Template create karne ke baad use kaise karenge?

Student: Sir, QuestionAnswerAdvisor ke `.promptTemplate()` method ke through attach karenge.

Official documentation ka code:

```
String question =
    "Where does the adventure of Anacletus and Birba take place?";

QuestionAnswerAdvisor qaAdvisor =
    QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
        .promptTemplate(customPromptTemplate)
        .build();
```

Line-by-line explanation:

1\. Question

```
String question = "...";
```

User ka question define kar rahe hain.

2\. QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder()

VectorStore provide karke Advisor create kar rahe hain.

3\. promptTemplate()

```
.promptTemplate(customPromptTemplate)
```

Custom template Advisor ko provide kar rahe hain.

4\. build()

Configured Advisor create kar rahe hain.

## 17. Custom Advisor ko ChatClient ke saath use karna

Official documentation ka code:

```
String response = ChatClient.builder(chatModel).build()
    .prompt(question)
    .advisors(qaAdvisor)
    .call()
    .content();
```

Teacher: Ab complete process explain karo.

Student:

1. ChatClient create hota hai.
2. `.prompt(question)` user question provide karta hai.
3. `.advisors(qaAdvisor)` Custom QuestionAnswerAdvisor attach karta hai.
4. Advisor VectorStore se relevant documents retrieve karta hai.
5. Custom PromptTemplate retrieved context aur user question ko combine karta hai.
6. LLM final answer generate karta hai.
7. `.content()` response ka text return karta hai.

### Complete Custom PromptTemplate flow

User Question

QuestionAnswerAdvisor

VectorStore – Retrieve Context

Custom PromptTemplate

`query` = User Question

`question_answer_context` = Retrieved Documents

LLM

Final Response

# PART 7 – Important Difference: PromptTemplate vs TemplateRenderer

## 18. Kya QuestionAnswerAdvisor PromptTemplate aur ChatClient TemplateRenderer same hain?

Teacher: Documentation mein ek important distinction diya gaya hai. Kya dono same kaam karte hain?

Student: Nahi sir! Dono ka purpose different hai.

### ChatClient ka templateRenderer()

```
.templateRenderer(...)
```

Ye initial user/system prompt content ko render karta hai.

Ye Advisor ke execute hone se pehle initial prompt rendering handle karta hai.

### QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka promptTemplate()

```
.promptTemplate(...)
```

Ye Advisor ke andar retrieved context aur user query ko merge karne ka format customize karta hai.

### Comparison

| ChatClient `.templateRenderer()`            | Advisor `.promptTemplate()`                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| ChatClient-level configuration              | QuestionAnswerAdvisor-level configuration                |
| Initial user/system prompt render karta hai | Retrieved context ko user query ke saath merge karta hai |
| Advisor run hone se pehle                   | Advisor ke context augmentation behavior mein            |

Easy example:

- ChatClient TemplateRenderer = Initial prompt ka format.
- QuestionAnswerAdvisor PromptTemplate = Retrieved context + user question ka format.

Interview tip: Dono ko same mat samajhna. Documentation explicitly kehti hai ki inka purpose alag hai.

# PART 8 – Deprecation Note

## 19. userTextAdvise() deprecated kyu hai?

Teacher: Documentation mein ek deprecation warning di gayi hai. Kaunsi method deprecated hai?

Student: Sir:

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor.Builder.userTextAdvise()
```

Ye method deprecated hai.

Teacher: Iske badle kya use karna chahiye?

Student: Documentation ke according:

```
.promptTemplate()
```

use karna chahiye.

Teacher: Kyu?

Student: Kyunki `.promptTemplate()` prompt customization ke liye more flexible approach provide karta hai.

Deprecated

userTextAdvise()

Recommended

promptTemplate()

Spring AI official documentation ki deprecation guidance ke according.

# PART 9 – Complete Theory Revision

## 20. Important concepts ek jagah

| Concept                 | Simple meaning                                                       |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| RAG                     | External information retrieve karke LLM ko context provide karna     |
| Advisor API             | Ready-made AI processing functionality                               |
| QuestionAnswerAdvisor   | VectorStore se documents retrieve karke context add karna            |
| VectorStore             | Documents aur unke vector representations ke retrieval ke liye store |
| Similarity Search       | User question se related documents find karna                        |
| SearchRequest           | Search configuration                                                 |
| TopK                    | Maximum documents retrieve karne ki limit                            |
| Similarity Threshold    | Minimum similarity score                                             |
| Filter Expression       | Metadata ke basis par search restrict karna                          |
| Dynamic Filter          | Runtime par request-specific filter                                  |
| PromptTemplate          | Custom prompt structure                                              |
| TemplateRenderer        | Template render karna                                                |
| StTemplateRenderer      | StringTemplate-based renderer                                        |
| query                   | User question ka placeholder                                         |
| question_answer_context | Retrieved context ka placeholder                                     |
| userTextAdvise()        | Deprecated method                                                    |

# PART 10 – Interview Questions and Answers

Q1. What is Retrieval Augmented Generation?

RAG is a technique that retrieves external relevant information and provides it as context to an LLM to generate responses.

Q2. Why do we use RAG in Spring AI?

To overcome LLM limitations related to long-form content, factual accuracy and context-awareness.

Q3. What is QuestionAnswerAdvisor?

QuestionAnswerAdvisor is a Spring AI Advisor that performs similarity search in a VectorStore and appends retrieved documents to the user text as context.

Q4. Which dependency is required for QuestionAnswerAdvisor?

`spring-ai-vector-store-advisor`.

Q5. What is SearchRequest?

SearchRequest is used to customize VectorStore search parameters.

Q6. What is TopK?

TopK specifies the maximum number of search results to retrieve.

Q7. What is Similarity Threshold?

It specifies the minimum similarity score required for documents to qualify as search results.

Q8. What is Filter Expression?

It is an SQL-like expression used to filter VectorStore search results based on metadata.

Q9. What is Dynamic Filter Expression?

It allows a filter expression to be updated at runtime for an individual ChatClient request.

Q10. What is PromptTemplate?

PromptTemplate defines the structure of a prompt using placeholders that are replaced with actual values.

Q11. Which placeholders are mandatory in QuestionAnswerAdvisor's custom PromptTemplate?

- `query`
- `question_answer_context`

Q12. What is StTemplateRenderer?

It is a template renderer based on the StringTemplate engine.

Q13. What is the difference between `.templateRenderer()` and `.promptTemplate()`?

ChatClient's `.templateRenderer()` renders the initial user/system prompt. QuestionAnswerAdvisor's `.promptTemplate()` customizes how retrieved context is merged with the user query.

Q14. Which method is deprecated in QuestionAnswerAdvisor.Builder?

`userTextAdvise()`.

Q15. What should be used instead of userTextAdvise()?

`.promptTemplate()`.

Aapke Spring Boot + Spring AI project mein is topic ko implement karne ka sequence:

Implementation progress

Verify VectorStore documents are already loaded

Implement basic QuestionAnswerAdvisor

Implement Dynamic Filter Expression

Final concept yaad rakho, bhai:

`QuestionAnswerAdvisor` ka main kaam hai VectorStore se relevant documents retrieve karna aur unhe user question ke saath context ke form mein LLM ko provide karna.

Iske teen important customization areas hain:

1. SearchRequest – Kaunse documents retrieve karne hain.
2. Filter Expression – Kis type ke documents search karne hain.
3. PromptTemplate – Retrieved context aur user question ko LLM ke saamne kis format mein rakhna hai.

Ye teen concepts properly samajh gaye toh QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka theory aur practical foundation clear ho jayega.
