# Spring AI – QuestionAnswerAdvisor (Detailed Hinglish Notes)

Teacher: Chalo, aaj hum Spring AI mein ek important concept samajhne wale hain, jiska naam hai QuestionAnswerAdvisor.

Abhi tak humne Spring AI mein RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) implement kiya tha. Is video mein hum dekhenge ki Spring AI ka QuestionAnswerAdvisor hamare RAG implementation ko kaise simple bana deta hai.

Chalo step-by-step samajhte hain.

Teacher: Sabse pehle batao, previous video mein humne kya implement kiya tha?

Student: Previous video mein humne RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) implement kiya tha.

RAG ka main purpose hai ki jab user koi question poochta hai, toh hum us question ko directly LLM (Large Language Model) ke paas nahi bhejte.

Uske badle pehle apne database se related information retrieve karte hain aur phir us information ko LLM ke saath share karte hain.

Isse LLM ko user ke question ka relevant context mil jaata hai.

Example:

Maan lo hum ek company ke liye AI chatbot bana rahe hain.

Company ke paas apna data hai:

- Employee information
- Company policies
- Leave policies
- Project documentation
- Technical documentation

Hum ye data apne Vector Database mein store kar denge.

Ab agar user poochta hai:

`What is the company's leave policy?`

Toh hum directly ye question LLM ko nahi bhejenge.

Pehle vector database se leave policy se related information retrieve karenge. Phir retrieved information aur user question ko LLM ke paas bhejenge.

Is process ko RAG kehte hain.

Teacher: RAG implement karne ke liye humne apna data kahan store kiya tha?

Student: Humne apna data Vector Database / Vector Store mein store kiya tha.

Vector database mein hum different types ka data store kar sakte hain.

For example:

- Company data
- Application-related data
- User-related information
- Technical documentation
- PDF documents

Video mein instructor ne bataya hai ki data small ya large ho sakta hai. Aage ke videos mein PDF jaise documents ko load karna bhi cover kiya jayega.

Previous implementation mein instructor ne test execution ke through data load kiya tha.

Ek baar data vector database mein load ho jaane ke baad hum us par similarity search perform kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Ab mujhe RAG ka complete working flow samjhao.

Student: RAG ka working flow kuch is tarah hota hai:

1. User Spring Boot application ko question send karta hai.
2. Application user question ko directly LLM ke paas nahi bhejti.
3. Sabse pehle Vector Store mein Similarity Search perform hoti hai.
4. Vector Store user question se related documents retrieve karta hai.
5. Retrieved documents ko hum context ke roop mein use karte hain.
6. User question aur retrieved context ko combine karke LLM ke paas bhejte hain.
7. LLM provided context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.
8. Finally, generated answer user ko return kar diya jaata hai.

Example:

Vector Database mein ye information stored hai:

`Java Compiler converts Java source code into bytecode.`

User question:

`Who converts Java source code into bytecode?`

Application similarity search perform karegi aur Java Compiler se related information retrieve karegi.

Ab LLM ko ye dono information milengi:

- User Question: Who converts Java source code into bytecode?
- Context: Java Compiler converts Java source code into bytecode.

LLM answer generate karega:

`Java Compiler converts Java source code into bytecode.`

Is tarah LLM ko relevant context milta hai aur woh uske basis par answer generate karta hai.

Teacher: Similarity Search kya hoti hai aur iska kya use hai?

Student: Similarity Search ek technique hai jiska use Vector Database mein user query se related information search karne ke liye hota hai.

Jab user question send karta hai, toh hum us question ke basis par vector database mein similar documents search karte hain.

Vector Store search configuration ke according Top 3, Top 4 ya Top 5 relevant documents return kar sakta hai.

Iske liye hum kuch properties configure kar sakte hain:

- Top-K
- Similarity Threshold
- Filter Expression

In properties ko hum aage detail mein samjhenge.

Teacher: Previous implementation mein humne similarity search kaise perform ki thi?

Student: Previous implementation mein humne similarity search manually perform ki thi.

Humne khud code likha tha:

1. Vector Store se similarity search method call kiya.
2. SearchRequest create kiya.
3. Top-K configure kiya.
4. Similarity Threshold configure kiya.
5. Search execute ki.
6. Retrieved documents se context prepare kiya.
7. Context ko manually prompt mein inject kiya.

Yaani RAG ka kaafi saara kaam hum khud kar rahe the.

Teacher: Manually prompt kyun likhna padta tha?

Student: Kyunki humein LLM ko instructions deni hoti thi ki answer kis tarah generate karna hai.

Previous implementation mein humne ek custom system prompt banaya tha.

Us prompt mein humne LLM ko instruction di thi:

- Provided documents ko read karo.
- Agar answer documents mein available hai, toh uske basis par answer generate karo.
- Apni general knowledge use karke answer mat do.
- Agar answer provided documents mein available nahi hai, toh user ko batao ki query database mein available nahi hai.

Isse hum LLM ke response ko provided context tak restrict karne ki koshish karte hain.

Example:

Maan lo database mein sirf Java aur Spring Boot ki information available hai.

User poochta hai:

`What is Java?`

LLM provided context se answer generate karega.

Lekin agar user poochta hai:

`What is Python?`

Aur Python se related information database mein available nahi hai, toh LLM ko batana chahiye ki provided context mein iska answer available nahi hai.

Teacher: Kya Spring AI mein humein ye saara manual kaam karna zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi!

Spring AI humein Advisors provide karta hai.

Advisors ki help se hum apne AI application mein additional processing automatically perform karwa sakte hain.

Spring AI documentation ke RAG section mein video ke according do important advisors explain kiye gaye hain:

1. QuestionAnswerAdvisor
2. RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor

Is video mein hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor ko detail mein samajh rahe hain.

Teacher: QuestionAnswerAdvisor kya hota hai?

Student: QuestionAnswerAdvisor Spring AI ka ek advisor hai jo automatically user question ke basis par Vector Store mein similarity search perform karta hai.

Ye retrieved documents ko context ke roop mein user message ke saath append kar deta hai.

Iske baad updated user message LLM ke paas jaata hai aur LLM context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.

Sabse important baat:

Ab humein manually similarity search perform karne aur retrieved context ko prompt mein inject karne ka code likhne ki zaroorat nahi hoti.

QuestionAnswerAdvisor ye kaam automatically handle karta hai.

Teacher: QuestionAnswerAdvisor internally kaise work karta hai?

Student: Iska working flow samjho:

Step 1 – User Query

User Spring Boot application ko question send karta hai.

Example:

`What is Spring Boot?`

Step 2 – Advisor Execution

ChatClient ke andar configured QuestionAnswerAdvisor execute hota hai.

Step 3 – Similarity Search

QuestionAnswerAdvisor user query ke basis par Vector Store mein similarity search perform karta hai.

Step 4 – Retrieve Documents

Vector Store relevant documents return karta hai.

Step 5 – Context Injection

QuestionAnswerAdvisor retrieved documents ko context ke roop mein user message mein append karta hai.

Step 6 – LLM Processing

Updated message LLM ke paas jaata hai.

Step 7 – Response Generation

LLM provided context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.

Yaani QuestionAnswerAdvisor RAG ke basic retrieval aur context-injection steps ko automatically handle karta hai.

Teacher: QuestionAnswerAdvisor ko Spring AI application mein kaise use karte hain?

Student: Iske liye humein QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka object create karna hota hai aur usmein Vector Store pass karna hota hai.

Conceptual Java example:

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor advisor =
        QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
                .build();
```

Yahan:

- `QuestionAnswerAdvisor` advisor class hai.
- `builder()` advisor create karne ke liye builder provide karta hai.
- `vectorStore` hamara configured Vector Store hai.
- `build()` advisor ka object create karta hai.

Ab is advisor ko ChatClient ke saath configure kar sakte hain.

```
ChatClient chatClient =
        ChatClient.builder(chatModel)
                .defaultAdvisors(advisor)
                .build();
```

Ab jab bhi ChatClient ke through request process hogi, configured QuestionAnswerAdvisor retrieval process mein participate karega.

Note: Ye video mein samjhaye gaye concept ka illustrative code hai. Exact API signatures Spring AI version ke according verify karne chahiye.

Teacher: Kya QuestionAnswerAdvisor ke saath doosre advisors bhi use kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan.

Video mein instructor ke existing configuration mein kuch aur advisors bhi use ho rahe the:

- MessageChatMemoryAdvisor – Conversation memory manage karne ke liye.
- SimpleLoggerAdvisor – Request aur response logging ke liye.
- SafeGuardAdvisor – Safety-related processing ke liye.
- QuestionAnswerAdvisor – Vector Store se relevant context retrieve karne ke liye.

Yaani hum apne application mein multiple advisors configure kar sakte hain.

Teacher: QuestionAnswerAdvisor use karne ke baad kya humein manually system prompt likhna padega?

Student: Basic QuestionAnswerAdvisor workflow ke liye nahi.

QuestionAnswerAdvisor apna Default Prompt Template provide karta hai.

Ismein instructions hoti hain ki retrieved context ko kaise use karna hai.

Video ke console output mein instructor ne kuch important instructions observe ki:

- Given context ko use karke answer generate karo.
- Provided history ko consider karo.
- Apni prior knowledge ke bajay supplied context ko priority do.
- Agar answer context mein available nahi hai, toh user ko inform karo ki question ka answer nahi diya ja sakta.

Is tarah advisor automatically context aur required prompt instructions ko user message mein add karta hai.

Teacher: Kaise confirm karenge ki QuestionAnswerAdvisor properly work kar raha hai?

Student: Iske liye hum backend console logs check kar sakte hain.

Video mein instructor ne console mein ChatClient request inspect ki.

Wahan user message ke andar ye information dikh rahi thi:

1. Original user query.
2. Retrieved context.
3. Context ko identify karne wali information.
4. Answer generate karne ke instructions.

Isse confirm hua ki QuestionAnswerAdvisor automatically retrieved context ko request mein add kar raha tha.

Teacher: Kya QuestionAnswerAdvisor mein default search configuration hi use karna zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi.

Hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor ko customize bhi kar sakte hain.

Iske liye QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder() ke through custom configuration provide kar sakte hain.

Video mein instructor ne specifically SearchRequest ke through search configuration customize karke dikhaya.

Ismein hum mainly teen important properties configure kar sakte hain:

1. Top-K
2. Similarity Threshold
3. Filter Expression

Chalo inhe detail mein samajhte hain.

Teacher: Top-K kya hota hai?

Student: Top-K define karta hai ki Vector Store similarity search se maximum kitne relevant documents return karega.

Example:

Maan lo humne Top-K = 3 configure kiya hai.

Toh similarity search maximum 3 relevant documents return karegi.

Agar Top-K = 5 hai, toh maximum 5 results retrieve honge.

Practical use:

Agar database mein thousands of documents stored hain, toh hum user query ke according sirf top 3 ya top 5 relevant documents retrieve kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Similarity Threshold kya hota hai?

Student: Similarity Threshold minimum similarity level define karta hai jiske basis par search results filter kiye ja sakte hain.

Example:

Maan lo humne:

`similarityThreshold = 0.5`

configure kiya hai.

Toh search configuration ke according 0.5 ya usse zyada similarity wale results consider kiye ja sakte hain.

Iska purpose irrelevant documents ko search results mein aane se reduce karna hai.

Important: Similarity score ki exact interpretation aur threshold behavior underlying Vector Store implementation par depend kar sakta hai.

Teacher: Filter Expression kya hota hai?

Student: Filter Expression ka use Vector Store mein documents ko additional conditions ke basis par filter karne ke liye hota hai.

Maan lo hamare Vector Database mein multiple departments ka data stored hai:

- HR
- Finance
- IT
- Marketing

Ab humein sirf HR department se related documents search karne hain.

Toh hum metadata filter expression use kar sakte hain.

Isse search ko specific documents tak restrict kiya ja sakta hai.

Video mein instructor ne bataya ki SearchRequest ke andar SQL-style filter expression pass kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Ab mujhe batao, custom SearchRequest kaise banate hain?

Student: Pehle hum SearchRequest ka builder use karte hain.

Example:

```
SearchRequest searchRequest =
        SearchRequest.builder()
                .topK(3)
                .similarityThreshold(0.5)
                .build();
```

Yahan:

- `topK(3)` ka meaning maximum 3 results retrieve karna hai.
- `similarityThreshold(0.5)` ka meaning minimum similarity threshold configure karna hai.
- `build()` SearchRequest object create karta hai.

Ab is SearchRequest ko QuestionAnswerAdvisor mein pass karenge.

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor advisor =
        QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
                .searchRequest(searchRequest)
                .build();
```

Ab QuestionAnswerAdvisor configured SearchRequest ke according similarity search perform karega.

Important: Video mein instructor ne bhi yahi approach use ki thi:

- Top-K = 3
- Similarity Threshold = 0.5

Isse hum retrieval process par apna control rakh sakte hain.

Teacher: Kya QuestionAnswerAdvisor ko sirf simple builder se hi configure kar sakte hain?

Student: Nahi. Video mein do approaches dikhayi gayi hain.

First approach – Simple Builder

```
QuestionAnswerAdvisor advisor =
        QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
                .build();
```

Ismein hum directly Vector Store pass karte hain aur default search behavior use karte hain.

Second approach – Custom SearchRequest

```
SearchRequest searchRequest =
        SearchRequest.builder()
                .topK(3)
                .similarityThreshold(0.5)
                .build();

QuestionAnswerAdvisor advisor =
        QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder(vectorStore)
                .searchRequest(searchRequest)
                .build();
```

Is approach mein hum apni search configuration customize kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Ab video ke practical demonstration ko samjhao. Instructor ne application test kaise ki?

Student: Instructor ne sabse pehle database start kiya.

Uske baad Spring Boot application ko start/restart kiya.

Finally Postman open karke API request send ki.

Postman mein instructor ne ye question poocha:

`Who converts Java source code into bytecode?`

API ne response diya:

`Java source code is converted into bytecode by the Java Compiler, which is a part of the JDK and is typically invoked using the javac command.`

Is response se verify hua ki application properly answer generate kar rahi thi.

Teacher: Is practical test mein QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka role kaise verify kiya?

Student: Instructor ne backend console mein ChatClient request inspect ki.

Console mein user question ke saath retrieved context bhi automatically add hua tha.

Instructor ne observe kiya ki context mein Java Compiler se related information thi.

Yaani manually context add nahi kiya gaya tha.

QuestionAnswerAdvisor ne automatically:

- Similarity Search perform ki.
- Relevant information retrieve ki.
- Context prepare kiya.
- User message mein context inject kiya.

Teacher: Ab agar mujhe sirf 3 documents retrieve karne hain toh kya karna padega?

Student: Humein custom SearchRequest create karna padega aur Top-K ko 3 set karna padega.

Video mein instructor ne exactly yahi demonstration kiya tha.

Configuration:

```
SearchRequest searchRequest =
        SearchRequest.builder()
                .topK(3)
                .similarityThreshold(0.5)
                .build();
```

Is configuration ke baad instructor ne application run ki aur Postman se question poocha:

`Tell me Java Framework.`

LLM ne Spring Boot ko Java framework ke example ke roop mein explain kiya.

Console mein instructor ne verify kiya ki search configuration ke according 3 results consider kiye gaye.

Isse confirm hua ki custom SearchRequest properly work kar raha hai.

Teacher: Kya hum QuestionAnswerAdvisor ka default prompt template customize kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan.

Spring AI humein apna Custom PromptTemplate define karne ki facility deta hai.

Agar humein default prompt instructions change karni hain, toh hum apna prompt template create kar sakte hain.

Iske liye video mein kuch important concepts introduce kiye gaye:

- `PromptTemplate.builder()`
- `StTemplateRenderer.builder()`
- `render()`
- `QuestionAnswerAdvisor.builder()`

Teacher: PromptTemplate kya hota hai?

Student: PromptTemplate ek template hota hai jiske andar hum dynamic variables use kar sakte hain.

Jab application ko user query milti hai, toh hum us query ko template ke andar dynamically insert kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
Answer the following question:

{question}
```

Yahan `{question}` ek dynamic variable hai.

Agar user question hai:

`What is Spring Boot?`

Toh render hone ke baad prompt kuch aisa banega:

```
Answer the following question:

What is Spring Boot?
```

Is tarah dynamic values ke saath prompt generate hota hai.

Teacher: StTemplateRenderer ka kya use hai?

Student: StTemplateRenderer template ke andar defined dynamic variables ko render karne ke liye use hota hai.

Video mein instructor ne explain kiya ki hum renderer builder ke through variable placeholders configure kar sakte hain.

Uske baad template ko render karke final PromptTemplate obtain kar sakte hain.

Overall flow:

1. Custom prompt define karo.
2. Dynamic variables define karo.
3. Template renderer configure karo.
4. Prompt render karo.
5. Rendered template QuestionAnswerAdvisor mein pass karo.
6. Advisor ko ChatClient ke andar configure karo.

Isse hum apne use case ke according custom instructions define kar sakte hain.

Note: Video transcript mein complete runnable custom-template implementation nahi diya gaya hai. Isliye yahan iska conceptual flow explain kiya gaya hai.

Teacher: Ab tak humne QuestionAnswerAdvisor mein kya-kya customize karna seekha?

Student: Humne mainly do important customization options samjhe:

1\. Search Configuration

SearchRequest ke through:

- Top-K
- Similarity Threshold
- Filter Expression

2\. Prompt Configuration

PromptTemplate ke through:

- Custom prompt
- Dynamic variables
- Template rendering

Isse hum retrieval behavior aur prompt instructions dono customize kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Video ke end mein instructor ne kaunsa next topic introduce kiya?

Student: Instructor ne RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor introduce kiya.

Unhone bataya ki har baar user ki original query ko directly vector database mein search karna sufficient nahi hota.

Kabhi-kabhi query ko optimize ya transform karna pad sakta hai.

Kabhi retrieved documents par bhi additional processing karni pad sakti hai.

Isi wajah se advanced RAG workflows mein multiple stages use kiye ja sakte hain.

Teacher: Advanced RAG mein kaun-kaun se stages ho sakte hain?

Student: Instructor ne video mein teen important stages ka naam liya:

1\. Pre-Retrieval

Retrieval se pehle query ya input par processing karna.

2\. Retrieval

Vector Store ya kisi retrieval mechanism se relevant information search karna.

3\. Post-Retrieval

Retrieved information par additional processing karna, taaki LLM ko useful context diya ja sake.

Instructor ne bataya ki in operations ko detail mein next video mein explain kiya jayega.

Teacher: Ab mujhe QuestionAnswerAdvisor aur RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor ka basic difference samjhao.

Student: Is video ke according:

QuestionAnswerAdvisor:

Iska focus simple RAG retrieval workflow ko automatically handle karna hai.

- User question receive karta hai.
- Vector Store mein similarity search karta hai.
- Relevant documents retrieve karta hai.
- Retrieved context ko user message mein append karta hai.
- LLM context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.

RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor:

Iska use advanced RAG workflows ke liye introduce kiya gaya hai, jahan Pre-Retrieval, Retrieval aur Post-Retrieval jaise multiple operations perform karne ki zaroorat ho sakti hai.

Iski detailed implementation next video ka topic hai.

## Complete Working Flow – QuestionAnswerAdvisor

Teacher: Ab poore concept ko ek real application ke example se explain karo.

Student: Maan lo hum ek Java Documentation Chatbot bana rahe hain.

Hamare Vector Database mein Java, Spring Boot aur Microservices ke documents stored hain.

User question poochta hai:

`What is Spring Boot?`

Ab complete workflow:

1\. User Query

`What is Spring Boot?`

2\. QuestionAnswerAdvisor

Advisor request ko process karta hai.

3\. Similarity Search

Vector Store mein relevant documents search hote hain.

4\. Retrieved Context

Spring Boot se related documents retrieve hote hain.

5\. Context Injection

User question + Retrieved Context

6\. LLM

Provided context ke basis par answer generate karta hai.

7\. Final Response

Spring Boot is a Java framework that simplifies application development.

Teacher: Is complete process mein developer ko manually kya-kya karne ki zaroorat nahi padti?

Student: QuestionAnswerAdvisor use karne par basic RAG workflow mein:

- Manually similarity search call nahi karna padta.
- Manually retrieved documents se context prepare nahi karna padta.
- Manually context ko user message mein inject nahi karna padta.
- Basic workflow ke liye apna complete RAG prompt manually maintain nahi karna padta.

Ye kaam QuestionAnswerAdvisor automatically handle karta hai.

Teacher: Very good! Ab batao, aaj ke video se sabse important learning kya hai?

Student: Aaj humne seekha ki Spring AI ka QuestionAnswerAdvisor RAG implementation ko kaafi simple bana deta hai.

Pehle humein manually Vector Store se similarity search perform karni padti thi, documents retrieve karne padte the aur context ko LLM prompt mein inject karna padta tha.

Lekin QuestionAnswerAdvisor ye basic retrieval aur context-injection process automatically handle karta hai.

Hum SearchRequest ke through Top-K, Similarity Threshold aur Filter Expression customize kar sakte hain.

Aur PromptTemplate ke through apna custom prompt bhi define kar sakte hain.

Next video mein hum RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor aur advanced RAG workflow ke baare mein seekhenge.

Bas, yahi tha aaj ke video ka complete concept!
========================================================================================================================
# Spring AI Tutorial – Part 26: Advanced RAG Flow

Video: 26 – Advanced RAG Flow | Spring AI Tutorial in Hindi

Main topic: Spring AI mein `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` aur Advanced RAG Pipeline ke different phases ko samajhna.

Is video mein hum seekhenge ki simple RAG se aage badhkar ek complete modular RAG pipeline kaise design hoti hai. Ismein query ko improve karna, relevant documents retrieve karna, retrieved data clean karna aur final context LLM ko dena shamil hai.

Notes beginner level se explain kiye gaye hain. Ek important baat: uploaded transcript mein kuch beech ka practical demonstration portion incomplete hai, isliye us part ke missing code ya output ko invent nahi karunga.

## 1. Sabse pehle RAG ko samjho

Teacher: RAG ka full form kya hai?

Student: RAG ka full form hai Retrieval-Augmented Generation.

Iska simple meaning hai: AI se answer generate karne se pehle kisi external knowledge source, jaise vector database, se relevant information retrieve karna aur phir us information ki help se answer generate karna.

Teacher: Humein RAG ki zaroorat kyun padti hai?

Student: Normal LLM (Large Language Model) apni training aur request mein diye gaye context ke basis par answer deta hai. Lekin ho sakta hai uske paas hamare private company documents, latest project information ya application-specific data available na ho.

Example:

Suppose company ke paas ek document hai:

```
Company Leave Policy:

Employees get 20 paid leaves per year.
Employees must apply for leave through the HR portal.
```

Ab user poochta hai:

```
How many paid leaves does our company provide?
```

Normal LLM ko company ki private policy ka pata nahi ho sakta.

RAG kya karega?

1. User ka question receive karega.
2. Company ke knowledge database mein relevant information search karega.
3. Matching document retrieve karega.
4. Retrieved information ko user question ke saath combine karega.
5. Dono ko LLM ko send karega.
6. LLM context ke basis par answer generate karega.

Expected answer:

```
Your company provides 20 paid leaves per year.
```

Teacher: Kya RAG LLM ko dobara train karta hai?

Student: Nahi. RAG normally model ko retrain nahi karta. Ye relevant external information retrieve karke model request mein context provide karta hai.

## 2. Simple RAG Flow kaise kaam karta hai?

Teacher: Video mein instructor pehle simple RAG flow revise kyun karata hai?

Student: Kyunki Advanced RAG samajhne ke liye Simple RAG ka working flow samajhna zaroori hai.

Simple RAG mein mainly ye steps hote hain:

1. User application ko question bhejta hai.
2. Application question ko retrieve/search process mein bhejti hai.
3. Vector database mein similarity search hoti hai.
4. Relevant documents retrieve hote hain.
5. Retrieved documents ko question ke saath combine kiya jata hai.
6. Combined context aur question LLM ko send hote hain.
7. LLM answer generate karta hai.
8. Application answer user ko return karti hai.

User Question

Similarity Search

Vector database mein relevant information search

Retrieve Documents

Relevant documents select karna

Add Context to Question

LLM

Context ke basis par answer generate karta hai

Final Answer to User

Teacher: Vector database mein kya store hota hai?

Student: RAG application mein documents ko process karke unke chunks aur embeddings store kiye jaate hain. Embedding ek numerical representation hota hai jo text ka semantic meaning represent karne mein help karta hai.

Jab user question poochta hai, us question ka embedding banakar similar stored document chunks search kiye ja sakte hain.

Teacher: Similarity Search ka matlab kya hai?

Student: Similarity Search ka matlab hai query ke meaning ke similar documents ya document chunks find karna.

Example:

Database mein document hai:

```
Java is an object-oriented programming language.
```

User poochta hai:

```
What is Java?
```

Exact words match na hone par bhi semantic similarity ki wajah se document retrieve ho sakta hai.

Teacher: Simple RAG ko Spring AI mein kaise implement kar sakte hain?

Student: Video mein instructor do approaches ka reference deta hai:

1. Manual RAG: Developer khud similarity search, context creation aur prompt construction implement karta hai.
2. QuestionAnswerAdvisor: Spring AI ka Advisor common question-answer RAG flow ko simplify karta hai.

`QuestionAnswerAdvisor` simple RAG implementation ke liye use kiya ja sakta hai.

## 3. Advanced RAG ki zaroorat kyun hai?

Teacher: Simple RAG available hai, phir Advanced RAG kyun chahiye?

Student: Simple RAG mein generally query retrieve hoti hai, relevant documents milte hain aur context LLM ko bhej diya jata hai.

Lekin real-world applications mein har query itni simple nahi hoti.

Kuch problems dekho:

- User ka question unclear ho sakta hai.
- Query mein unnecessary words ho sakte hain.
- User alag language mein question pooch sakta hai.
- Ek hi query ke multiple meanings ho sakte hain.
- Multiple data sources se documents retrieve karne pad sakte hain.
- Retrieved documents mein irrelevant ya duplicate information ho sakti hai.
- Retrieved document bahut bada ho sakta hai.
- LLM ko bhejne se pehle context optimize karna pad sakta hai.

Advanced RAG in problems ko solve karne ke liye pipeline mein additional processing stages provide karta hai.

Example:

User query:

```
I am studying ML. What is an LLM?
```

Is query ko better search query mein rewrite kiya ja sakta hai:

```
Explain the definition of a Large Language Model.
```

Isse retrieval process ko relevant information find karne mein help mil sakti hai.

Teacher: Kya Advanced RAG ka matlab sirf ek better vector database hai?

Student: Nahi. Advanced RAG ka focus poori retrieval pipeline ko modular banana hai. Ismein query transformation, document retrieval, document joining, post-processing aur context augmentation jaise steps ho sakte hain.

## 4. `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` kya hai?

Teacher: Spring AI mein Advanced RAG implement karne ke liye video mein kaunsa Advisor introduce kiya gaya hai?

Student: `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor`.

Ye Spring AI ka Advisor hai jo modular architecture ke through common RAG pipeline implement karne mein help karta hai.

Teacher: Modular Architecture ka simple meaning kya hai?

Student: Modular Architecture ka matlab hai ek bade process ko chhote-chhote independent modules mein divide karna.

Example: Ek complete RAG pipeline ko hum alag phases mein divide kar sakte hain:

1. Pre-Retrieval
2. Retrieval
3. Post-Retrieval
4. Generation

Har phase ka apna responsibility hota hai.

Iska benefit ye hai ki hum zaroorat ke according alag-alag components add, remove ya customize kar sakte hain.

Teacher: Kya `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` sirf Advanced RAG ke liye use hota hai?

Student: Nahi. Video mein instructor batata hai ki is Advisor se simple RAG, jise Naive RAG bhi kehte hain, implement kiya ja sakta hai. Iske saath additional modules configure karke advanced pipeline bhi banayi ja sakti hai.

### `QuestionAnswerAdvisor` vs `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor`

| Concept             | `QuestionAnswerAdvisor`                | `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor`                           |
| ------------------- | -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| Main use            | Common simple question-answer RAG      | Modular RAG pipeline                                     |
| Customization       | Comparatively simple flow              | Multiple RAG modules configure kar sakte hain            |
| Retrieval           | Common retrieval-based flow            | Custom document retriever configure kar sakte hain       |
| Advanced processing | Limited compared with modular pipeline | Query transformation, post-processing, augmentation etc. |

Easy way to remember:

- `QuestionAnswerAdvisor` = Simple RAG ko easily implement karna.
- `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` = RAG ke different stages ko modular way mein configure karna.

## 5. Spring AI dependency aur class identify karna

Teacher: `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` use karne ke liye kya chahiye?

Student: Project mein compatible Spring AI RAG module/dependency honi chahiye.

Video mein instructor apne Maven project mein Spring AI RAG dependency already added dikhata hai aur IntelliJ IDEA mein `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` class search karta hai.

Teacher: IntelliJ mein class kaise search karte hain?

Student: Video mein instructor `Double Shift` press karke class search karta hai.

Search:

```
RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor
```

Isse IDE mein available class locate ki ja sakti hai.

Teacher: Kya ye Advisor ek interceptor ki tarah kaam karta hai?

Student: Conceptually haan. Advisor ChatClient request ke process mein participate karta hai aur configured retrieval-augmentation flow execute karne mein help karta hai.

Simple flow:

```
Controller
    |
    v
Service
    |
    v
ChatClient
    |
    v
RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor
    |
    v
RAG Pipeline
    |
    v
LLM
    |
    v
Response
```

Important: Advisor ko ChatClient ke saath register karne ke baad configured RAG flow request ke dauran execute hota hai. Actual component execution aur method signatures Spring AI version par depend karte hain.

## 6. Simple RAG ko `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` se implement karna

Teacher: Video mein instructor practical implementation kahan se start karta hai?

Student: Instructor existing service class mein jata hai, jahan pehle `QuestionAnswerAdvisor` use kiya gaya tha.

Ab usko replace karke `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` use karna hai.

### Step 1: Document Retriever samjho

Teacher: `DocumentRetriever` kya hota hai?

Student: Retriever ek component hai jo kisi knowledge source se relevant documents fetch karta hai.

Knowledge source ka example:

- Vector database
- Database
- Web search
- Custom document source

User ki query ke basis par relevant documents retrieve karna iska main responsibility hai.

### Step 2: `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever`

Teacher: Video mein kaunsa retriever use kiya gaya hai?

Student: `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever`.

Ye `DocumentRetriever` ka implementation hai jo Vector Store se relevant documents retrieve karne mein help karta hai.

Simple working:

1. User question receive hota hai.
2. Question ke basis par search request prepare hoti hai.
3. Vector Store similarity search execute karta hai.
4. Relevant documents retrieve hote hain.
5. Retrieved documents RAG pipeline ko return hote hain.

Teacher: Kya hamesha `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever` hi use karna zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi. Ye knowledge source par depend karta hai. Agar documents kisi aur source se retrieve karne hain, to appropriate retriever use ya implement kar sakte hain.

Video mein instructor batata hai ki `DocumentRetriever` interface ko custom implementation ke through bhi use kiya ja sakta hai.

### Step 3: `DocumentRetriever` interface

Teacher: Agar mujhe apna custom retriever banana hai, to kya karna hoga?

Student: Video mein instructor `DocumentRetriever` interface ka reference deta hai.

Is interface mein `retrieve` operation important hai. Transcript ke according `retrieve` abstract method hai aur `apply` default method hai.

Conceptual skeleton:

```
public class CustomDocumentRetriever
        implements DocumentRetriever {

    @Override
    public List<Document> retrieve(
            Query query) {

        // Fetch relevant documents
        // from your own data source

        return List.of();
    }
}
```

Code explanation:

- `implements DocumentRetriever`: Class batati hai ki woh DocumentRetriever contract follow karegi.
- `retrieve(Query query)`: Query receive karke relevant documents return karta hai.
- `List<Document>`: Retrieved documents ki list return hoti hai.

Ye sirf conceptual example hai. Actual `Query` type, method signature aur required implementation apni Spring AI version ke according verify karna chahiye.

### Step 4: Retriever ko configure karna

Video mein instructor `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever` builder ka use karke retriever configure karta hai.

Conceptual configuration:

```
DocumentRetriever documentRetriever =
    VectorStoreDocumentRetriever.builder()
        .vectorStore(vectorStore)
        .topK(3)
        .similarityThreshold(0.05)
        .build();
```

Line-by-line explanation:

- `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever.builder()`: Retriever configuration start karta hai.
- `.vectorStore(vectorStore)`: Batata hai ki kaunsa Vector Store use karna hai.
- `.topK(3)`: Maximum 3 relevant documents retrieve karne ki setting.
- `.similarityThreshold(0.05)`: Similarity score ke basis par minimum threshold configure karne ki setting.
- `.build()`: Configured retriever object banata hai.

Teacher: `topK(3)` ka kya meaning hai?

Student: Search ke dauran maximum 3 matching documents retrieve karne ki setting hai.

Example:

```
Vector Database Search Results:

Document 1 - Java Basics
Document 2 - Java Features
Document 3 - Java Architecture
Document 4 - Python Basics
Document 5 - Database Concepts
```

Agar `topK(3)` set hai, to retrieval operation maximum 3 results return karne ke liye configure hota hai.

Teacher: Similarity Threshold kya karta hai?

Student: Ye similarity score ke basis par documents filter karne mein help karta hai.

Example ke liye, agar threshold `0.05` hai to configured similarity metric aur implementation ke according threshold se match karne wale documents hi qualify karenge.

Important: Similarity score ka meaning aur direction har implementation mein same nahi hota. Threshold `0.05` ko universally best value mat samajhna. Apne Vector Store ke scoring behaviour ke according configure karo.

### Step 5: Advisor build karna

Conceptual code:

```
RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor advisor =
    RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor.builder()
        .documentRetriever(documentRetriever)
        .build();
```

Explanation:

1. `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor.builder()` Advisor configuration start karta hai.
2. `.documentRetriever(documentRetriever)` batata hai ki documents kahan se retrieve karne hain.
3. `.build()` configured Advisor create karta hai.

### Step 6: ChatClient mein Advisor register karna

Video mein instructor is Advisor ko ChatClient ke Advisors mein pass karta hai.

Conceptual example:

```
String response = chatClient.prompt()
    .user(question)
    .advisors(advisor)
    .call()
    .content();
```

Is code mein:

- `.prompt()` request create karta hai.
- `.user(question)` user ka question set karta hai.
- `.advisors(advisor)` RAG Advisor attach karta hai.
- `.call()` synchronous model call execute karta hai.
- `.content()` response text retrieve karta hai.

Teacher: Ab complete request mein kya hoga?

Student: User ka question ChatClient tak aayega. Advisor configured retriever ke through relevant documents fetch karega, context prepare karega aur model interaction mein RAG process apply karega.

Important: Upar ke code snippets teaching examples hain. Exact builder methods, package imports aur registration API tumhari Spring AI version ke according verify karna hoga.

## 7. Advanced RAG ke four main phases

Teacher: Ab tak humne Advisor aur Retriever samjha. Lekin Advanced RAG pipeline ke actual phases kaun se hain?

Student: Video mein instructor four main phases explain karta hai:

1. Pre-Retrieval
2. Retrieval
3. Post-Retrieval
4. Generation

Inhe detail mein samajhte hain.

## Advanced RAG Pipeline

1\. PRE-RETRIEVAL

Query ko prepare, rewrite, translate ya expand karna.

2\. RETRIEVAL

Knowledge base se relevant documents search aur retrieve karna.

3\. POST-RETRIEVAL

Retrieved documents clean, filter, reorder aur compress karna.

4\. GENERATION

Query aur relevant context combine karke final LLM answer generate karna.

## 8. Phase 1 – Pre-Retrieval

Teacher: Pre-Retrieval ka simple meaning kya hai?

Student: Pre-Retrieval ka matlab hai documents search karne se pehle user ki query ko prepare aur optimize karna.

Yaani database mein search karne se pehle hi query ko better banaya jaata hai.

Teacher: Query ko optimize karne ki zaroorat kyun hai?

Student: Kabhi-kabhi user ka question unclear, incomplete, unnecessarily long ya different language mein hota hai.

Aisi query se relevant documents retrieve karna difficult ho sakta hai.

Example:

```
User Query:
I am studying ML. What is an LLM?
```

Is query ko rewrite karke:

```
Explain the definition of a Large Language Model.
```

Banaya ja sakta hai.

Ab search ke liye query ka meaning zyada clear hai.

Teacher: Pre-Retrieval mein kaun se components use hote hain?

Student: Video mein instructor `QueryTransformer` aur uske different implementations explain karta hai.

### 8.1 Query Transformer kya hai?

Teacher: Query Transformer ka kya role hai?

Student: Query Transformer ek component hai jo retrieval se pehle user query ko transform karta hai.

Transform ka meaning hai query ko modify, rewrite, translate, compress ya expand karna, taaki search process better ho sake.

Simple flow:

```
Original User Query
        |
        v
Query Transformer
        |
        v
Modified / Optimized Query
        |
        v
Document Retrieval
```

Video mein instructor kuch important query transformer implementations ke naam batata hai.

### 8.2 Rewrite Query Transformer

Teacher: Rewrite Query Transformer kya karta hai?

Student: Ye original user query ko rewrite karke ek clearer ya more effective search query banata hai.

Example:

Original:

```
I am studying ML. What is an LLM?
```

Rewritten:

```
Explain what a Large Language Model is.
```

Why useful?

- Unnecessary words reduce ho sakte hain.
- Query ka intent clear ho sakta hai.
- Search ke liye useful keywords mil sakte hain.

### 8.3 Translation Query Transformer

Teacher: Translation Query Transformer kya karta hai?

Student: Ye query ko ek language se doosri language mein translate karta hai.

Example:

Original query:

```
Java kya hai?
```

Translated query:

```
What is Java?
```

Real-world use case:

Suppose company ka knowledge base English mein hai, lekin users Hindi, Marathi ya kisi doosri language mein question poochte hain.

Aise scenario mein query translation useful ho sakta hai.

### 8.4 Compression Query Transformer

Teacher: Compression ka simple meaning kya hai?

Student: Compression ka matlab hai query ko short aur concise banana, unnecessary information remove karke important meaning preserve karna.

Example:

Original:

```
I am currently learning Java programming and I want
to understand what the Spring Boot framework is
and why developers use it in enterprise applications.
```

Compressed:

```
What is Spring Boot and why is it used?
```

Why useful?

Long query ko concise banane se search process ko relevant information par focus karne mein help mil sakti hai.

### 8.5 Multi-Query Expander

Teacher: Multi-Query Expander kya karta hai?

Student: Ye ek original query se multiple related query variations generate karta hai.

Example:

Original query:

```
What is Spring Boot?
```

Possible query variations:

```
1. Explain Spring Boot framework.
2. What are the benefits of Spring Boot?
3. How does Spring Boot simplify Java development?
```

Ab multiple query variations ki help se broader relevant information search ki ja sakti hai.

Teacher: Iska benefit kya hai?

Student: Kabhi-kabhi user ek hi tarah se question poochta hai, jabki knowledge base mein same topic different words se explain hota hai.

Multiple query variations search coverage improve kar sakti hain.

Important: Multiple queries se additional retrieval work aur duplicate documents aa sakte hain. Isliye baad mein joining aur filtering useful ho sakti hai.

## 9. Phase 2 – Retrieval

Teacher: Pre-Retrieval complete hone ke baad kya hota hai?

Student: Ab optimized query ko knowledge base mein search ke liye bheja jaata hai. Isi process ko Retrieval kehte hain.

Is phase ka main purpose relevant documents retrieve karna hai.

### 9.1 Document Retriever

Teacher: Document Retriever kya hai?

Student: Ye component knowledge base se relevant documents fetch karta hai.

Knowledge base ka example:

- Vector Database
- Document store
- Other supported data sources

Video mein instructor `DocumentRetriever` ko retrieval module ka important component batata hai.

### 9.2 Vector Store Document Retriever

Teacher: `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever` kya karta hai?

Student: Ye Vector Store se similar documents retrieve karta hai.

Ismein generally ye configuration important ho sakti hai:

- `topK`: Kitne maximum documents retrieve karne hain.
- `similarityThreshold`: Minimum similarity score criteria.
- Metadata filter: Kis type ke documents search karne hain.

Example:

```
User Query:
What is Java?

Vector Store Results:

1. Java Introduction
2. Java Features
3. Java Architecture
4. Python Introduction
5. Database Concepts
```

Agar top K = 3 hai, to retrieval maximum 3 documents return karne ke liye configure hoga.

### 9.3 Metadata Filtering

Teacher: Metadata kya hota hai?

Student: Metadata ka matlab hai document ke baare mein additional information.

Example:

```
Document Content:
Java is an object-oriented language.

Metadata:
category = programming
language = English
source = java-guide.pdf
```

Agar user sirf Java programming documents search karna chahta hai, to metadata filter relevant documents ko select karne mein help kar sakta hai.

Example filter condition:

```
category = programming
```

Isse sirf matching category wale documents retrieve karne ki condition lagayi ja sakti hai.

Teacher: Similarity Threshold aur Metadata Filter mein kya difference hai?

Student:

- Similarity Threshold: Query aur document ke similarity score par selection karta hai.
- Metadata Filter: Document ki additional properties ke basis par selection karta hai.

Dono alag purposes serve karte hain.

### 9.4 Document Joiner

Teacher: Agar multiple retrievers se documents aa rahe hain to kya karenge?

Student: Un documents ko combine karne ke liye Document Joiner use kar sakte hain.

Example:

Suppose application do sources se information retrieve karti hai:

```
Source 1: Vector Database

Document A
Document B

Source 2: Web Search

Document C
Document D
```

Ab Document Joiner retrieved documents ko combine kar sakta hai.

Iska purpose ek combined document result prepare karna hai.

Teacher: Iski zaroorat kyun padti hai?

Student: Multiple sources se information retrieve karne ke baad unhe ek result mein combine karna useful hota hai.

Isse downstream processing ko combined documents mil sakte hain.

### 9.5 `ConcatenationDocumentJoiner`

Teacher: Video mein kaunsa Document Joiner bataya gaya hai?

Student: `ConcatenationDocumentJoiner`.

Ye multiple retrieved document lists ko concatenate karke ek combined list banane ke liye use hota hai.

Example:

```
List 1:
Document A
Document B

List 2:
Document C
Document D

Combined List:
Document A
Document B
Document C
Document D
```

Important: Concatenation ka matlab automatically duplicate documents remove karna nahi hota. Deduplication ke liye additional logic ya suitable implementation ki zaroorat ho sakti hai.

Teacher: Document Joiner ka token usage se kya relation hai?

Student: Multiple sources se documents aane par unnecessary ya duplicate content model ko bhejne ka risk hota hai.

Joining ke baad deduplication aur filtering jaise operations se unnecessary context reduce kiya ja sakta hai. Isse prompt tokens aur model cost reduce karne mein help mil sakti hai.

## 10. Phase 3 – Post-Retrieval

Teacher: Documents retrieve ho gaye. Kya ab direct LLM ko bhej dena chahiye?

Student: Har situation mein nahi. Retrieved documents mein irrelevant, duplicate, noisy ya unnecessary information ho sakti hai.

Isliye LLM ko bhejne se pehle documents ko process karna useful hota hai. Is phase ko Post-Retrieval kehte hain.

### 10.1 Document Post-Processor

Teacher: Document Post-Processor kya karta hai?

Student: Ye retrieved documents ko further process karta hai.

Video mein instructor kuch possible operations batata hai:

1. Cleaning
2. Reordering
3. Compression
4. Irrelevant information removal
5. Redundant information removal
6. Noise reduction

### Example

Suppose Vector Database se ye document retrieve hua:

```
Java is a programming language.

Java supports OOP.

Random unrelated text: Weather is sunny today.

Java runs on JVM.

Repeated information: Java supports OOP.
```

Post-processing ke baad context kuch aisa ho sakta hai:

```
Java is a programming language.

Java supports Object-Oriented Programming.

Java runs on JVM.
```

Teacher: Cleaning kyun important hai?

Student: Retrieved documents mein irrelevant information ho sakti hai. Cleaning se unnecessary content remove karne mein help milti hai.

### 10.2 Reordering / Reranking

Teacher: Reordering ka simple meaning kya hai?

Student: Documents ko unki relevance ya importance ke according arrange karna.

Example:

```
Before Reordering:

1. Java History
2. Java Features
3. Java Definition

After Reordering:

1. Java Definition
2. Java Features
3. Java History
```

Agar user ne Java definition poochhi hai, to definition wala document pehle aana useful ho sakta hai.

Teacher: Reranking kya hota hai?

Student: Reranking ka matlab retrieved documents ko dobara relevance ke basis par rank karna hai.

Initial similarity search ke baad ek additional ranking process use karke better order prepare kiya ja sakta hai.

### 10.3 Document Compression

Teacher: Document Compression kya karta hai?

Student: Large documents ko short, useful information mein reduce karne mein help karta hai, while preserving relevant information.

Example:

Original document mein 10 paragraphs hain, lekin user ke question ke liye sirf 2 paragraphs relevant hain.

Compression process useful information ko concise form mein prepare kar sakta hai.

Why useful?

- Prompt size reduce ho sakta hai.
- Token usage reduce ho sakta hai.
- Irrelevant context kam ho sakta hai.
- Model ko relevant information par focus karne mein help mil sakti hai.

Important: Compression ke dauran important facts accidentally remove ho sakte hain. Isliye compressed context ki quality verify karni chahiye.

## 11. Phase 4 – Generation

Teacher: Post-Retrieval ke baad next phase kya hai?

Student: Generation.

Is phase mein user ki original query aur retrieved/processed context ko combine karke final model request prepare ki jaati hai.

### 11.1 Query Augmenter

Teacher: Query Augmenter kya karta hai?

Student: Ye original query ko retrieved context ke saath enrich karta hai.

Simple meaning: User ka question + relevant documents = enriched prompt.

Example:

Original Query:

```
What is Java?
```

Retrieved Context:

```
Java is a high-level, object-oriented programming language.
It runs on the Java Virtual Machine (JVM).
```

Enriched Prompt:

```
Use the following context to answer the question.

Context:
Java is a high-level, object-oriented programming language.
It runs on the Java Virtual Machine (JVM).

Question:
What is Java?
```

Ab LLM ko question ke saath relevant context bhi mil gaya.

### 11.2 `ContextualQueryAugmenter`

Teacher: Video mein kaunsa Query Augmenter dikhaya gaya hai?

Student: `ContextualQueryAugmenter`.

Ye retrieved document context ko query ke saath combine karne mein help karta hai.

Teacher: Iska benefit kya hai?

Student: LLM ko question ke saath relevant information milti hai. Isse application retrieved context ke basis par grounded answer generate karne mein help kar sakti hai.

Important: Context provide karne se hallucination ka risk kam ho sakta hai, lekin completely eliminate nahi hota. Prompt mein clear instructions aur output validation bhi useful hain.

### 11.3 Final Answer Generation

Teacher: Ab LLM ko kya send hota hai?

Student: LLM ko enriched query milti hai, jisme user ka question aur retrieved context hota hai.

LLM in dono ko process karke final answer generate karta hai.

Complete example:

```
User Question:
What is Java?

Retrieved Context:
Java is a high-level, object-oriented programming language.

Enriched Prompt:
Answer the question using the given context.

Question:
What is Java?

Context:
Java is a high-level, object-oriented programming language.

LLM Response:
Java is a high-level, object-oriented programming language.
```

## 12. Advanced RAG ke important interfaces aur implementations

Teacher: Video ke end mein instructor kaun se important Spring AI components identify karta hai?

Student: Instructor IntelliJ IDEA mein different interfaces aur implementations dikhata hai.

| Phase          | Component                      | Responsibility                                |
| -------------- | ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------- |
| Pre-Retrieval  | `QueryTransformer`             | Query ko transform karna                      |
| Pre-Retrieval  | `RewriteQueryTransformer`      | Query rewrite karna                           |
| Pre-Retrieval  | `TranslationQueryTransformer`  | Query translate karna                         |
| Pre-Retrieval  | `CompressionQueryTransformer`  | Query compress karna                          |
| Pre-Retrieval  | `MultiQueryExpander`           | Multiple query variations banana              |
| Retrieval      | `DocumentRetriever`            | Relevant documents retrieve karna             |
| Retrieval      | `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever` | Vector Store se documents retrieve karna      |
| Retrieval      | `DocumentJoiner`               | Multiple retrieval results combine karna      |
| Retrieval      | `ConcatenationDocumentJoiner`  | Document lists concatenate karna              |
| Post-Retrieval | `DocumentPostProcessor`        | Retrieved documents process karna             |
| Generation     | `QueryAugmenter`               | Query mein context add karna                  |
| Generation     | `ContextualQueryAugmenter`     | Retrieved context ke saath query enrich karna |

Important: Ye video mein discuss kiye gaye component names aur unki conceptual responsibilities hain. Har implementation ka exact package, constructor aur builder API tumhari Spring AI release ke hisaab se verify karna chahiye.

## 13. Complete Advanced RAG Example

Teacher: Ab ek real-world example se saare phases ko connect karte hain.

Student: Suppose hum ek company chatbot bana rahe hain jo company ke technical documents se answers deta hai.

User question:

```
I am studying ML. What is an LLM?
```

### Step 1: Pre-Retrieval

Query Transformer question ko rewrite karta hai:

```
Explain the definition of a Large Language Model.
```

### Step 2: Retrieval

Document Retriever vector database mein search karta hai.

Retrieved documents:

```
Document 1:
An LLM is a Large Language Model.

Document 2:
LLMs are trained on large amounts of text data.

Document 3:
LLMs can perform language understanding and generation tasks.
```

### Step 3: Post-Retrieval

Document Post-Processor:

- Unnecessary content remove karta hai.
- Relevant documents ko reorder karta hai.
- Duplicate information reduce karta hai.
- Required context concise banata hai.

### Step 4: Generation

Contextual Query Augmenter query aur retrieved documents combine karta hai.

Final prompt:

```
Question:
What is an LLM?

Context:
An LLM is a Large Language Model.
It is trained on large amounts of text data.
It can perform language understanding and generation tasks.

Answer using the provided context.
```

### Step 5: LLM Response

```
An LLM (Large Language Model) is an AI model
trained on large amounts of text data. It can
understand and generate human language.
```

Is tarah complete Advanced RAG pipeline execute hoti hai.

## 14. Is video mein kya practically implement hua aur kya next video mein hoga?

Teacher: Kya instructor ne complete Advanced RAG pipeline is video mein implement ki?

Student: Nahi. Is video ka main purpose Advanced RAG ke architecture aur different phases ko samjhana hai.

Instructor ne:

- `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` introduce kiya.
- Simple RAG ke liye Advisor aur retriever configure karne ka demonstration diya.
- `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever` ka use dikhaya.
- `topK`, similarity threshold aur optional filter ka concept explain kiya.
- Advanced RAG ke different phases aur components explain kiye.

Uploaded transcript ke beech ke practical demonstration ka kuch hissa incomplete hai. Isliye us part ke exact code, error resolution aur final output ko confirmed implementation ke roop mein nahi likha gaya hai.

Teacher: Next video mein kya seekhne wale hain?

Student: Instructor batata hai ki next video mein:

1. Advanced RAG ka practical example implement karenge.
2. Complete Advanced RAG pipeline configure karenge.
3. PDF documents ko Vector Database mein store karenge.
4. Ek additional database/source ka use bhi dekhenge.

Yaani is video mein architecture samjha aur next video mein uska practical implementation aane wala hai.

## 15. Final Revision – Advanced RAG

Teacher: Advanced RAG ke sabse important points kya hain?

Student:

1. RAG: Relevant external knowledge retrieve karke LLM ko context provide karna.
2. Simple RAG: Query → Similarity Search → Retrieve Documents → Add Context → LLM.
3. Advanced RAG: RAG pipeline ko multiple modular phases mein divide karna.
4. `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor`: Spring AI ka Advisor jo modular RAG flow implement karne mein help karta hai.
5. Pre-Retrieval: Database search se pehle query prepare karna.
6. Query Transformer: Query rewrite, translate, compress ya expand karna.
7. Retrieval: Knowledge base se relevant documents fetch karna.
8. Document Retriever: Documents retrieve karne ki responsibility.
9. Document Joiner: Multiple retrieval results combine karna.
10. Post-Retrieval: Retrieved documents clean, reorder, filter aur compress karna.
11. Document Post-Processor: Retrieved documents ko further process karna.
12. Generation: Query aur context ko combine karke LLM se answer generate karna.
13. Query Augmenter: Retrieved context ko query mein add karna.
14. `ContextualQueryAugmenter`: Context aur question ko combine karne wala component.

### Complete Concept Flow

```
USER QUESTION
      |
      v
RETRIEVAL AUGMENTATION ADVISOR
      |
      v
1. PRE-RETRIEVAL
      |
      |-- Query Transformer
      |-- Rewrite Query
      |-- Translate Query
      |-- Compress Query
      |-- Expand Query
      |
      v
2. RETRIEVAL
      |
      |-- Document Retriever
      |-- Vector Store Retriever
      |-- Document Joiner
      |
      v
3. POST-RETRIEVAL
      |
      |-- Document Post-Processor
      |-- Cleaning
      |-- Reordering
      |-- Compression
      |
      v
4. GENERATION
      |
      |-- Query Augmenter
      |-- Contextual Query Augmenter
      |
      v
LLM
      |
      v
FINAL ANSWER
```

Teacher: Simple RAG aur Advanced RAG mein sabse important difference kya hai?

Student: Simple RAG mein basic retrieval aur context generation flow hota hai. Advanced RAG mein query preparation, multiple retrieval sources, document joining, post-processing aur context augmentation jaise additional modules configure kar sakte hain.

Is video ka main takeaway: `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` aur uske modular building blocks ki help se hum Spring AI mein apni requirement ke according simple se advanced RAG pipeline design kar sakte hain.
========================================================================================================================
# Spring AI Tutorial – Part 27: Implementing RAG Pipeline Using Spring AI

Topic: Pre-Retrieval | Retrieval | Post-Retrieval | Query Transformation | Query Expansion | Document Retrieval | Document Joining | Query Augmentation

Style: Simple Hinglish – Teacher asks → Student answers

Is video mein hum Advanced RAG ko practically Spring AI project mein implement karna seekhenge. Pichhle video mein humne Advanced RAG ke concepts samjhe the. Is video mein unhi components ko code ke through implement kiya gaya hai.

## 1. Project Setup – Advanced RAG

Teacher: Is video mein hum kya implement karne wale hain?

Student: Hum Spring AI ki help se ek Advanced RAG Pipeline implement karenge.

Normal RAG mein hum user ki query ko vector database mein search karte hain, relevant documents retrieve karte hain aur LLM ko context ke saath bhejte hain.

Lekin Advanced RAG mein hum sirf documents retrieve nahi karte. Retrieval se pehle aur baad mein bhi multiple operations perform karte hain.

Advanced RAG ke main phases:

1. Pre-Retrieval: Query ko improve karna.
2. Retrieval: Relevant documents fetch karna.
3. Post-Retrieval: Retrieved documents ko process aur combine karna.
4. Generation: Context ke saath LLM se answer generate karwana.

Teacher: Instructor ne project mein kya setup kiya hai?

Student: Instructor ne ek naya Spring Boot project banaya hai:

```
advanced-rag
```

Existing project se basic configuration copy ki gayi hai.

Project mein initially:

- ChatClient configuration
- Logger Advisor
- Safe Guard Advisor
- Chat Service
- Chat Controller
- Vector Store mein data save karne ka method

Chat Memory ka use is practical project mein nahi kiya gaya hai.

Important: Project ka initial setup sirf demonstration ke liye hai. Actual RAG implementation Chat Service ki implementation class mein ki jayegi.

### 1.1 Controller se Service tak request ka flow

Teacher: User ki query application mein kaise aati hai?

Student: User Postman se query send karta hai. Controller query receive karta hai aur Chat Service ke `getResponse()` method ko call karta hai.

Basic flow:

```
Postman
   |
   v
ChatController
   |
   v
ChatService.getResponse()
   |
   v
Response
```

Initially instructor ne testing ke liye hardcoded response return kiya:

```
return "This is testing response from Chat Service";
```

Isse confirm kiya gaya ki Controller aur Service properly connected hain.

## 2. First Step – Direct LLM Call

Teacher: RAG implement karne se pehle instructor ne kya kiya?

Student: Sabse pehle direct ChatClient call implement kiya, jisme user ki query directly LLM ko send hoti hai.

Example:

```
String response = chatClient.prompt()
        .user(userQuery)
        .call()
        .content();

return response;
```

### 2.1 Code ko line by line samjho

Teacher: `chatClient.prompt()` kya karta hai?

Student: Ye ChatClient ke through ek prompt request prepare karta hai.

Teacher: `.user(userQuery)` kya karta hai?

Student: Ye user ki query ko prompt ke user message ke roop mein set karta hai.

Example:

```
What is Java?
```

Teacher: `.call()` kya karta hai?

Student: Ye synchronous call execute karta hai. Matlab application LLM ko request bhejti hai aur response ka wait karti hai.

Teacher: `.content()` kya return karta hai?

Student: Ye generated response ka text content return karta hai.

### 2.2 Direct LLM Call ka flow

```
User Query
    |
    v
ChatClient.prompt()
    |
    v
.user(userQuery)
    |
    v
.call()
    |
    v
LLM
    |
    v
Generated Answer
```

Teacher: Kya is flow mein RAG use ho raha hai?

Student: Nahi. Is flow mein:

- Vector Database search nahi hoti.
- Documents retrieve nahi hote.
- Context inject nahi hota.
- Query preprocessing nahi hoti.

User ki query directly LLM ko bhej di jaati hai.

Example:

User:

```
What is Java?
```

LLM apne existing knowledge ke basis par answer generate karega.

Lekin agar hum chahte hain ki answer hamare private documents ya company knowledge base ke basis par aaye, toh humein RAG pipeline implement karni hogi.

## 3. RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor – Advanced RAG ka Main Component

Teacher: Spring AI mein Advanced RAG implement karne ke liye kaunsa Advisor use karenge?

Student: Hum RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor use karenge.

Ye Spring AI ka Advisor hai jo RAG pipeline ke different components ko organize aur execute karne mein help karta hai.

Iske through hum alag-alag stages ki implementations provide kar sakte hain:

- Query Transformer
- Query Expander
- Document Retriever
- Document Joiner
- Document Post Processor
- Query Augmenter

Teacher: Kya ye sab components ek hi kaam karte hain?

Student: Nahi. Har component ka responsibility alag hai.

| Component             | Responsibility                                  |
| --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- |
| QueryTransformer      | User query ko modify karta hai                  |
| QueryExpander         | Ek query se multiple queries generate karta hai |
| DocumentRetriever     | Data source se documents retrieve karta hai     |
| DocumentJoiner        | Multiple results ko combine karta hai           |
| DocumentPostProcessor | Retrieved documents process karta hai           |
| QueryAugmenter        | User query ke saath context combine karta hai   |

Teacher: Advisor ko ChatClient mein kaise attach karenge?

Student: Instructor ne Advisor ko ChatClient request ke andar add kiya hai.

Conceptual code:

```
chatClient.prompt()
    .user(userQuery)
    .advisors(retrievalAugmentationAdvisor)
    .call()
    .content();
```

Ab jab ChatClient LLM ko request bhejega, toh Advisor pehle apni RAG pipeline execute karega.

Important: Is video mein `RetrievalAugmentationAdvisor` ko manually configure kiya gaya hai. Iska purpose hai ki hum pipeline ke different stages ko apni requirement ke according control kar saken.

# 4. Pre-Retrieval Phase

Teacher: Pre-Retrieval ka kya meaning hai?

Student: Pre-Retrieval ka matlab hai documents ko vector database se search karne se pehle user ki query ko improve karna.

Kabhi-kabhi user ki query:

- Bahut lengthy hoti hai.
- Ambiguous hoti hai.
- Unnecessary information contain karti hai.
- Database mein stored language se different language mein hoti hai.
- Search ke liye sufficient information provide nahi karti.

Aisi situation mein original query se relevant documents retrieve karna difficult ho sakta hai.

Isliye hum query ko transform ya expand karte hain.

### 4.1 QueryTransformer Interface

Teacher: Spring AI mein query transformation ke liye kaunsa interface use hota hai?

Student: Video mein `QueryTransformer` interface explain kiya gaya hai.

Is interface ka responsibility original query ko transform karna hai.

Iske implementations alag-alag operations perform kar sakte hain.

Video mein teen implementations explain ki gayi hain:

1. CompressionQueryTransformer
2. RewriteQueryTransformer
3. TranslationQueryTransformer

## 5. CompressionQueryTransformer

Teacher: CompressionQueryTransformer kya karta hai?

Student: Ye conversation history ko compress karta hai aur usse ek standalone query banane mein help karta hai.

Simple language mein samjho:

User aur chatbot ke beech conversation chal rahi hai. User har baar complete question nahi poochta. Kabhi previous conversation ke reference mein short question poochta hai.

Example:

```
User: What is Java?

Assistant: Java is a programming language.

User: What are its features?
```

Yahan second query:

```
What are its features?
```

Apne aap mein incomplete hai. Humein previous conversation se samajhna padega ki user Java ke features pooch raha hai.

CompressionQueryTransformer conversation history ko process karke meaningful standalone query banane mein help karta hai.

Possible transformed query:

```
What are the features of Java programming language?
```

Teacher: Iska benefit kya hai?

Student: Isse unnecessary conversation history ko LLM tak bhejne ki zarurat kam ho sakti hai aur retrieval ke liye meaningful query milti hai.

Important: Video mein instructor ne is transformer ko practical code mein use nahi kiya. Sirf iska purpose explain kiya.

## 6. RewriteQueryTransformer

Teacher: RewriteQueryTransformer kya karta hai?

Student: Ye user ki original query ko rewrite karta hai, taaki vector database se better matching documents retrieve ho saken.

Is transformer mein LLM ka use hota hai.

### 6.1 Example

Original query:

```
Tell me something about Java and what exactly it is
and explain it in detail.
```

Rewritten query:

```
What is Java programming language?
```

Ab query short aur clear ho gayi.

Teacher: Rewrite karne ki requirement kab hoti hai?

Student: Jab user ki query:

- Bahut lengthy ho.
- Unnecessary information contain karti ho.
- Ambiguous ho.
- Search ke liye clear na ho.

Toh RewriteQueryTransformer query ko better search format mein convert kar sakta hai.

### 6.2 Code Implementation

Video mein instructor ne `RewriteQueryTransformer` ka builder use kiya.

```
RewriteQueryTransformer rewriteQueryTransformer =
        RewriteQueryTransformer.builder()
                .chatClientBuilder(
                        chatClient.mutate().build().mutate()
                )
                .build();
```

Important accuracy note: Transcript mein builder ko configure karne ke liye ChatClient ka builder pass kiya gaya hai. Exact method chain Spring AI version ke according verify karni chahiye. Video mein instructor `chatClient.mutate()` se builder obtain karne aur clone/build karne ka approach explain karte hain.

Conceptually:

```
Original User Query
       |
       v
RewriteQueryTransformer
       |
       v
ChatClient / LLM
       |
       v
Rewritten Query
```

Teacher: RewriteQueryTransformer internally LLM ko kyun call karta hai?

Student: Kyunki query ko meaningful search query mein rewrite karne ke liye LLM se help li jaati hai.

Video mein bataya gaya hai ki transformer LLM ko instruction deta hai:

- Given user query ko rewrite karo.
- Irrelevant information remove karo.
- Search ke liye better query generate karo.

Important: RewriteQueryTransformer khud vector database se documents retrieve nahi karta. Ye sirf query ko improve karta hai.

## 7. TranslationQueryTransformer

Teacher: TranslationQueryTransformer ka kya use hai?

Student: Ye user ki query ko ek language se doosri language mein translate karta hai.

Iska use tab hota hai jab user ek language mein question poochta hai, lekin vector database mein documents kisi doosri language mein stored hote hain.

### 7.1 Real-world example

Suppose:

Vector Database mein documents English language mein stored hain.

User Hindi mein question poochta hai:

```
Java programming language kya hai?
```

Agar retrieval English documents ke against karna hai, toh query ko English mein translate kar sakte hain.

Translated query:

```
What is Java programming language?
```

Ab English query ke basis par vector database search ki ja sakti hai.

Teacher: TranslationQueryTransformer internally kya use karta hai?

Student: Ye bhi LLM ka use karta hai.

Isliye isko bhi ChatClient builder provide karna padta hai.

### 7.2 TranslationQueryTransformer Implementation

Video mein instructor ne `TranslationQueryTransformer` ka builder use kiya aur target language set ki.

Conceptual code:

```
TranslationQueryTransformer translationTransformer =
        TranslationQueryTransformer.builder()
                .chatClientBuilder(chatClientBuilder)
                .targetLanguage("English")
                .build();
```

Explanation:

- `builder()` – Transformer configure karne ke liye builder create karta hai.
- `chatClientBuilder(...)` – LLM call karne ke liye ChatClient builder provide karta hai.
- `targetLanguage("English")` – Query ko English mein translate karne ka target set karta hai.
- `build()` – Transformer object create karta hai.

Video mein exact configuration ka method chain Spring AI version ke hisaab se check karna chahiye.

### 7.3 Multiple Query Transformers

Teacher: Kya hum ek hi transformer use kar sakte hain?

Student: Haan, lekin requirement ke according multiple transformers bhi use kar sakte hain.

Example:

```
Original Query
      |
      v
RewriteQueryTransformer
      |
      v
TranslationQueryTransformer
      |
      v
Transformed Query
```

Suppose user ne Hindi mein lengthy query poochi:

```
Mujhe Java ke baare mein detail mein batao.
```

Possible operations:

1. Rewrite transformer query ko clear banayega.
2. Translation transformer query ko English mein convert karega.
3. Ab transformed query retrieval ke liye use ho sakti hai.

Important: Multiple transformers ko dene ka order important hai. Ek transformer ka output next transformer ko mil sakta hai.

# 8. Retrieval Phase – Document Retrieve Karna

Teacher: Pre-Retrieval ke baad next phase kaunsa hai?

Student: Next phase hai Retrieval Phase.

Is phase mein hum improved query ke basis par relevant documents retrieve karte hain.

Spring AI mein video ke according iske liye `DocumentRetriever` interface use hota hai.

### 8.1 DocumentRetriever

Teacher: DocumentRetriever ka responsibility kya hai?

Student: Ye underlying data source se relevant documents retrieve karne ke liye responsible hota hai.

Underlying data source kuch bhi ho sakta hai, depending on its implementation.

Video mein instructor vector database use kar rahe hain.

Isliye implementation hai:

```
VectorStoreDocumentRetriever
```

Teacher: Kya DocumentRetriever sirf Vector Database ke liye hai?

Student: Nahi. `DocumentRetriever` abstraction hai. Iski different implementations banayi ja sakti hain.

Example:

- Vector Database se documents retrieve karna.
- Internet se documents retrieve karna.
- Kisi external data source se documents retrieve karna.

Agar custom implementation banani ho, toh interface implement karke retrieval logic likh sakte hain.

### 8.2 VectorStoreDocumentRetriever Implementation

Video mein instructor ne builder ka use kiya:

```
VectorStoreDocumentRetriever documentRetriever =
        VectorStoreDocumentRetriever.builder()
                .vectorStore(vectorStore)
                .topK(3)
                .similarityThreshold(0.5)
                .build();
```

Note: Transcript mein similarity threshold ki value audio mein unclear hai. Isliye yahan 0.5 ko sirf illustrative example samjhein; ise video ki confirmed value na maanein.

Line-by-line explanation:

1\. `VectorStoreDocumentRetriever.builder()`

Document retriever ka builder create karta hai.

2\. `.vectorStore(vectorStore)`

Yahan configured VectorStore object provide karte hain.

Ye wahi VectorStore hai jisme humne documents aur unki embeddings store ki hain.

3\. `.topK(3)`

Iska meaning hai maximum 3 relevant documents retrieve karne hain.

Example:

```
Vector Database
       |
       v
Similarity Search
       |
       v
Top 3 Documents
```

4\. `.similarityThreshold(...)`

Ye minimum similarity threshold define karta hai.

Iske basis par retrieval results filter ho sakte hain.

5\. `.build()`

Final DocumentRetriever object create karta hai.

Teacher: `topK(3)` ka real-world example do.

Student: Suppose database mein Java ke 100 documents stored hain.

User poochta hai:

```
What is Java?
```

Similarity search ke baad top 3 relevant documents retrieve kiye ja sakte hain.

```
Document 1: Java is a programming language.

Document 2: Java supports object-oriented programming.

Document 3: Java is platform independent.
```

Ye documents aage context ke roop mein use honge.

# 9. DocumentJoiner – Multiple Results Combine Karna

Teacher: Agar multiple queries ya multiple data sources se documents retrieve hue, toh unko kaise combine karenge?

Student: Iske liye `DocumentJoiner` use hota hai.

Iska responsibility multiple retrieval results ko combine karke ek document collection banana hai.

### 9.1 Example

Suppose humne ek query ko 3 queries mein expand kiya.

```
Query 1: What is Java?
Query 2: What are Java features?
Query 3: How does Java work?
```

Ab har query se 3 documents retrieve hue.

```
Query 1 --> D1, D2, D3

Query 2 --> D4, D5, D6

Query 3 --> D7, D8, D9
```

Ab humein results combine karne hain.

```
D1, D2, D3
D4, D5, D6
D7, D8, D9
       |
       v
 DocumentJoiner
       |
       v
Combined Document List
```

### 9.2 ConcatenationDocumentJoiner

Teacher: Video mein kaunsa DocumentJoiner use kiya gaya?

Student: Instructor ne `ConcatenationDocumentJoiner` use kiya.

Ye multiple document collections ko concatenate/combine karta hai.

Conceptual code:

```
DocumentJoiner documentJoiner =
        new ConcatenationDocumentJoiner();
```

Teacher: Kya iska kaam duplicate documents remove karna bhi hai?

Student: Video mein sirf documents ko concatenate karne ka purpose explain kiya gaya hai. Automatic duplicate removal ka behavior video mein establish nahi kiya gaya.

Important: Agar duplicate removal, ranking ya custom merging logic chahiye, toh uske liye appropriate custom implementation ki zarurat ho sakti hai.

# 10. QueryAugmenter – Query ke Saath Context Add Karna

Teacher: Ab documents retrieve aur join ho gaye. Next step kya hai?

Student: Ab retrieved documents ko original user query ke saath combine karna hai.

Iske liye Spring AI mein `QueryAugmenter` interface use hota hai.

### 10.1 QueryAugmenter kya karta hai?

Ye original query aur retrieved documents ko use karke ek augmented query prepare karta hai.

Example:

Original User Query:

```
What is Java?
```

Retrieved Context:

```
Java is a high-level programming language.

Java is object-oriented.

Java is platform independent.
```

Augmented Query:

```
Context:
Java is a high-level programming language.
Java is object-oriented.
Java is platform independent.

Question:
What is Java?
```

Ab LLM ko query ke saath relevant context bhi milega.

### 10.2 ContextualQueryAugmenter

Teacher: Spring AI mein QueryAugmenter ki kaunsi implementation video mein explain ki gayi?

Student: `ContextualQueryAugmenter`.

Video mein instructor ne bataya ki ye default implementation ke roop mein use hota hai.

Iska kaam user query aur retrieved document context ko combine karna hai.

Conceptual code:

```
QueryAugmenter queryAugmenter =
        ContextualQueryAugmenter.builder()
                .build();
```

Important: Video mein is implementation ko explicitly configure karne ka example dikhaya gaya. Exact constructor/builder API version ke according verify karni chahiye.

### 10.3 Custom QueryAugmenter

Teacher: Kya hum apna QueryAugmenter bana sakte hain?

Student: Haan.

Video mein instructor ne bataya ki `QueryAugmenter` interface implement karke custom logic likh sakte hain.

Iske liye interface ka required method implement karna hoga.

Conceptually:

```
public class CustomQueryAugmenter
        implements QueryAugmenter {

    // Implement the required augment method
}
```

Is custom implementation mein hum apne rules ke according:

- Original query receive kar sakte hain.
- Retrieved documents receive kar sakte hain.
- Context create kar sakte hain.
- Augmented query return kar sakte hain.

Exact method signature Spring AI version ki interface documentation se verify karni hogi.

# 11. DocumentPostProcessor – Retrieved Documents Process Karna

Teacher: Retrieval ke baad documents ko directly LLM ko bhejna zaroori hai?

Student: Nahi. Kabhi-kabhi retrieved documents ko process karna useful hota hai.

Iske liye `DocumentPostProcessor` interface use hota hai.

Ye Post-Retrieval Phase mein kaam karta hai.

Possible operations:

- Irrelevant documents remove karna.
- Documents ko reorder karna.
- Documents ko compress karna.
- Retrieved information ko process karna.

Example:

```
Retrieved Documents:

D1: Java programming language
D2: Python programming language
D3: Java object-oriented features
D4: Unrelated database information
```

Agar user ka question Java ke baare mein hai, toh post processor relevant documents ko filter ya reorder karne mein help kar sakta hai.

Teacher: Kya video mein ready-made DocumentPostProcessor implementation use ki gayi?

Student: Nahi. Instructor ne interface explain kiya aur bataya ki custom implementation likh sakte hain.

Conceptual structure:

```
public class CustomDocumentPostProcessor
        implements DocumentPostProcessor {

    // Implement document processing logic
}
```

Important: Transcript mein exact method signature aur complete implementation code nahi diya gaya hai.

# 12. QueryExpander – One Query to Multiple Queries

Teacher: Video mein ek aur important component explain kiya gaya. QueryExpander kya karta hai?

Student: QueryExpander ek original query se multiple related queries generate karta hai.

Isse retrieval ke time different perspectives se information search kar sakte hain.

### 12.1 Example

Original query:

```
What is Java?
```

QueryExpander isse multiple queries mein convert kar sakta hai:

```
Query 1: What is Java programming language?

Query 2: What are the key features and uses of Java?

Query 3: How does Java compare to other programming languages?

Query 4: What is the historical development of Java?
```

Teacher: Multiple queries generate karne ka benefit kya hai?

Student: Agar hum sirf ek query search karenge, toh ho sakta hai kuch relevant information miss ho jaaye.

Lekin multiple related queries search karne se different aspects ke documents retrieve ho sakte hain.

### 12.2 MultipleQueryExpander
