CREATE TABLE employees (
    id BIGINT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    name VARCHAR(100),
    department VARCHAR(100),
    salary DECIMAL(10,2),
    city VARCHAR(100)
);

INSERT INTO employees (name, department, salary, city)
VALUES
('Rahul', 'IT', 65000, 'Pune'),
('Amit', 'HR', 45000, 'Mumbai'),
('Priya', 'IT', 75000, 'Pune'),

```
CREATE USER 'mcp_readonly'@'%'
IDENTIFIED BY 'McpDemo@123';

GRANT SELECT ON mcp_demo.*
TO 'mcp_readonly'@'%';

FLUSH PRIVILEGES;
```

Ab ye user sirf `mcp_demo` database ka data read kar sakta hai.

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0
         https://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">

    <parent>
        <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
        <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-parent</artifactId>
        <version>3.5.15</version>

    </parent>

    <groupId>com.example</groupId>
    <artifactId>mysql-mcp-server</artifactId>
    <version>0.0.1-SNAPSHOT</version>

    <properties>
        <java.version>17</java.version>

    </properties>

    <dependencyManagement>
        <dependencies>

                <groupId>org.springframework.ai</groupId>
                <artifactId>spring-ai-bom</artifactId>
                <version>${spring-ai.version}</version>
                <type>pom</type>
                <scope>import</scope>
            </dependency>
        </dependencies>
    </dependencyManagement>

        <!-- MCP Server using Streamable HTTP -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.springframework.ai</groupId>

        </dependency>

        <!-- JDBC -->
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
            <artifactId>spring-boot-starter-jdbc</artifactId>
        </dependency>

        <!-- MySQL Driver -->

            <groupId>com.mysql</groupId>

        </dependency>

    </dependencies>

    <build>
        <plugins>

                <groupId>org.springframework.boot</groupId>
                <artifactId>spring-boot-maven-plugin</artifactId>
            </plugin>
        </plugins>
    </build>

</project>

```

## **Step 7. Create MySQL MCP Tools**

Ye hamare project ki main class hai.

`tools/MysqlTools.java`

```

package com.example.mysqlmcp.tools;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.ai.mcp.server.annotation.McpTool;
import org.springframework.ai.mcp.server.annotation.McpToolParam;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import javax.sql.DataSource;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.ResultSetMetaData;
import java.sql.Statement;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

@Service
public class MysqlTools {

    private static final Logger log =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(MysqlTools.class);

    private final DataSource dataSource;

    private static final Pattern TABLE_NAME =
            Pattern.compile("[A-Za-z0-9_]+");

    public MysqlTools(DataSource dataSource) {
        this.dataSource = dataSource;
    }

    // TOOL 1: List all tables

    @McpTool(description =
            "List all tables available in the configured MySQL database")
    public String listTables() {

        log.info("MCP TOOL CALLED: listTables");

        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();

        try (Connection connection = dataSource.getConnection();
             Statement statement = connection.createStatement();
             ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery("SHOW TABLES")) {

            while (rs.next()) {
                result.append(rs.getString(1)).append("\n");
            }

            return result.length() == 0
                    ? "No tables found"
                    : result.toString();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Error listing tables", e);
            return "Database error: " + e.getMessage();
        }
    }

    // TOOL 2: Describe table

    @McpTool(description =
            "Get the columns, data types and structure of a MySQL table")
    public String describeTable(
            @McpToolParam(
                    description = "Name of the database table",
                    required = true
            )
            String tableName) {

        log.info("MCP TOOL CALLED: describeTable: {}", tableName);

        if (tableName == null ||
                !TABLE_NAME.matcher(tableName).matches()) {
            return "Invalid table name";
        }

        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();

        String sql = "DESCRIBE `" + tableName + "`";

        try (Connection connection = dataSource.getConnection();
             Statement statement = connection.createStatement();
             ResultSet rs = statement.executeQuery(sql)) {

            while (rs.next()) {

                result.append("Field: ")
                        .append(rs.getString("Field"))
                        .append(", Type: ")
                        .append(rs.getString("Type"))
                        .append(", Null: ")
                        .append(rs.getString("Null"))
                        .append(", Key: ")
                        .append(rs.getString("Key"))
                        .append("\n");
            }

            return result.toString();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Error describing table", e);
            return "Database error: " + e.getMessage();
        }
    }

    // TOOL 3: Execute read-only SQL query

    @McpTool(description =
            "Execute a read-only SELECT, SHOW, DESCRIBE or EXPLAIN SQL query. " +
            "Use this tool to retrieve records and analyze database data.")
    public String executeReadOnlyQuery(
            @McpToolParam(
                    description = "Read-only SQL query to execute",
                    required = true
            )
            String sql) {

        log.info("MCP TOOL CALLED: executeReadOnlyQuery: {}", sql);

        String validatedSql;

        try {
            validatedSql = validateSql(sql);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return "Query rejected: " + e.getMessage();
        }

        try (Connection connection = dataSource.getConnection()) {

            // Request a read-only connection
            connection.setReadOnly(true);

            try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {

                statement.setMaxRows(100);
                statement.setQueryTimeout(15);

                try (ResultSet rs =
                             statement.executeQuery(validatedSql)) {

                    return formatResult(rs);
                }
            }

        } catch (Exception e) {

            log.error("SQL execution failed", e);

            return "SQL execution failed: " + e.getMessage();
        }
    }

    private String validateSql(String sql) {

        if (sql == null || sql.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("SQL cannot be empty");
        }

        String query = sql.trim();

        // Remove one optional trailing semicolon
        if (query.endsWith(";")) {
            query = query.substring(0, query.length() - 1).trim();
        }

        // Do not allow multiple statements
        if (query.contains(";")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Multiple SQL statements are not allowed");
        }

        String upper = query.toUpperCase();

        // Only read operations
        if (!upper.matches(
                "(?s)^(SELECT|SHOW|DESCRIBE|DESC|EXPLAIN)\\b.*")) {

            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Only SELECT, SHOW, DESCRIBE and EXPLAIN are allowed");
        }

        // Block potentially dangerous SELECT patterns
        String[] blocked = {
                "INTO OUTFILE",
                "INTO DUMPFILE",
                "LOAD_FILE(",
                "SLEEP(",
                "BENCHMARK(",
                "FOR UPDATE",
                "LOCK IN SHARE MODE"
        };

        for (String keyword : blocked) {
            if (upper.contains(keyword)) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        "Unsafe SQL operation detected: " + keyword);
            }
        }

        return query;
    }

    private String formatResult(ResultSet rs) throws Exception {

        ResultSetMetaData metaData = rs.getMetaData();

        int columnCount = metaData.getColumnCount();

        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();

        // Column names
        for (int i = 1; i <= columnCount; i++) {

            result.append(metaData.getColumnLabel(i));

            if (i < columnCount) {
                result.append(" | ");
            }
        }

        result.append("\n");

        result.append("----------------------------------\n");

        // Rows
        int rowCount = 0;

        while (rs.next()) {

            for (int i = 1; i <= columnCount; i++) {

                result.append(rs.getString(i));

                if (i < columnCount) {
                    result.append(" | ");
                }
            }

            result.append("\n");
            rowCount++;
        }

        result.append("\nTotal rows: ").append(rowCount);

        return result.toString();
    }
}

```

**What is happening here?**

| Method                   | MCP tool               | Responsibility |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------- | -------------- |
| `listTables()`           | `listTables`           | Tables list    |
| `describeTable()`        | `describeTable`        | Table schema   |
| `executeReadOnlyQuery()` | `executeReadOnlyQuery` | Read-only SQL  |

Spring AI's MCP server starter discovers registered MCP tool methods and exposes them to connected MCP clients.&#x20;

[image](https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=https://docs.spring.io\&sz=32)

docs.spring.io+1



**Security note**

SQL validation in Java is an additional guard, not a complete SQL security boundary. The database account's SELECT-only permissions are the actual enforcement. Do not expose this unauthenticated development MCP endpoint to a public network. Add authentication and authorization before production use.

```

## **Step 10. Configure application.properties**

Apne existing Ollama AI project ke `application.properties` mein ye properties add karo:

```
server.port=8081

# Ollama
spring.ai.ollama.base-url=http://localhost:11434

spring.ai.ollama.chat.options.model=qwen2.5:3b

spring.ai.ollama.chat.options.temperature=0.1

# MCP Client
spring.ai.mcp.client.enabled=true

spring.ai.mcp.client.type=SYNC

spring.ai.mcp.client.request-timeout=60s

# MySQL MCP Server connection

```

**Aapka filesystem MCP server already configured hai?**

Agar haan, toh uski existing configuration remove mat karna. Existing STDIO configuration ke saath MySQL ka Streamable HTTP connection bhi rakh sakte ho. Spring AI multiple MCP server connections support karta hai.&#x20;

[image](https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=https://docs.spring.io\&sz=32)

docs.spring.io+1



## **Step 11. Create ChatClientConfig.java**

Ye class MCP tools ko ChatClient ke saath register karegi.

```
package com.example.mcp.config;

import org.springframework.ai.chat.client.ChatClient;
import org.springframework.ai.tool.ToolCallbackProvider;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;

@Configuration
public class ChatClientConfig {

    @Bean
    public ChatClient chatClient(
            ChatClient.Builder builder,
            ToolCallbackProvider mcpToolCallbackProvider) {

        return builder
                .defaultTools(mcpToolCallbackProvider)
                .build();
    }
}
```

**Important:** `ToolCallbackProvider` ke through Spring AI ko connected MCP servers ke tools milte hain. ChatClient ko ye provider explicitly dena zaroori hai.&#x20;

[image](https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=https://github.com\&sz=32)

GitHub+1



## **Step 12. Create ChatRequest.java**

```
package com.example.mcp.dto;

public record ChatRequest(String message) {
}
```

## **Step 13. Create ChatResponse.java**

```
package com.example.mcp.dto;

public record ChatResponse(String response) {
}
```

## **Step 14. Create ChatController.java**

Ye hamara main REST API controller hai.

```

package com.example.mcp.controller;

import com.example.mcp.dto.ChatRequest;
import com.example.mcp.dto.ChatResponse;

import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import org.springframework.ai.chat.client.ChatClient;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api")
public class ChatController {

    private static final Logger log =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(ChatController.class);

    private final ChatClient chatClient;

    public ChatController(ChatClient chatClient) {
        this.chatClient = chatClient;
    }

    @PostMapping("/chat")
    public ChatResponse chat(@RequestBody ChatRequest request) {

        log.info("User question: {}", request.message());

        String systemPrompt = """
                You are an AI MySQL database assistant.

                You have access to MySQL database tools through MCP.

                Instructions:

                1. Understand the user's question.
                2. Use the available MySQL MCP tools when database
                   information is required.
                3. Use listTables to list available tables.
                4. Use describeTable to inspect table structure.
                5. Use executeReadOnlyQuery for database queries.
                6. Only execute read-only SQL queries.
                7. Never generate INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE or DROP queries.
                8. Do not invent database records or query results.
                9. Explain the result in simple language.
                10. If a tool returns an error, explain the error.

                You are connected to the mcp_demo database.
                """;

        String answer = chatClient
                .prompt()
                .system(systemPrompt)
                .user(request.message())
                .call()
                .content();

        log.info("AI response: {}", answer);

        return new ChatResponse(answer);
    }
}

```

# **Part 4 – Run the complete application**

Ab aapke paas two separate Spring Boot applications hain.

**Application startup order**

1. **Start MySQL**

   Confirm karo ki MySQL server running hai aur `mcp_demo` database available hai.
2. **Start Ollama Docker**

   Check karo:
   ```
   curl.exe http://localhost:11434/api/tags
   ```
   Agar Qwen model installed nahi hai:
   ```
   docker exec -it ollama ollama pull qwen2.5:3b
   ```
   Agar aapke container ka naam alag hai toh command mein woh naam use karo.
3. **Start MySQL MCP Server**

   Password set karke port 8090 par run karo:
   ```
   $env:MYSQL_MCP_PASSWORD="McpDemo@123"
   mvn spring-boot:run
   ```
4. **Start Ollama AI Client**

   Apne existing Spring Boot project ko port 8081 par start karo.

# **Part 5 – Postman testing**

API ready

**Request**

- Method: `POST`
- URL: `http://localhost:8081/api/chat`
- Body: `raw` → `JSON`

## **Test 1 – List all tables**

```
{
  "message": "List all tables available in my database."
}
```

Expected response:

```
{
  "response": "The database contains the employees table."
}
```

## **Test 2 – Get table structure**

```
{
  "message": "Describe the employees table and show its columns."
}
```

AI `describeTable` tool invoke karega.

## **Test 3 – Get all employees**

```
{
  "message": "Show all employees from my database."
}
```

AI approximately ye SQL execute karega:

```
SELECT * FROM employees;
```

## **Test 4 – Filter employees by department**

```
{
  "message": "Find all employees working in IT department."
}
```

AI approximately ye SQL execute karega:

```
SELECT *
FROM employees
WHERE department = 'IT';
```

## **Test 5 – Calculate average salary**

```
{
  "message": "Calculate average salary of all employees."
}
```

AI approximately ye SQL execute karega:

```
SELECT AVG(salary) AS average_salary
FROM employees;
```

## **Test 6 – Department-wise employee count**

```
{
  "message": "Show department-wise employee count."
}
```

AI approximately ye SQL execute karega:

```
SELECT department, COUNT(*) AS total_employees
FROM employees
GROUP BY department;
```

# **Part 6 – Understand actual execution flow**

Ye sabse important part hai. Samjho internally kya ho raha hai.

User Postman se question bhejta hai:

```
Show employees whose salary is greater than 60000.
```

**Step 1 – Spring Boot**

ChatController user question receive karta hai.

**Step 2 – Ollama**

Ollama question understand karta hai aur decide karta hai ki database tool ki zarurat hai.

**Step 3 – Tool selection**

Ollama MCP Client ke through `executeReadOnlyQuery` tool select karta hai.

**Step 4 – MCP request**

MCP Client request MySQL MCP Server ko bhejta hai.

**Step 5 – Java tool execution**

MySQL MCP Server ka `executeReadOnlyQuery()` method execute hota hai.

**Step 6 – MySQL execution**

SQL query database par execute hoti hai:

```
SELECT *
FROM employees
WHERE salary > 60000;
```

**Step 7 – Result return**

MySQL result MCP Server ko return karta hai. MCP Client result Ollama ko deta hai.

**Step 8 – Final AI response**

Ollama result ko natural language mein convert karta hai aur Postman ko answer milta hai.

**Expected sample result**

| Employee | Department | Salary |
| -------- | ---------- | ------ |
| Rahul    | IT         | 65,000 |
| Priya    | IT         | 75,000 |
| Vijay    | IT         | 85,000 |

3 employees found

## **Part 7 – Debugging: Kaise confirm karein ki MCP tool execute hua?**

MySQL MCP Server ke console mein ye logs check karo:

```
MCP TOOL CALLED: executeReadOnlyQuery
```

Agar user tables list karta hai:

```
MCP TOOL CALLED: listTables
```

Agar table describe karta hai:

```
MCP TOOL CALLED: describeTable: employees
```

Agar ye logs aa rahe hain, toh corresponding MCP tool method execute ho raha hai.

**Note:** Agar Ollama tool invoke nahi kar raha hai, toh pehle check karo ki model tool calling support karta hai aur client ke logs mein tools discover hue hain. Smaller models jaise Qwen 3B kabhi-kabhi tool selection mein inconsistent ho sakte hain.

# **Final project structure**

**mysql-mcp-server**

Port 8090 – Database tools expose karta hai

pom.xml

application.properties

MysqlMcpServerApplication.java

tools/MysqlTools.java

**ollama-mcp-client**

Port 8081 – User questions aur AI responses

pom.xml

application.properties

config/ChatClientConfig.java

controller/ChatController.java

dto/ChatRequest.java

dto/ChatResponse.java

## **Aapke liye next step**

Pehle ye practical run karo. Agar error aaye toh **MySQL MCP Server ka startup log, Ollama AI Client ka startup log aur Postman response** share karna. Hum actual tool invocation ko step-by-step debug karenge.

Ek important distinction yaad rakho: **MCP Server database connection aur tools provide karta hai; Ollama decide karta hai ki user ke question ke liye kaunsa tool invoke karna hai.**
